|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}|{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{2}|{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|\lesssim z_{j}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+\zeta_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\big)\Big|_{\zeta_{j}=z_{j}^{2}(t-s)^{-1}}ds.

Claim: for
0\leq t<T
,

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+\zeta_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\big)\Big|_{\zeta_{j}=z_{j}^{2}(t-s)^{-1}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-2},&z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T\\
\begin{cases}|\ln T|^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{T}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle,&t\leq\frac{T}{2},\\
\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2(T-t))|}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle,&t>\frac{T}{2},z_{j}^{2}<T-t,\\
\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(T-t+z_{j}^{2})|}+|\ln T|(\ln z_{j})^{-2},&t>\frac{T}{2},z_{j}^{2}\geq T-t,\end{cases}&z_{j}^{2}<t+T\end{cases}

(3.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}<t+T\}}+T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T\}}.

By (
3.20
) and (
3.21
), we have

(3.22)

|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}|{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{2}|{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|\lesssim z_{j}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}<t+T\}}+T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T\}}.

Using (
3.11
), (
3.26
), (
3.29
), and (
3.22
), we have

(3.23)

|\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}|\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{2}|\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim z_{j}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}<t+T\}}+T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T\}}.

Proof of Claim (
3.21
).

For
0\leq t<T
, denote
g(z_{j},t):=\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+\zeta_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\big)\big|_{\zeta_{j}=z_{j}^{2}(t-s)^{-1}}ds
.

\bullet
For
z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T
,
g(z_{j},t)=z_{j}^{-2}\int_{-T}^{t}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds\sim z_{j}^{-2}|\ln T|\int_{T-t}^{2T}|\ln s_{1}|^{-2}ds_{1}\sim T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-2}
.

\bullet
For
z_{j}^{2}<t+T
,
g(z_{j},t)=\int_{-T}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}ds+z_{j}^{-2}\int_{t-z_{j}^{2}}^{t}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds
.
If
0\leq t\leq\frac{T}{2}
,

g(z_{j},t)\sim|\ln T|^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{t+T}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle\sim|\ln T|^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{T}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle.

If
t>\frac{T}{2}
and
z_{j}^{2}<T-t
,

g(z_{j},t)\sim\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2(T-t))|}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle

since

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}ds=\bigg(\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}ds\sim\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{T-s}ds+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{1}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\sim\

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{|\ln T|}{(T-s)|\ln(T-s)|^{2}}ds+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\ln(\frac{T-t}{z_{j}^{2}})=\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2(T-t))|}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\ln(\frac{T-t}{z_{j}^{2}}),

z_{j}^{-2}\int_{t-z_{j}^{2}}^{t}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds\sim|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|.

If
t>\frac{T}{2}
and
T-t\leq z_{j}^{2}<t+T
, then we have

g(z_{j},t)\lesssim\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(T-t+z_{j}^{2})|}+|\ln T|(\ln z_{j})^{-2}

since

\int_{-T}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}ds\sim\int_{-T}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{T-s}ds\sim\int_{-T}^{t-z_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\ln T|}{(T-s)|\ln(T-s)|^{2}}ds=\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(2T)|}-\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(T-t+z_{j}^{2})|},

\displaystyle z_{j}^{-2}\int_{t-z_{j}^{2}}^{t}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds\sim z_{j}^{-2}|\ln T|\int_{t-z_{j}^{2}}^{t}(\ln(T-s))^{-2}ds=z_{j}^{-2}|\ln T|\int_{T-t}^{T-t+z_{j}^{2}}(\ln v)^{-2}dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{j}^{-2}|\ln T|(T-t+z_{j}^{2})(\ln(T-t+z_{j}^{2}))^{-2}\sim|\ln T|(\ln z_{j})^{-2}.

In sum, we get the first part of (
3.21
).
In particular, for
z_{j}^{2}<t+T,t\leq\frac{T}{2}
, we have
z_{j}^{2}\gtrsim\left[\frac{|\ln T|(T-t)}{|\ln(T-t)|^{2}}\right]^{2}\sim T^{2}|\ln T|^{-2}
.
Thus
|\ln T|^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{T}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle\lesssim 1
.
For
z_{j}^{2}<t+T,t>\frac{T}{2},z_{j}^{2}<T-t
,
we have
|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)|\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{z_{j}^{2}})\rangle\lesssim\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(T-t)|^{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)})\rangle\lesssim 1
.
Thus, we have the second part of (
3.21
).
∎

3.4.
New errors produced by the global corrections

In this subsection, we will calculate the new errors produced by the introduction of
\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
defined in (
3.11
), that is,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=\

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\big[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\big)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU_{\infty}\wedge\right)\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-b\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

(3.24)

\displaystyle+a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}+b|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\wedge U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\big[-2\nabla_{x}\big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\big)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big].

Both precise versions in different modes and rough upper bounds will be deduced, which will be used for solving reduced equations and the estimates for the forthcoming gluing system.
By (
3.10
),

{\partial}_{t}\Big(\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}(z_{j},t)}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Big)=\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Big[{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\dot{\lambda}_{j}\lambda_{j}-\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})}{\sqrt{r_{j}^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\Big]-\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{\mu}r_{j}^{\mu-2}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\mu\dot{\lambda}_{j}\lambda_{j}^{\mu-1}r_{j}^{\mu}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})^{2}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0},

{\partial}_{r_{j}}\Big(\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}(z_{j},t)}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Big)=\frac{r_{j}^{\mu+1}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})(r_{j}^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{\mu}r_{j}^{\mu-1}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})^{2}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0},

\displaystyle{\partial}_{r_{j}r_{j}}\Big(\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}(z_{j},t)}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Big)=

\displaystyle\frac{r_{j}^{\mu+2}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})(r_{j}^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2})}{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\big[\frac{2\mu\lambda_{j}^{\mu}r_{j}^{\mu}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})^{2}(r_{j}^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}r_{j}^{\mu}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})(r_{j}^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big]{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{(\mu-1)\mu\lambda_{j}^{2\mu}r_{j}^{\mu-2}-(\mu+1)\mu\lambda_{j}^{\mu}r_{j}^{2\mu-2}}{(r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu})^{3}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}.

Then by (
3.11
), we have

\displaystyle{\partial}_{r_{j}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}=\

\displaystyle\Big[\big[\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu+1}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu-1}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\big]e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}},

(3.25)

\displaystyle{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}=\

\displaystyle\Big[\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}ie^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}=\Big[\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}ie^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|^{2}=|\nabla_{x^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|^{2}=|{\partial}_{r_{j}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|^{2}+r_{j}^{-2}|{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu+1}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu-1}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\bigg|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{-2}\bigg|\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\bigg|^{2}.

Since
\mu\geq 2
, it follows that

(3.26)

|\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}|\lesssim|{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{-1}|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|.

\bullet
By (
2.3
),

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})=\

\displaystyle\Big[\Big\{\frac{-\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu}(\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\lambda}_{j})}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\Big[\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\mu\dot{\lambda}_{j}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}}

(3.27)

\displaystyle+\frac{i\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}(\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\Big]\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\Big\}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

\bullet

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\Big[\big({\partial}_{r_{j}r_{j}}+\frac{1}{r_{j}}{\partial}_{r_{j}}+\frac{1}{r_{j}^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta_{j}\theta_{j}}\big)\big(\frac{r_{j}^{\mu}}{r_{j}^{\mu}+\lambda_{j}^{\mu}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}e^{i\theta_{j}}\big),0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big[\Big\{\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu+2}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\big[\frac{2\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big]{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}

(3.28)

\displaystyle+\big[\mu^{2}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}-\rho_{j}^{2\mu-2}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{3}}-\frac{\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\big]\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\Big\}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

Since
\mu\geq 2
, we have

(3.29)

|\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim|{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{-1}|{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|+z_{j}^{-2}|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}|.

\bullet

By (
3.6
), (
3.27
), (
3.28
), and (
3.12
), we have

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU_{\infty}\wedge\right)\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big[\Big\{\frac{\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\big[\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}}+\frac{i\lambda_{j}^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\big]\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\Big\}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle+\Big[\big[\frac{-\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\big]e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle+\Big[(a-ib)\Big\{\frac{-\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}+\big[\frac{2\mu\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big]{\partial}_{z_{j}}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}

(3.30)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}\big[\mu^{2}\frac{1-\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{3}}-\frac{1}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}\big]\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}\Big\}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}+\mathcal{E}_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\Big[\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}+\mathcal{E}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},

where we have used (
3.13
), and

(3.31)

\displaystyle\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=2^{-1}\rho_{j}\big[(\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-i\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})e^{i\theta_{j}}+(\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+i\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})e^{-i\theta_{j}}\big],

\displaystyle\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=2^{-1}\rho_{j}\big[(\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+i\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})e^{i\theta_{j}}+(\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-i\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})e^{-i\theta_{j}}\big].

By (
3.7
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\Big[\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\frac{-2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big[\big[\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\big)+i\dot{\gamma}_{j}\big]e^{i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})},-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}+\Big[\frac{2(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}+i\dot{\gamma}_{j})\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big[\Big\{(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}+i\dot{\gamma}_{j})\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{\mu-1}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}+\frac{4\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}\Big\}e^{i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})},\frac{4\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}^{2}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

Then by (
2.20
),

\displaystyle\bigg(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\Big[\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\bigg)\bigg)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\bigg[(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}+i\dot{\gamma}_{j})\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{\mu-1}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}+\frac{4\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}\bigg]-\frac{8\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{3}}

(3.32)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}+i\dot{\gamma}_{j})\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{\mu-1}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{\mu}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}},

(3.33)

\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\Big[\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\bigg)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\frac{4\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\rho_{j}^{\mu+1}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}.

\bullet

\displaystyle-b(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\frac{-2b}{|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1}\Big[y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}\cos\gamma_{j}-y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j},y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j}+y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cos\gamma_{j},-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-2b}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big[(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2},-(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1},(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}\cos\gamma_{j}-y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j})(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-(y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j}+y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cos\gamma_{j})(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-2b}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big[(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2},-(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1},\rho_{j}{\rm{Re}}\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right]\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}},

where for the last equality, we use the following formula. For any
a_{1},a_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\big(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}\cos\gamma_{j}-y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j}\big)a_{1}-\big(y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\sin\gamma_{j}+y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cos\gamma_{j}\big)a_{2}=\rho_{j}\left(a_{1}\cos(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})-a_{2}\sin(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\rho_{j}{\rm{Re}}\big[(a_{1}-ia_{2})e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\big]=\rho_{j}{\rm{Im}}\big[(a_{2}+ia_{1})e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\big].

Then by (
2.20
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\left[-b(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\right)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=\frac{-2b}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\bigg\{\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\rho_{j}{\rm{Re}}\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right]\bigg\}=\frac{2ib}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\overline{\big((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}+i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}\big)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bigg\{\frac{2ib\rho_{j}^{\mu+2}}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}{\partial}_{z_{j}z_{j}}\overline{\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}}+\frac{2ib\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big[\frac{2\mu}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\rho_{j}^{2}+2}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big]{\partial}_{z_{j}}\overline{\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}}

(3.34)

\displaystyle-\frac{2ib\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu-2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\bigg[\mu^{2}\frac{\rho_{j}^{\mu}-1}{(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)^{3}}+\frac{1}{\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1}\bigg]\overline{\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}}\bigg\}e^{i\gamma_{j}},

where we used (
3.28
) for the last equality.

(3.35)

\displaystyle\left[-b(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\frac{-2b}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\bigg\{\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\rho_{j}^{2}-1}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\rho_{j}{\rm{Re}}\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}-i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right]\bigg\}=\frac{-2b\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Im}}\left[\left((\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{1}+i(\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{2}\right)e^{-i(\theta_{j}+\gamma_{j})}\right].

\bullet
By (
2.5
) and (
3.11
),

a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=a\lambda_{j}^{-2}|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\Big[\frac{8a\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)}\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}e^{i\theta_{j}},0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

Then by (
2.20
),

(3.36)

\displaystyle\left(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\left(a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=\frac{8a\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\left(\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}e^{-i\gamma_{j}}\right),

\displaystyle\left(a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\frac{16a\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{1+\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{3}(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)}{\rm{Re}}\left(\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}e^{-i\gamma_{j}}\right).

\bullet

Notice

(3.37)

\big(b|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0.

By (
2.15
) and (
3.36
), we have

(3.38)

\left(b|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=b\left(\left[\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\left(|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\wedge U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=\frac{-8ib\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{\mu}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}(\rho_{j}^{\mu}+1)}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\big(\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{0}e^{-i\gamma_{j}}\big).

\bullet
Notice

{\partial}_{r_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\lambda_{j}^{-1}w_{\rho_{j}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
,

{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\sin w(\rho_{j})Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}.

By (
3.11
) and (
3.25
), we have

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=-2{\partial}_{r_{j}}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}){\partial}_{r_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2r_{j}^{-2}{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}){\partial}_{\theta_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle:=\

\displaystyle M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\rho_{j},t):=\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg)\bigg\{(a-ib)\bigg[

(3.42)

not so small to reserve some vanishing of

as

in the new error. From now on, we take

\displaystyle M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg)\bigg[(a-ib)\bigg\{\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg[\frac{\rho_{j}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}K_{0}(\zeta_{j})

\displaystyle~-ib\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\overline{\dot{p}_{j}}(s)e^{i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg[-\frac{2\rho_{j}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}\overline{K_{0}}(\zeta_{j})

\displaystyle~-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\bigg(\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\frac{8\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)}K_{0}(\zeta_{j})ds\bigg)

\displaystyle~-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg\{\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg[\frac{8\rho_{j}^{3}(\rho_{j}^{3}+3\rho_{j}^{2}+4)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{2}}K_{0}(\zeta_{j})+\frac{16\rho_{j}^{5}}{(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}}\zeta_{j}K_{0\zeta_{j}}(\zeta_{j})\bigg]ds\bigg\}

(3.46)

\displaystyle~+p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{2}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}.

By (

), we have

\displaystyle M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg)\bigg\{(a-ib)\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg\{\frac{\rho_{j}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\bigg(\frac{a+ib}{2}+O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\bigg]+\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}(\rho_{j}^{3}-3\rho_{j}^{2}-2)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{2}}\left(O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(O\left(\zeta_{j}^{2}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)\bigg\}ds\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle-ib\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\overline{\dot{p}_{j}}(s)e^{i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg\{-\frac{2\rho_{j}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}

\displaystyle\times\left[\left(\frac{a-ib}{2}+O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right]+\frac{8\rho_{j}^{3}(2\rho_{j}^{5}+\rho_{j}^{3}+5\rho_{j}^{2}+4)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{2}}\left(O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{8\rho_{j}^{5}}{(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}}\left(O\left(\zeta_{j}^{2}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)\bigg\}ds

\displaystyle-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\bigg\{\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\frac{8\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)}

\displaystyle-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\bigg\{\frac{8\rho_{j}^{3}(\rho_{j}^{3}+3\rho_{j}^{2}+4)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{2}}\left[\left(\frac{a+ib}{2}+O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\zeta_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{16\rho_{j}^{5}}{(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}}\left(O\left(\zeta_{j}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+O\left(\zeta_{j}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)\bigg\}ds\bigg]

\displaystyle+p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{2}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\bigg(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg)\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}

\displaystyle+p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}[\rho_{j}^{2}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)}-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}.

\displaystyle|\tilde{M}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{t-s}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+\zeta_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}\right)ds\lesssim|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,\quad|M_{-1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|.

By (
3.48
), we have

(3.49)

\big|\big(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\mathcal{S}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}\big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-3}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\big(\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+1\big).

Integrating (
3.41
) and (
3.49
), we have

(3.50)

|\mathcal{S}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|.

4.
Gluing system

In this section, we will derive the inner-outer gluing system and present the corresponding topologies with carefully designed weights such that solutions with desired asymptotics can be found.

4.1.
Error analysis

We look for the solution
u
of the form

(4.1)

\displaystyle u=(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*},

\displaystyle\Phi(x,t):=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|,t)\right)+\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t),

\displaystyle\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0\mbox{ \ for all \ }t\in(0,T),\quad j=1,2,\dots,N,

where

(4.2)

\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)=\eta\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)}{\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)}\Big),\quad\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)=\eta\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)}{d_{q}}\Big),

\eta
is a smooth cut-off function satisfying
0\leq\eta(x)\leq 1
,

\eta(x)=1
if
|x|\leq 1
and
\eta(x)=0
if
|x|\geq 2
;
A
is a real-valued function to to make
|u|=1
;
\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
and
\Phi_{\rm out}
will be solved in the inner-outer gluing system, where
\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
solves the inner problem near each bubble
U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, while
\Phi_{\rm out}
handles the region away from the concentration zones;
\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}
is defined in (
3.11
).
Throughout this paper, we make the ansatz

(4.3)

R(t)=\lambda_{*}^{-\beta}(t),\quad|\Phi|\ll 1,

where
0<\beta<1
will be chosen later.
Notice that

\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\equiv 1\mbox{ \ in \ }|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|\leq 2\lambda_{*}(t)R(t).

The scalar function
A
will be chosen in (
4.1
) to make
|u|=1
. Indeed,

\displaystyle|u|^{2}=1\Leftrightarrow(1+A)^{2}|U_{*}|^{2}+2(1+A)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(1-|U_{*}|^{2})+|\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}|^{2}=1

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\bigg[1+A+\frac{(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(1-|U_{*}|^{2})}{|U_{*}|^{2}}\bigg]^{2}=\frac{1-|\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}|^{2}}{|U_{*}|^{2}}+\bigg[\frac{(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(1-|U_{*}|^{2})}{|U_{*}|^{2}}\bigg]^{2}.

We take

(4.4)

A=\bigg\{1+\frac{1-|U_{*}|^{2}-|\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}|^{2}}{|U_{*}|^{2}}+\bigg[\frac{(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(1-|U_{*}|^{2})}{|U_{*}|^{2}}\bigg]^{2}\bigg\}^{1/2}-1-\frac{(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(1-|U_{*}|^{2})}{|U_{*}|^{2}}.

By (
3.4
), (
3.2
), and (
4.3
), we have

(4.5)

A=\big(1+O(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2})+O(\lambda_{*}^{2}|\Phi|^{2})\big)^{1/2}-1+O(\lambda_{*}|\Phi|)=O(\lambda_{*}+\lambda_{*}|\Phi|+|\Phi|^{2})=O(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}).

One important insight is that we only need to solve

(4.6)

S\left[u\right]=\Xi(x,t)U_{*}

for some scalar function
\Xi
.
Indeed, since
|u|=1
is kept for all
t\in(0,T)
, and as the perturbation,
u-U_{*}
is uniformly small, then

(U_{*}\cdot u)\Xi=S[u]\cdot u=-\frac{1}{2}{\partial}_{t}(|u|^{2})+\frac{a}{2}\Delta|u|^{2}=0.

If
U_{*}\cdot u\geq\delta_{0}>0
, then
\Xi\equiv 0
. (
4.6
) provides us the flexibility to adjust the error terms in
U_{*}
direction and we call this
U_{*}
-operation
mentioned earlier.
We compute

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\Phi

\displaystyle=-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm out}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}\big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\big[-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\big(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\gamma}_{j}J\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]-Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\},

(4.7)

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}\Phi

\displaystyle=\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Delta_{x}\big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\big(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big),

where we used

{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma_{j}})=\dot{\gamma}_{j}JQ_{\gamma_{j}}=\dot{\gamma}_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}J
with
J:=\begin{bmatrix}0&-1&0\\
1&0&0\\
0&0&0\end{bmatrix}
.
Notice that

\displaystyle U_{*}\Delta_{x}A+(1+A)\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left\{2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\right\}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+(1+A)\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+U_{*}\left[\Delta_{x}A+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}(1+A)\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\Phi

\displaystyle+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left\{2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\right\}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left[1+A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right]\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+U_{*}\left\{\Delta_{x}A+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}(1+A)-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right\},

and

\displaystyle\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]+\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}+U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(AU_{*}\right)+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}+U_{*}\wedge\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(AU_{*}\right)+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}.

By the above identities, we arrange terms in the error as

\displaystyle S[u]=-U_{*}\partial_{t}A-(1+A)\partial_{t}U_{*}-\partial_{t}\Phi+(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\partial_{t}U_{*}+U_{*}\partial_{t}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle+a\Big\{U_{*}\Delta_{x}A+(1+A)\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Delta_{x}[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}]

\displaystyle+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\Big\}

\displaystyle-b\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-(1+A)\partial_{t}U_{*}-\partial_{t}\Phi+(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\partial_{t}U_{*}+U_{*}\left[\partial_{t}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-\partial_{t}A\right]

\displaystyle+a\bigg[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\Phi

\displaystyle+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left\{2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}\right\}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left[1+A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right]\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+U_{*}\left\{\Delta_{x}A+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}(1+A)-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right\}\bigg]

\displaystyle-b\Big\{\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}+U_{*}\wedge\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(AU_{*}\right)+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\Phi+a\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}\Phi\right]-b\left\{\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}+U_{*}\wedge\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U_{*}+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]\partial_{t}U_{*}+\mathcal{N}[\Phi]+\Xi[\Phi]U_{*},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}[\Phi]:=a\Big[\left\{\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}+2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+\big|\nabla_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big|^{2}\right\}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left[1+A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right]\Delta_{x}U_{*}\Big]-b\Big\{\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(AU_{*}\right)+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi

(4.8)

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}\Big\},

\Xi[\Phi]:=\partial_{t}\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]+a\left\{\Delta_{x}A+\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}(1+A)-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right\}.

Then

\displaystyle S[u]=

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\Phi+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]+a\Phi\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+b\Phi\wedge\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U_{*}+a\Phi\sum\limits_{j,k=1,j\neq k}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]\partial_{t}U_{*}+\mathcal{N}[\Phi]+\Xi[\Phi]U_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\Phi+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg[\Delta_{x}\Phi-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg]+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Phi

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U_{*}+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg\{-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi\cdot\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+a\Phi\sum\limits_{j,k=1,j\neq k}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]\partial_{t}U_{*}+\mathcal{N}[\Phi]+\Xi[\Phi]U_{*}.

Using (
4.1
) and (
4.7
), we have

\displaystyle S[u]=

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm out}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\partial}_{t}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left[-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\gamma}_{j}J\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]-\sum_{j=1}^{N}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg\{\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Delta_{x}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)

\displaystyle-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\bigg[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\bigg]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Phi_{\rm{out}}+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U_{*}+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg\{-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi\cdot\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+a\Phi\sum\limits_{j,k=1,j\neq k}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]\partial_{t}U_{*}+\mathcal{N}[\Phi]+\Xi[\Phi]U_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm out}+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{\rm{out}}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Delta_{x}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\bigg\{-{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\lambda_{j}^{-2}\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Delta_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\left(W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle+2\left(\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\left\{\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\Phi_{\rm{out}}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left[\left(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\gamma}_{j}J\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{N}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left\{-\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\left(W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\left(2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}b\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\wedge\bigg\{\Delta_{x}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\nabla_{x}\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg\{-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\left[\Phi\cdot\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\bigg\{-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\bigg[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\bigg]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+a\Phi\sum\limits_{j,k=1,j\neq k}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A\right]\partial_{t}U_{*}+\mathcal{N}[\Phi]+\Xi[\Phi]U_{*}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}+U_{*}\right)\bigg\{-2a\left(\nabla_{x}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)+a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle+\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg\}.

4.2.
Simplification of the nonlinear terms
\mathcal{N}[\Phi]

In this subsection, we will single out the second-order derivatives of
\Phi
in
\mathcal{N}[\Phi]
in (
4.8
) and extract terms involving
\Phi
and its derivatives in
\mathcal{N}[\Phi]
. The purpose of this step is to obtain a convenient form for the inner-outer gluing system and estimates in the construction.

(4.9)

\displaystyle\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(AU_{*}\right)+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}A+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\left[A\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-AU_{*}\wedge[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}]

\displaystyle+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}A+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle-(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+AU_{*})\wedge[2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}]+[A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})]\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge(2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}-2A(\Phi\cdot U_{*})]U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}.

Next, we give explicit formulas for
\nabla_{x}A
and
\Delta_{x}A
.
Due to the choice of (
4.1
),
|u|=1
is equivalent to

(4.10)

(1+A)^{2}|U_{*}|^{2}+2(1+A)\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)+|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2}=1.

Acting
\nabla_{x}
on both sides of (
4.10
), we get

2(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}\nabla_{x}A+(1+A)^{2}\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})+\nabla_{x}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})+2(1+A)\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)+2(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\nabla_{x}A=0.

So

(4.11)

\nabla_{x}A=-\frac{(1+A)^{2}\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})+\nabla_{x}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})+2(1+A)\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)}{2(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+2(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)}.

Acting
\Delta_{x}
on both sides of (
4.10
), we have

\displaystyle(1+A)^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})+|U_{*}|^{2}\big[2(1+A)\Delta_{x}A+2|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}\big]+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A

\displaystyle+2(1+A)\Delta_{x}\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)+2\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)\Delta_{x}A+4\nabla_{x}\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+\Delta_{x}\big(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2}\big)=0.

Thus, we have

(4.12)

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}A=-2^{-1}\big[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)\big]^{-1}\Big[\Delta_{x}\big(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2}\big)+2(1+A)\Delta_{x}\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)

\displaystyle+4\nabla_{x}\big(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A+(1+A)^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\Big].

Notice that

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})=

\displaystyle 2\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}]+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})

and

\Delta_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)=(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})-2\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*}).

Then (
4.12
) can be rephrased into

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}A=\

\displaystyle-2^{-1}\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\Big[2\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+\left[2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+2(1+A)(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\right]\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|^{2}+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+4[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}

(4.13)

\displaystyle+4\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\Big].

By (
4.13
), part of the terms in (
4.9
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}A+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\Delta_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left\{2\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+\left[2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+2(1+A)(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\right]\left(U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left(U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi\right)

\displaystyle-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\Big\{\left[2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+2(1+A)(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\right]\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|^{2}+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+4[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}+4\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle-(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\left[2\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+2\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi\right)\right]

\displaystyle+AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi

\displaystyle-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\Big\{2\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|^{2}+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+4[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}

(4.14)

\displaystyle+4\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\Big\}.

Combining (
4.8
), (
4.9
), and (
4.14
), we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}[\Phi]=b\Big\{(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\left[\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi\right)\right]

\displaystyle-AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-\left(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi\Big\}

\displaystyle+a\bigg[\left\{\left|\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\right|^{2}-\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}+2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)+\left|\nabla_{x}\left(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right|^{2}\right\}\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi

\displaystyle+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\Delta_{x}U_{*}\bigg]

\displaystyle-b\bigg[-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\Big\{2\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|^{2}+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+4[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})

\displaystyle+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}+4\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+4(1+A)\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}A+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)]^{2}\Delta_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle-(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+AU_{*})\wedge[2\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}]+[A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})]\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge(2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})+[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}-2A(\Phi\cdot U_{*})]U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\left[(1+A)\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}\bigg].

Since

\displaystyle 2\nabla_{x}\left[(1+A)U_{*}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)=2\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big\{\left[\left({\partial}_{x_{k}}A\right)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}\Phi+(1+A){\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}\Phi\right]

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{x_{k}}\left(U_{*}\cdot\Phi\right)\left[|U_{*}|^{2}{\partial}_{x_{k}}A+(1+A)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\right]-\left(U_{*}\cdot\Phi\right)\left[\left({\partial}_{x_{k}}A\right)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}+(1+A)\left|{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\right|^{2}\right]\Big\},

then
\mathcal{N}[\Phi]
can be expanded as

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}[\Phi]=b\Big\{(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\big[\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\Phi\right)\big]

\displaystyle-AU_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi-\left(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\wedge\Delta_{x}\Phi\Big\}+a\bigg\{\bigg[|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}|U_{*}|^{2}+2(1+A)\nabla_{x}A\cdot\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)+A(2+A)\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big\{\left[\left({\partial}_{x_{k}}A\right)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}\Phi+A{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}\Phi\right]-{\partial}_{x_{k}}\left(U_{*}\cdot\Phi\right)\left[|U_{*}|^{2}{\partial}_{x_{k}}A+(1+A)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(U_{*}\cdot\Phi\right)\left[\left({\partial}_{x_{k}}A\right)U_{*}\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}+(1+A)\left|{\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\right|^{2}\right]\Big\}+\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\left|{\partial}_{x_{k}}\Phi-U_{*}{\partial}_{x_{k}}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)-(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{k}}U_{*}\right|^{2}\bigg]\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi

\displaystyle+2\left(\nabla_{x}A+U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\left(A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\Delta_{x}U_{*}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+2a\left[\left(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\Phi-\left(\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]-2a\left(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)(U_{*}\cdot\Phi)U_{*}

\displaystyle-2bU_{*}\wedge\left[\left(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\Phi-\left(\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]

\displaystyle+b(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)-b(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left(2\nabla_{x}\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle-b\bigg[-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\bigg\{2\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)|\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)|^{2}+2|\nabla_{x}\Phi|^{2}+8[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)](U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+2|U_{*}|^{2}|\nabla_{x}A|^{2}+4\left[-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}A

\displaystyle+8(1+A)\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+2\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)\right]^{2}\left(|\nabla_{x}U_{*}|^{2}+U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)\bigg\}

\displaystyle-(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi+AU_{*})\wedge\left[2\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]+[A-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})]\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge(2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}-2A(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right]U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\left[A\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\left(\nabla_{x}A+U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]\bigg]

(4.15)

\displaystyle+2bAU_{*}\wedge\left[\left(\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right],

where
U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}
,
\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi
are defined in (
2.2
).

4.3.
Inner-outer gluing system

By
U_{*}
-operation (
4.6
), we can adjust the terms in the
U_{*}
direction flexibly. By the expansion form of
S[u]
at the end of Subsection
4.1
and (
4.15
), a sufficient condition for
S[u]=0
is that
(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\Phi_{\rm out})
solve the following inner-outer gluing system

(4.16)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm{out}}=\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}+\mathcal{G}&\mbox{ in }~\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T),\\
\Phi_{\rm out}(x,0)=Z_{*}(x)+\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{n=1}^{3}c_{mn}\vartheta_{mn}(x)&\mbox{ in }~\mathbb{R}^{2};\end{cases}

(4.17)

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=

\displaystyle\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Delta_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\left(W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle+2\left(\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]+\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\quad\mbox{ in }~\mathsf{D}_{2C_{\lambda}R},

where
C_{\lambda}
is given in (
3.2
),

(4.18)

\mathsf{D}_{2C_{\lambda}R}:=\left\{(y,t)\ |\ |y|<2C_{\lambda}R(t),\ t\in(0,T)\right\};\quad\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=\mathcal{H}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},

(4.19)

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{2}Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Big[\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\tilde{L}_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]+\left(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+e^{i\theta_{j}}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}^{-1}}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\tilde{L}_{W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm{out}}]+\lambda_{j}^{2}\left(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+e^{i\theta_{j}}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{2}Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\bigg[2\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big\{\left[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\bigg]

(4.20)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 2\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left\{\left[\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\left[\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right\};

\displaystyle-b\bigg[-2^{-1}(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\bigg\{2\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\left(\Phi\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)

\displaystyle+8(1+A)\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}A+2\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-(1+A)\right]^{2}\left(|\nabla_{x}U_{*}|^{2}+U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right)\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\wedge(2\nabla_{x}A\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})+\left[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}-2A(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\right]U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+(1+A)U_{*}\wedge\left[A\Delta_{x}U_{*}+2\left(\nabla_{x}A+U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]\bigg]

(4.21)

\displaystyle+2bAU_{*}\wedge\left[\left(\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]+\Xi_{\mathcal{G}}(x,t)U_{*},

where

is some scalar function from the aforementioned

-operation;

Z_{*}(x)\in C_{0}^{\infty}({{\mathbb{R}}}^{2}),\quad\mathsf{supp}Z_{*}\subset B_{C_{q}},\quad\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\ll 1,\quad\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}Z_{*1}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}Z_{*2}+i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}Z_{*2}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}Z_{*1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\neq 0,

4.4.
Weighted topologies for the inner and outer problems

The topologies for the inner and outer problems are listed in this part.
Recall (
3.2
) and the form of (
4.17
). It is natural to introduce new time variables

(4.26)

\tau_{j}=\tau_{j}(t):=\int_{0}^{t}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)ds+C_{\tau}T\lambda_{*}^{-2}(0),\quad\tau_{j}(0)=\tau_{0}:=C_{\tau}T\lambda_{*}^{-2}(0)

with a constant
C_{\tau}>0
sufficiently large. It follows that
\lambda_{j}^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}={\partial}_{\tau}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
,

(4.27)

\displaystyle\tau_{j}(t)\sim|\ln T|^{-2}(T-t)^{-1}|\ln(T-t)|^{4},\quad\ln(\tau_{j}(t))\sim|\ln(T-t)|,

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}(t(\tau_{j}))\sim|\ln T|^{-1}\tau_{j}^{-1}(\ln\tau_{j})^{2},\quad T-t(\tau_{j})\sim|\ln T|^{-2}\tau_{j}^{-1}(\ln\tau_{j})^{4},\quad{\partial}_{\tau_{j}}t(\tau_{j})=\lambda_{j}^{2}(t(\tau_{j})),

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{\tau_{j}}\lambda_{*}(t(\tau_{j}))=({\partial}_{t}\lambda_{*})(t(\tau_{j})){\partial}_{\tau_{j}}t(\tau_{j})\sim-|\ln T|^{-1}\tau_{j}^{-2}(\ln\tau_{j})^{2}.

\bullet
We endow solutions of the inner problems with the following norms.

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}:=\sup\limits_{(y,\tau_{j})\in\mathcal{D}_{2C_{\lambda}R}}\Big[\big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}(t(\tau_{j}))\langle y\rangle^{-l}\big)^{-1}\Big(\big|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,\tau_{j})\big|+\langle y\rangle\big|D\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,\tau_{j})\big|+\langle y\rangle^{2}\big|D^{2}\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,\tau_{j})\big|\Big)\Big],

\displaystyle[\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}]_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l,\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}:=\sup\limits_{(y,\tau_{j})\in\mathcal{D}_{2C_{\lambda}R},\max\{\tau_{0},\tau_{j}-R^{2}(t(\tau_{j}))\}\leq s_{1}<s_{2}\leq\tau_{j}}\bigg\{\big[\big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}R^{2-\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}\big)(t(\tau_{j}))\big]^{-1}\frac{|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,s_{1})-\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\varsigma_{\rm{in}}/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\big[\big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}R^{1-\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}\big)(t(\tau_{j}))\big]^{-1}\frac{|D\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,s_{1})-D\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y,s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\varsigma_{\rm{in}}/2}}\bigg\},

(4.28)

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l,\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}:=\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}+\big[\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l,\varsigma_{\rm{in}}},

where
\mathcal{D}_{2R}:=\left\{(y,\tau_{j})\ |\ \tau_{j}>\tau_{0},\ |y|<2C_{\lambda}R(t(\tau_{j}))\right\}
,

(4.29)

l>0,\quad 0<\varsigma_{\rm{in}}<1,\quad 0<\delta_{0}<\nu<1.

Set
R_{0}(t)=\lambda_{*}^{-\delta_{0}/6}(t)
, which will be used in the inner problems and reduced equations.

The inner problems will be solved in the following space

(4.30)

B_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=\left\{{\mathbf{f}}\ |\ \|{\mathbf{f}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l,\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}\leq\Lambda_{\rm{in}},\quad{\mathbf{f}}\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0\right\},\quad j=1,2,\dots,N

for a constant
\Lambda_{\rm{in}}\geq 1
to be determined later.

\bullet
For the outer problem, we use the following weights to control the right-hand side of the outer problem

(4.31)

\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}},\quad\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}:=T^{-\sigma_{0}}\frac{\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}}{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}},\quad\varrho_{3}:=T^{-\sigma_{0}},

where
d_{q}
is given in (
3.1
),

(4.32)

\Theta+\beta-1<0,\quad 0<\Theta<1,\quad 0<\sigma_{0}<1.

For a function
f(x,t)
, we define the
L^{\infty}
-weighted norm

(4.33)

\|f\|_{**}:=\sup_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T)}\bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)+\varrho_{3}\bigg]^{-1}|f(x,t)|.

Also, we define the
L^{\infty}
-weighted norm for
\Phi_{\rm out}
:

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}:=\left(|\ln T|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}(0)R(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)^{-1}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)^{-1}\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T)}\left[|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}(t)R(t)+(T-t)\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right]^{-1}|\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t)-\Phi_{\rm out}(x,T)|

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T)}\big[\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(t)+(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\big]^{-1}\left|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t)-\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(x,T)\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{x,x_{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{2},0<t<t_{*}<T,\ t_{*}-t<(T-t)/4}\left[\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(t)(\lambda_{*}(t)R(t))^{-\alpha}+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right]^{-1}\frac{|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t)-\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(x_{*},t_{*})|}{\big(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}\big)^{\alpha}}

(4.34)

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{2},0<t<t_{*}<T,\ t_{*}-t<(T-t)/4}\left[T^{A_{\rm{o,h}}}(1+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})})\right]^{-1}\frac{|\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t)-\Phi_{\rm out}(x,t_{*})|}{(t_{*}-t)^{\alpha/2}}

under assumptions (
D.1
) for the parameters.
The outer problem will be solved in

(4.35)

B_{\rm{out}}:=\left\{{\mathbf{f}}\ |\ \|{\mathbf{f}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\leq\Lambda_{\rm{o}},\ {\mathbf{f}}(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)=0\mbox{ \ for \ }j=1,2,\dots,N\right\},

where
\Lambda_{\rm{o}}\geq 1
will be determined later.

We take
T
,
\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\ll 1
depending on
\Lambda_{\rm{o}}
such that
\||\Phi_{\rm{out}}|+|\nabla\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}\ll 1
. Since
\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\in B_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
with (
4.29
) and
\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}
satisfies (
3.23
), for
\Phi
given in (
4.1
),
|\Phi|\ll a
holds.

4.5.
Strategy for solving the inner problems

In order to find inner solutions with sufficient space-time decay, we need to impose orthogonality conditions for
\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
4.18
).
Due to the non-local feature at mode
0
, we will only solve the non-local problem at the leading order and leave the remainder to another piece of an inner problem without the orthogonality condition at mode
0
.

By (
2.23
) and (
2.22
),
we reformulate

(4.36)

\left(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\left[\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\tilde{L}_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]\right]\right)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}}(x,t)=\tilde{L}_{j}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)+\tilde{l}_{j}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](x,t),

where the leading term
\tilde{L}_{j}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]
is given by

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{j}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t):=\tilde{L}_{j,0}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t)+e^{i\theta_{j}}\tilde{L}_{j,1}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t)+e^{2i\theta_{j}}\tilde{L}_{j,2}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t),

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{j,0}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t):=(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}w_{\rho_{j}}^{2}(\rho_{j})e^{-i\gamma_{j}}\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}+i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t),

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{j,1}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t):=(a-ib)2\lambda_{j}^{-1}w_{\rho_{j}}(\rho_{j})\cos w(\rho_{j})\left[-{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}+i{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t),

(4.37)

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{j,2}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t):=(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}w_{\rho_{j}}^{2}(\rho_{j})e^{i\gamma_{j}}\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}-i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t),

and the smaller term
