\displaystyle-\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}+i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Big\}

\displaystyle+e^{i\theta_{j}}2\lambda_{j}^{-1}w_{\rho_{j}}(\rho_{j})\cos w(\rho_{j})\left\{\left[-{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}+i{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}\right](x,t)-\left[-{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}+i{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{3}\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\right\}

\displaystyle+e^{2i\theta_{j}}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\rho_{j}w_{\rho_{j}}^{2}(\rho_{j})e^{i\gamma_{j}}\Big\{\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}-i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}\right)\right](x,t)

(4.38)

\displaystyle-\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}-i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{2}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\left(\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)_{1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Big\}\Big].

in

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{\alpha-1}\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{\alpha-2}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

),

into non-orthogonal inner problems instead of orthogonal inner problems. Here, the orthogonal (resp. non-orthogonal) inner problem denotes the inner problem with (resp. without) orthogonality conditions at corresponding modes imposed.

,

Orthogonal inner problems:

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j1]$}}}}=

Non-orthogonal inner problems:

\displaystyle{\rm DC}_{j}[\mathbf{f}]={\rm DC}_{j}[\mathbf{f}](t):=(a-ib)\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}f_{1}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}f_{2}+i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}f_{2}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}f_{1}\right)\right](q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)

5.
Reduced equations

5.1.
Reformulation of reduced equations

We will consider the reduced equations

\displaystyle c_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))+\Big(\int_{0}^{2}\eta(r)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}^{2}(r)rdr\Big)^{-1}c_{*0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))-\Big(\int_{0}^{2}\eta(r)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}^{2}(r)rdr\Big)^{-1}\lambda_{j}e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}\mathcal{R}_{0}[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]](t)=0,

(5.1)

\displaystyle c_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))=0\mbox{ \ for \ }t\in(0,T).

If (
5.1
) is true,
then
(
4.41
)
and
(
4.42
)
will give a solution
\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j1]$}}}}+\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j2]$}}}}
for (

).
In the following lemma, we write (

) in a form that is more convenient to handle.

Lemma 5.1
.

The reduced problem (
5.1
) is equivalent to

(5.2)

\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{j}](t)={\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](t)+\mathcal{R}_{0}[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]](t),

(5.3)

\displaystyle\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-i\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\left[\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\tilde{L}_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]\right]\right)_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}c_{*1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t)),

where

(5.4)

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{j}](t):=-\bigg\{\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)}{t-s}\left[\left(-1+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle\right)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}}\right]ds

\displaystyle+(a-ib)e^{i\gamma_{j}(t)}{\rm{Re}}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\left(O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}}\right)ds\bigg]+C_{p1}\dot{p}_{j}+C_{p2}e^{i\gamma_{j}(t)}\dot{\lambda}_{j}\bigg\}.

Proof.

Terms in
c_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))
.

There exists the correspondence
between kernels (
2.9
) and (

) through (

) and (

).
See (

),
\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}=\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-1}
.
By (
4.19
) and (
4.36
), we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}\big[(\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},0}-\lambda_{j}^{2}\tilde{l}_{j,0}^{\#}\big](\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\lambda_{j}^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\tilde{L}_{j,0}^{\#}+M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}.

Using (
4.44
), the first equation in (
5.1
) can be written as

(5.5)

\mathcal{R}_{0}[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]](t)+\lambda_{j}e^{i\gamma_{j}(t)}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\tilde{L}_{j,0}^{\#}+M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=0.

Here,

(5.6)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{L}_{j,0}^{\#}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\lambda_{j}^{-1}e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}],

where we used
\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}w_{\rho_{j}}^{2}(\rho_{j})\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{3}}d\rho_{j}=1
.

Recall (
3.17
),

\zeta_{j}=\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1),\iota_{j}=\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)(t-s)^{-1}
. By (
3.47
) and
\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}=\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-1}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}M_{0}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\rho_{j}^{3}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{2(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds

\displaystyle-\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\rho_{j}^{3}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-3}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-7}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]+b(ia+b)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg[

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\rho_{j}^{3}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]+b(ib-a)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg[

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{i\rho_{j}^{3}(3\rho_{j}^{7}+\rho_{j}^{6}+12\rho_{j}^{5}-15\rho_{j}^{4}+11\rho_{j}^{3}-24\rho_{j}^{2}-8)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{4(a+ib)\rho_{j}^{5}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds

\displaystyle+(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{8(a+ib)\rho_{j}^{5}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-3}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-7}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]

\displaystyle-(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}\mathrm{Re}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\bigg[(a+ib)\frac{4\rho_{j}^{5}(\rho_{j}^{3}+3\rho_{j}^{2}+4)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{2}}+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]+C_{p1}p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}+C_{p2}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\{\left[f_{1}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\right\}d\rho_{j}ds

\displaystyle+(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\left[(a+ib)f_{2}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\bigg\}d\rho_{j}ds\bigg]+C_{p1}p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}+C_{p2}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j},

where, for brevity, we denote

\displaystyle C_{p1}:=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{2\rho_{j}^{3}[\rho_{j}^{2}-\rho_{j}-(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}d\rho_{j}=-0.123584,

\displaystyle C_{p2}:=-\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{4\rho_{j}^{5}[\rho_{j}^{2}+\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1]}{[\rho_{j}+(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}](\rho_{j}^{3}+1)(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{3}}d\rho_{j}=-0.823455,

\displaystyle f_{1}(\rho_{j}):=\frac{-8\rho_{j}^{11}+3\rho_{j}^{10}+\rho_{j}^{9}-4\rho_{j}^{8}-15\rho_{j}^{7}+11\rho_{j}^{6}-32\rho_{j}^{5}-8\rho_{j}^{3}}{2(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}},

\displaystyle f_{2}(\rho_{j}):=\frac{4\rho_{j}^{11}-15\rho_{j}^{10}-\rho_{j}^{9}-16\rho_{j}^{8}+3\rho_{j}^{7}-11\rho_{j}^{6}+16\rho_{j}^{5}+8\rho_{j}^{3}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{\frac{7}{2}}(\rho_{j}^{3}+1)^{3}}.

Notice
\int_{0}^{\infty}f_{1}(\rho_{j})d\rho_{j}=-1
,

\int_{0}^{\infty}f_{2}(\rho_{j})d\rho_{j}=0
. Then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\{\left[f_{1}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\right\}d\rho_{j}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left(-1+O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\{\left[(a+ib)f_{2}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)\leq 1\}}+O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)>1\}}\right\}d\rho_{j}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}}

since
for
\iota_{j}>1
,
\int_{0}^{\infty}O\big(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\big)d\rho_{j}=O\big(\iota_{j}^{-1}\big)
, and
for
0<\iota_{j}\leq 1
,

\int_{0}^{(\iota_{j}^{-1}-1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[f_{1}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]d\rho_{j}+\int_{(\iota_{j}^{-1}-1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)d\rho_{j}=-1+O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle),

\int_{0}^{(\iota_{j}^{-1}-1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[(a+ib)f_{2}(\rho_{j})+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)\right]d\rho_{j}+\int_{(\iota_{j}^{-1}-1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}O\left(\iota_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-5}\right)d\rho_{j}=O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle).

Thus, we arrive at

(5.7)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}M_{0}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\left[\left(-1+O\left(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle\right)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}}\right]ds

\displaystyle+(a-ib)\lambda_{j}^{-1}{\rm{Re}}\bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)e^{-i\gamma_{j}(t)}}{t-s}\left(O(\iota_{j}\langle\ln\iota_{j}\rangle)\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}\leq 1\}}+O(\iota_{j}^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{\iota_{j}>1\}}\right)ds\bigg]+C_{p1}p_{j}^{-1}\dot{p}_{j}+C_{p2}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}.

By plugging (
5.6
) and (
5.7
) into
(
5.5
), we can re-write the first equation in (
5.1
) as (
5.2
).

Terms in
c_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))
.

See (
8.17
),
\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})=\frac{1}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}
. By (
4.19
), one has

(5.8)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\big(\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\Big\{\lambda_{j}^{2}\Big(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Big[(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\tilde{L}_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]\Big]\Big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}+\lambda_{j}^{2}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+(\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}\Big\}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j},

where we recall
M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
3.43
) and get

(5.9)

\int_{0}^{\infty}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=-(\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-i\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{2}}d\rho_{j}=-(\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-i\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\lambda_{j}^{-1}.

By (
5.8
) and (
5.9
), the second equation of (
5.1
) can be rewritten as (
5.3
).
∎

5.2.
Linear theory for the non-local reduced equations

To introduce the space for the parameter function
p_{j}(t)
,
we recall that the non-local operator
\mathcal{B}_{0}
given in (
5.4
) for mode 0 is of the approximate form

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[p]=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}}\frac{\dot{p}(s)}{t-s}ds\,+O(|\dot{p}(t)|).

For
\Theta\in(0,1)
,
\varpi\in\mathbb{R}
and a continuous function
g:[-T,T]\to\mathbb{C}
, we define the norm

\|g\|_{\Theta,\varpi}=\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\,(T-t)^{-\Theta}|\ln(T-t)|^{\varpi}|g(t)|,

and for
\alpha\in(0,1)
,
\tilde{m}
,
\varpi\in\mathbb{R}
, we define the semi-norm

[g]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi}=\sup_{-T\leq s<t\leq T,\ t-s\leq(T-t)/4}(T-t)^{-\tilde{m}}|\ln(T-t)|^{\varpi}\frac{|g(t)-g(s)|}{(t-s)^{\alpha/2}}.

The following proposition proved in
[
22
, Proposition 6.5, Proposition 6.6]
gives an approximate inverse of the non-local operator
\mathcal{B}_{0}
with a small remainder
\mathcal{R}_{0}
.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
\alpha_{0},\frac{\alpha}{2}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,
\varpi\in\mathbb{R}
,
C_{1}>1
. There exists
\flat>0
such that if
\Theta\in(0,\flat)
and
\tilde{m}\leq\Theta-\frac{\alpha}{2}
, then for
h(t):[0,T]\to\mathbb{C}
satisfying

(5.10)

C_{1}^{-1}\leq|h(T)|\leq C_{1},\quad T^{\Theta}|\ln T|^{1+\sigma-\varpi}\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi-1}\leq C_{1}

for some
\sigma\in(0,1)
and
T>0
small enough, there exist two linear operators
\mathcal{P}
and
\mathcal{R}_{0}
so that
p=\mathcal{P}[h]:[-T,T]\to\mathbb{C}
satisfies

\mathcal{B}_{0}[p](t)=h(t)+\mathcal{R}_{0}[h](t),\quad t\in[0,T]

with

|\mathcal{R}_{0}[h](t)|\leq C\Bigl(T^{\sigma_{1}}+T^{\Theta}\frac{\ln|\ln T|}{|\ln T|}\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi-1}\Bigr)\frac{(T-t)^{\tilde{m}+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}}}{|\ln(T-t)|^{\varpi}}

for some
\sigma_{1}>0
. Moreover,

\mathcal{P}[h]=p_{0,\kappa}[h]+\mathcal{P}_{1}[h]+\mathcal{P}_{2}[h],

where
p_{0,\kappa}[h]
is the leading term of
\mathcal{P}[h]
with

\displaystyle p_{0,\kappa}[h](t)=\kappa|\ln T|\int_{t}^{T}|\ln(T-s)|^{-2}ds=\kappa\left(1+O(|\ln T|^{-1})\right)|\ln T|(T-t)|\ln(T-t)|^{-2},

\displaystyle\kappa=\kappa[h]=h(T)\left(1+O(|\ln T|^{-1})\right),\quad|{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{1}[h](t)|\leq C\frac{|\ln T|^{1-\sigma}\left(\ln(|\ln T|)\right)^{2}}{|\ln(T-t)|^{3-\sigma}},

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{1}[h](t)|\leq C\frac{|\ln T|}{|\ln(T-t)|^{3}(T-t)},\quad\|{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{2}[h]\|_{\Theta,\varpi}\leq C\Big(T^{\frac{1}{2}+\sigma-\Theta}+\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}\Big),

(5.11)

\displaystyle[{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{2}[h]]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi}\leq C\Big(|\ln T|^{\varpi-3}T^{\flat-\tilde{m}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+T^{\Theta}|\ln T|^{-1}\ln|\ln T|\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi-1}\Big).

We now impose constraints on the parameters such that we can apply Proposition
5.1
to provide a linear mapping to
p_{j}
for (
5.2
) with
h={\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](t)
.
The vanishing and Hölder properties in (
5.10
) are exactly the ones inherited from the weighted topology (
4.34
) for the outer problem, namely

\displaystyle|{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](t)-{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](T)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)+(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})},

\displaystyle\frac{|{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](t)-{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](s)|}{|t-s|^{\alpha/2}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}(t)^{\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)}+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mbox{ \ for \ }|t-s|<\frac{T-t}{4}.

In order for both
\|{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\cdot)-{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}
,
[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},\tilde{m},\varpi-1}
to be finite, we need

(5.12)

\varpi-1-2\Theta<0,\quad\Theta<\alpha/2,\quad\tilde{m}<\min\left\{\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta),0\right\}.

We put the remainder
\mathcal{R}_{0}[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]]
in the non-orthogonal inner problem (
4.42
). For the gluing to work, suitable parameters will be chosen such that
\mathcal{R}_{0}[{\rm DC}_{j}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]]
has fast time decay.

6.
Linear theory for the outer problem

6.1.
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}
,
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}
spaces, and regularity results

Given a vector-valued function
\mathbf{f}
defined in
Q:=\Omega\times(t_{0},t_{1})\subset\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
,
for
X=(x,t)\in Q
,
(B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega)\times(t-r^{2},t)\subset Q
, we define

\displaystyle\omega_{\mathbf{f},Q}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X):=\fint_{(B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega)\times(t-r^{2},t)}\Big|\mathbf{f}(y,s)-\fint_{B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega}\mathbf{f}(z,s)dz\Big|dyds,

\displaystyle\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q):=\sup\left\{\omega_{\mathbf{f},Q}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X)\ |\ X\in Q\right\}\quad\text{and}\quad\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r):=\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,\mathbb{R}^{d+1}).

We say that
\mathbf{f}
is of
Dini mean oscillation in
\mathbf{x}
over
Q
and write
\mathbf{f}\in\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)
if
\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)
satisfies the Dini condition

\int_{0}^{1}r^{-1}\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)dr<+\infty
.
Denote the
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)

semi-norm
as
[{\mathbf{f}}]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}:=\int_{0}^{1}r^{-1}\omega_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)dr
.
Similarly, for
X=(x,t)\in Q
,
(B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega)\times(t-r^{2},t)\subset Q
, we define

\displaystyle|\omega|_{\mathbf{f},Q}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X):=\fint_{(B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega)\times(t-r^{2},t)}\fint_{B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega}\left|\mathbf{f}(y,s)-\mathbf{f}(z,s)\right|dzdyds,

\displaystyle|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q):=\sup\left\{|\omega|_{\mathbf{f},Q}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X)\ |\ X\in Q\right\}\quad\text{and}\quad|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r):=|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,\mathbb{R}^{d+1}).

We say that
\mathbf{f}
is of
Dini mean absolute oscillation in
\mathbf{x}
over
Q
and write
\mathbf{f}\in\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
if
|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)
satisfies the Dini condition
\int_{0}^{1}r^{-1}|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)dr<+\infty
.
Denote the
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)

semi-norm
as
[{\mathbf{f}}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}:=\int_{0}^{1}r^{-1}|\omega|_{\mathbf{f}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q)dr
, and

\|f\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(Q)}:=[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(Q)}.

If
|B_{r}(x)\cap\Omega|\geq C|B_{r}(x)|
with a constant
C\in(0,1)
for all
x\in\Omega
, it follows that
|\omega|_{\mathbf{f},Q}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X)\lesssim|\omega|_{\mathbf{f},\mathbb{R}^{d+1}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,X)
and thus
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{d+1})\subset\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
.

We present some basic properties about
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)
and
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
in the following lemma.

Lemma 6.1
.

(1)

For
f\in\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
, then
[f]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}\leq[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}
,
[|f|]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}
.

(2)

If
|f(x,t)-f(y,t)|\leq C|x-y|^{\alpha_{f}}
with constants
C>0
,
0<\alpha_{f}\leq 1
for all
(x,t),(y,t)\in Q
, then
[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq\alpha_{f}^{-1}C
.

(3)

For
f,g\in\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q))
,
c\in\mathbb{R}
, then
[f+g]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}\leq[f]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}+[g]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}
,
[cf]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}=|c|[f]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)}

([f+g]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}+[g]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)},[cf]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}=|c|[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)})
.

(4)

For
f\in\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
satisfying
|f|\geq C_{1}>0
uniformly in
Q
, then
[\frac{1}{f}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq C_{1}^{-2}[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}
,

[|f|^{\theta}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq\theta C_{1}^{\theta-1}[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}
with
0<\theta<1
.

(5)

For
f,g\in\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)\cap L^{\infty}(Q)
, then
[fg]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}(Q)}+[g]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(Q)}
,

[|f|^{\theta}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}\leq\theta\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(Q)}^{\theta-1}[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)}
with
\theta>1
.

Proof.

The proof is straightforward by the definition.
∎

Compared with
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q)
,
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
has the advantage that the functions in
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q)
are closed under arithmetic under some weak assumptions.

Denote
Q_{r}^{-}(X)=Q_{r}^{-}(x,t):=B_{r}(x)\times(t-r^{2},t)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
.

Proposition 6.1
.

Consider the second-order parabolic system

\mathbf{u}_{t}-\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{d}\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}D_{\alpha\beta}\mathbf{u}+\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d}\mathbf{B}^{\alpha}D_{\alpha}\mathbf{u}+\mathbf{C}\mathbf{u}=\mathbf{g}\mbox{ \ in \ }Q_{2}^{-}(0),

where
\mathbf{u}=(u_{1},u_{2},\dots,u_{m})
,
\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}=(A^{\alpha\beta}_{ij}(x,t))_{i,j=1}^{m}
,
\mathbf{B}^{\alpha}=(\mathbf{B}^{\alpha}_{ij}(x,t))_{i,j=1}^{m}
,

\mathbf{C}=(\mathbf{C}_{ij}(x,t))_{i,j=1}^{m}
,
\mathbf{g}=(g_{1},g_{2},\dots,g_{m})
,

\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta},\mathbf{B}^{\alpha},\mathbf{C}\in{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))\cap L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))
, and
\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}
satisfies the
Legendre-Hadamard ellipticity

(6.1)

A^{\alpha\beta}_{ij}(x,t)\xi_{\alpha}\xi_{\beta}\vartheta^{i}\vartheta^{j}\geq c_{1}|\xi|^{2}|\vartheta|^{2}

with a constant
c_{1}>0

for all
(x,t)\in Q_{2}^{-}(0)
,
\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\vartheta\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
. Let
\mathbf{u}\in W_{2}^{1,2}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))
be the strong solution and

\mathbf{u}\in L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))
,
\mathbf{g}\in{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))\cap L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))
. Then

\|D^{2}\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{1}^{-}(0))}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{1}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\|\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+[{\mathbf{g}}]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}.

Proof.

It is a direct application of
[
31
, Lemma 4.13]
and the
L^{p}
estimates.
∎

Proposition 6.2
.

Consider the second-order parabolic system

\mathbf{u}_{t}-\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{d}\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}D_{\alpha\beta}\mathbf{u}+\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d}\mathbf{B}^{\alpha}D_{\alpha}\mathbf{u}+\mathbf{C}\mathbf{u}=\mathbf{g}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{d+1}.

Given
(x_{*},t_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
,
\rho_{*}>0
,
denote
\tilde{\mathbf{u}}(z,s):=\mathbf{u}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)
,

\tilde{\mathbf{g}}(z,s):=\rho_{*}^{2}\mathbf{g}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)
,
\tilde{\mathbf{A}}^{\alpha\beta}(z,s):=\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)
,
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\alpha}(z,s):=\rho_{*}\mathbf{B}^{\alpha}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)
,
\tilde{\mathbf{C}}(z,s):=\rho_{*}^{2}\mathbf{C}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)
. Suppose that
\tilde{\mathbf{u}}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{g}}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{A}}^{\alpha\beta}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\alpha}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{C}}
satisfy the assumption in Proposition
6.1
, and

[f]_{{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\leq C,\quad f=\tilde{\mathbf{A}}^{\alpha\beta},\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\alpha},\tilde{\mathbf{C}}

with a constant
C
independent of
x_{*},t_{*}
and
\rho_{*}
, then

\displaystyle\|(D_{x}^{2}\mathbf{u})(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{1}^{-}(0))}+\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{u})(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{1}^{-}(0))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho_{*}^{-2}\|\mathbf{u}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|\mathbf{g}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+[\mathbf{g}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}

with “
\lesssim
” independent of
x_{*},t_{*}
and
\rho_{*}
.

Proof.

Since
\partial_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{u}}-\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{d}\tilde{\mathbf{A}}^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{z_{\alpha}z_{\beta}}\tilde{\mathbf{u}}+\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\alpha}\partial_{z_{\alpha}}\tilde{\mathbf{u}}+\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{u}}=\tilde{\mathbf{g}}
,
the conclusion is a direct application of Proposition
6.1
.
∎

6.2.
Fundamental solution for the outer problem

Proposition 6.3
.

Consider the second-order parabolic system

(6.2)

\mathbf{u}_{t}=\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{d}\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}D_{\alpha\beta}\mathbf{u}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{d+1},

where
\mathbf{u}=(u_{1},u_{2},\dots,u_{m})
,
\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}=(A^{\alpha\beta}_{ij}(x,t))_{i,j=1}^{m}
,
[\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{d+1})}+\|\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d+1})}\leq\Lambda
for a constant
\Lambda>0
and Legendre-Hadamard ellipticity (
6.1
) for all
(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
. Then the parabolic system (
6.2
) has a fundamental solution
\Gamma(x,t,y,s)
satisfying

\Big({\partial}_{t}-\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{d}\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}D_{\alpha\beta}\Big)\Gamma(\cdot,\cdot,y,s)=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(s,\infty),\quad\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow s^{+}}\Gamma(\cdot,t,y,s)=\delta_{y}(\cdot)\mbox{ \ on \ }\mathbb{R}^{d}.

Moreover, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, there exists a universal constant
c>0
such that for
0<t-s\leq 1
,

(6.3)

\displaystyle(t-s)\left(|{\partial}_{t}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)|+|D_{x}^{2}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)|\right)+(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}|D_{x}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)|+|\Gamma(x,t,y,s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}};

for
s<t_{1}<t_{2}\leq s+1
,
x_{1},x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

(6.4)

\frac{|\Gamma(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-\Gamma(x_{2},t_{2},y,s)|}{\big(|x_{1}-x_{2}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{2}|}\big)^{\alpha}}\leq C(\alpha,d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)(t_{2}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[(t_{1}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x_{1}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{1}-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}+(t_{2}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x_{2}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{2}-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}\Big],

(6.5)

\displaystyle\frac{|(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{2},t_{2},y,s)|}{\big(|x_{1}-x_{2}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{2}|}\big)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\alpha,d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)(t_{2}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[(t_{1}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x_{1}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{1}-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}+(t_{2}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x_{2}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{2}-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}\Big].

Furthermore, for
f\in C^{3}_{0}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
satisfying
\mathsf{supp}f\subset B_{C_{1}}
with a constant
C_{1}>0
, denote

\left(\Gamma*f\right)(x,t):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)f(y)dy
. Then for
0<t-s\leq 1
,
s<t_{1}<t_{2}\leq s+1
,
x_{1},x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

(6.6)

\displaystyle\left|\Gamma*f\right|+\left|D_{x}\left(\Gamma*f\right)\right|+\left|D^{2}_{x}\left(\Gamma*f\right)\right|+\left|{\partial}_{t}\left(\Gamma*f\right)\right|\lesssim C(C_{1},d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)\|f\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{d})},

\displaystyle\frac{\left|D_{x}\left(\Gamma*\mathbf{f}\right)(x_{1},t_{1})-D_{x}\left(\Gamma*\mathbf{f}\right)(x_{2},t_{2})\right|}{\big(|x_{1}-x_{2}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{2}|}\big)^{\alpha}}\lesssim C(\alpha,C_{1},d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)\|\mathbf{f}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Proof.

The existence of the fundamental solution is a generalization of
[
34
, Theorems 1.1, 1.3]
to the parabolic system (
6.2
).
Indeed,

W_{p}^{2,1}
estimates for parabolic systems are given in
[
32
]
, which can be used to generalize
[
34
, Lemma 2.2]
to parabolic systems. The results
[
34
, Lemma 2.3]
and
[
31
, Theorem 3.3]
can also be generalized to parabolic systems.

Given
(x_{*},t_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
,
\rho_{*}\leq 1
,
[\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau)]_{\mathsf{DMO_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{d+1})}\leq\Lambda
. By a generalized version of
[
34
, Theorem 1.3]
,
[
31
, Theorem 3.2]
for parabolic systems, and the scaling argument similar to Proposition
6.2
, the validity of (
6.3
) follows,
where

C(d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta)
,
c>0
will vary from line to line. The constant “
C
” in the proof depends on
d,c_{1},\Lambda,\omega_{\mathbf{A}}^{\mathsf{x}},\delta
, and for simplicity, we will not stress this dependence by writing these explicitly.

For any
(x_{*},t_{*}),(y,s)\in\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
,
s<t_{*}\leq s+1
, set
\rho_{*}=(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Consider
\Gamma(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho^{2}_{*}\tau,y,s)
as a function of
z,\tau
. For
p>d+2
, set
\alpha_{1}=1-\frac{d+2}{p}
. Then

\displaystyle\rho_{*}^{-1-\alpha_{1}}\|\Gamma(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho^{2}_{*}\tau,y,s)\|_{L^{p}(B(0,\frac{1}{2})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}\|(t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x_{*}+\rho_{*}z-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}\|_{L^{p}(B(0,\frac{1}{2})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|x_{*}-y|\leq(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|x_{*}-y|>(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}\sim(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}},

where we used

\frac{3}{4}(t_{*}-s)\leq t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau-s\leq t_{*}-s,\quad\frac{|x_{*}+\rho_{*}z-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau-s}}\begin{cases}\lesssim 1&\mbox{ \ if \ }|x_{*}-y|\leq(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\sim\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|x_{*}-y|>(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{cases}

Similarly,

\displaystyle\rho_{*}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}\tau,y,s)\|_{L^{p}(B(0,\frac{1}{2})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}+\rho^{1-\alpha_{1}}_{*}\|(D_{x}^{2}\Gamma)(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho^{2}_{*}\tau,y,s)\|_{L^{p}(B(0,\frac{1}{2})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}

\displaystyle+\rho^{1-\alpha_{1}}_{*}\|({\partial}_{t}\Gamma)(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho^{2}_{*}\tau,y,s)\|_{L^{p}(B(0,\frac{1}{2})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}\lesssim(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}.

By the Sobolev embedding theorem (see
[
31
, Lemma 2.1]
for instance),

\sup\limits_{x_{1},x_{2}\in B(x_{*},\frac{(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2}),t_{1},t_{2}\in(t_{*}-\frac{t_{*}-s}{4},t_{*})}\frac{|(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{2},t_{2},y,s)|}{(|x_{1}-x_{2}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{2}|})^{\alpha_{1}}}\lesssim C(\alpha_{1})(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha_{1}}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}.

For
(x_{1},t_{1})\notin B(x_{*},\frac{(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2})\times(t_{*}-\frac{t_{*}-s}{4},t_{*})
, by (
6.3
), we have

\frac{|(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-(D_{x}\Gamma)(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)|}{(|x_{1}-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{*}|})^{\alpha_{1}}}\lesssim(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2}}\Big\{(t_{1}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{1}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{1}-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}\Big\}.

Similarly, we have

\frac{|\Gamma(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)|}{(|x_{1}-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{*}|})^{\alpha_{2}}}\lesssim C(\alpha_{2})(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\alpha_{2}}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}

for
(x_{1},t_{1})\in B(x_{*},\frac{(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2})\times(t_{*}-\frac{t_{*}-s}{4},t_{*})
, where

\alpha_{2}=\begin{cases}2-\frac{d+2}{p}&\mbox{ \ if \ }p<d+2\\
1-\epsilon\text{ for any }\epsilon\in(0,1),&\mbox{ \ if \ }p\geq d+2.\end{cases}

For
(x_{1},t_{1})\notin B(x_{*},\frac{(t_{*}-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2})\times(t_{*}-\frac{t_{*}-s}{4},t_{*})
, by (
6.3
), we get

\frac{|\Gamma(x_{1},t_{1},y,s)-\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)|}{(|x_{1}-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t_{1}-t_{*}|})^{\alpha_{2}}}\lesssim(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2}}\Big\{(t_{1}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{1}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{1}-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}\Big\}.

By (
6.3
), (
C.2
), one has
\left|\Gamma*f\right|\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}
.
By
[
31
, Theorem 3.2]
, and
W^{1,2}_{p}
estimates
[
32
, Theorem 2]
(where we used
\mathsf{supp}f\subset B_{C_{1}}
), we conclude the validity of (
6.6
).
∎

6.3.
Properties of the leading coefficients for the outer problem

Proposition 6.4
.

Suppose that
T\ll 1
,
\lambda_{j}
given in (
3.2
),
|\Phi|\ll a
,
\||\Phi_{\rm{out}}|+|\nabla\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}\leq 1
,

\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lesssim 1
, parameter assumption (
4.29
) and

(6.7)

\nu-\delta_{0}>1/2

holds,
then
\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}
defined in
(
4.22
) satisfies the
Legendre-Hadamard ellipticity (
6.1
) with a constant
c_{1}

and
\|\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}

\leq C
, where
c_{1},C
are positive constants independent of
T
.

Proof.

In this proof, we assume
\mathbb{R}_{T}^{2}=\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T)
and all “
\lesssim
” are independent of
T
.
Set
\mathbf{A}^{11}=\mathbf{A}^{22}=a\mathbf{I}_{3}-bU_{*}\wedge
,
and
\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}=0
for all
(\alpha,\beta)\neq(1,1),(2,2)
.
For
{\mathbf{f}}=(f_{1},f_{2})\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
,
{\mathbf{g}}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^{2}{\mathbf{g}}^{\mathsf{tr}}\mathbf{A}^{\alpha\beta}f_{\alpha}f_{\beta}{\mathbf{g}}={\mathbf{g}}^{\mathsf{tr}}[|{\mathbf{f}}|^{2}(a\mathbf{I}_{3}-bU_{*}\wedge){\mathbf{g}}]=|{\mathbf{f}}|^{2}\left(a|{\mathbf{g}}|^{2}-b{\mathbf{g}}^{\mathsf{tr}}[U_{*}\wedge{\mathbf{g}}]\right)=a|{\mathbf{f}}|^{2}|{\mathbf{g}}|^{2}.

By (
4.23
), (
4.5
), then
|\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{\Phi,U_{*}}|=O(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|)
. Taking
T\ll 1
and
|\Phi|\ll a
, one has that
\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}
satisfies (
6.1
) with a constant
c_{1}>0
independent of
T
. Next we will prove
\|\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}_{T}^{2})}\lesssim 1
.
Recall
U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
2.10
). Obviously,
|U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\equiv 1
.
Since

\frac{2y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1}=\frac{2\lambda_{j}(t)(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t))}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)},\quad\frac{|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}-1}{|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1}=1-\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)},

in order to get
[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
, it suffices to prove

(6.8)

[f]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1\mbox{ \ for \ }f=\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)},\quad\frac{\lambda_{j}(t)(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t))}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)}.

Proof of (
6.8
).

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}{|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}-\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}{|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}\bigg|=\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\left||z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}-|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}\right|}{\big(|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)\big(|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle|w-z|\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\frac{\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|+\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|}{\big(|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)\big(|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|w-z|\lambda_{j}(s)\big[\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}+\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}\big].

Then for any
Q_{r}^{-}(X)\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T}
,

\displaystyle\fint_{Q_{r}^{-}(X)}\fint_{B_{r}(x)}\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}{\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}-\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}{\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}\bigg|dzdwds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle r\fint_{Q_{r}^{-}(X)}\fint_{B_{r}(x)}\lambda_{j}(s)\Big[\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}+\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}\Big]dzdwds

\displaystyle\sim\

\displaystyle r\fint_{t-r^{2}}^{t}\fint_{B_{r}(x)}\lambda_{j}(s)\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}dzds\leq r\fint_{t-r^{2}}^{t}\fint_{B_{r}(0)}\lambda_{j}(s)\big(\left|z\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}dzds

(6.9)

\displaystyle\sim\

\displaystyle r^{-3}\int_{t-r^{2}}^{t}\lambda_{j}(s)\int_{0}^{r}\left(v^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\right)^{-1}vdvds\sim r^{-3}\int_{t-r^{2}}^{t}\lambda_{j}(s)\ln\big(1+\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)r^{2}\big)ds.

It suffices to prove that the following integral is bounded.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}r^{-4}\int_{t-r^{2}}^{t}\lambda_{j}(s)\ln\left(1+\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)r^{2}\right)dsdr=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{1}r^{-4}\lambda_{j}(s)\ln\left(1+\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)r^{2}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}drds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}+\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\Big)\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)\int_{\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}^{\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}z^{-4}\ln(1+z^{2})dzds.

Recall
\lambda_{j}
in (
3.2
). For the first part, since
T-t\leq T-s\leq 2(T-t)
,
there exist constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)\int_{\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}^{\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}z^{-4}\ln(1+z^{2})dzds\lesssim\Big(\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)]_{+}}^{t}\Big)\lambda_{j}^{-2}(t)\int_{c_{2}\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(t)}}^{\frac{c_{1}}{\lambda_{j}(t)}}z^{-4}\ln(1+z^{2})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)]_{+}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(t)\Big[\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(t)}\Big]^{-3}\ln\Big(1+\Big[\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(t)}\Big]^{2}\Big)ds+1\lesssim\int_{1}^{\frac{(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(t)}}y^{-2}\ln(1+y^{2})dy+1\lesssim 1.

For the second part, since
(T-s)/2\leq t-s\leq T-s
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)\int_{\frac{(t-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}^{\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}z^{-4}\ln(1+z^{2})dzds\leq\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(s)\int_{\frac{(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2}\lambda_{j}(s)}}^{\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(s)}}z^{-4}\ln(1+z^{2})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{j}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln\Big(1+\Big[\frac{(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2}\lambda_{j}(s)}\Big]^{2}\Big)ds\lesssim 1,

where the type II speed
\lambda_{j}(t)\lesssim(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}
with a constant
0<\epsilon\ll 1
is essential for the last step. Next for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(s)\big(w_{i}-\xi_{i}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\big)}{\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}-\frac{\lambda_{j}(s)\big(z_{i}-\xi_{i}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\big)}{\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(s)\bigg|\frac{\big(w_{i}-\xi_{i}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\big)\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)-\big(z_{i}-\xi_{i}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\big)\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)}{\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle\left|w-z\right|\lambda_{j}(s)\frac{\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)+\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|\left(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|+\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|\right)}{\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\left|w-z\right|\lambda_{j}(s)\big[\big(\left|w-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}+\big(\left|z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)\right|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(s)\big)^{-1}\big].

We conclude that
[\frac{\lambda_{j}(t)(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t))}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}(t)}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
by the same reasoning as (
6.9
). Thus, we deduce (
6.8
). It follows that
\|U_{*}\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
.

By Lemma
6.1
(5), the
\|\cdot\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}
-norm of
the multiplicity of finitely many terms of the components of
U_{*}
is finite.
By Lemma
6.1

(2)
and (
3.23
), then
\|\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
; similarly, we have
\|g\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
for
g=\Phi_{\rm{out}},~\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
since
\||\Phi_{\rm{out}}|+|\nabla\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\leq 1
.

By
\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lesssim 1
and parameter assumption (
4.29
), we have

\displaystyle\fint_{Q_{r}^{-}(X)}\fint_{B_{r}(x)}\Big|\eta\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)}{\lambda_{*}(s)R(s)}\Big)Q_{\gamma_{j}(s)}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)}{\lambda_{j}(s)},s\Big)-\eta\Big(\frac{z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)}{\lambda_{*}(s)R(s)}\Big)Q_{\gamma_{j}(s)}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big(\frac{z-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)}{\lambda_{j}(s)},s\Big)\Big|dxdzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle r^{-2}\int_{t-r^{2}}^{t}r\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}(s)ds\sim r^{-1}|\ln T|^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\int_{T-t}^{T-t+r^{2}}\frac{z^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}}{|\ln z|^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-2}}dz\lesssim|\ln T|^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\frac{r^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}}{|\ln r|^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-2}},

which is a Dini function under the assumption (
6.7
). And the corresponding
[\cdot]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
since
\nu-\delta_{0}<1
by (
4.29
).

In sum, for
\Phi
given in (
4.1
), by Lemma
6.1
, under the assumptions (
4.29
) and (
6.7
), we have

(6.10)

\|\Phi\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1.

For
A
given in (
4.4
), by (
3.4
), (
6.10
),
|\Phi|\ll 1
and Lemma
6.1
, we have
\|A\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
. By (
4.5
),
\lambda_{*}
in (
3.2
) and
|\Phi|\ll 1
, then
|A|\ll 1
. By the similar argument,
\|\mathbf{B}_{\Phi,U_{*}}\|_{(\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}\cap L^{\infty})(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{T})}\lesssim 1
.
∎

7.
Completion of the construction

7.1.
Proof of Theorem
1

Step 1.

Z_{*}(x)
is the leading part of the initial value of the outer problem (
4.16
). For
Z_{*}(x)
given in (
4.24
),
{\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]
in (
4.43
) is independent of
t
and satisfies (
5.10
). By Proposition
5.1
, as the leading term of
p_{j}
,
p_{j0}=\mathcal{P}[{\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]]
satisfies

(7.1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{j0}](t)={\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]+\mathcal{R}_{0}\left[{\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]\right],\quad t\in[0,T],

\displaystyle p_{j0}={\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]\left(1+O(|\ln T|^{-1})\right)\lambda_{*},\quad\dot{p}_{j0}=-{\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]\left(1+O(|\ln T|^{-1})\right)(T-t)^{-1}\lambda_{*}.

Set
p_{j}=p_{j0}+p_{j1}
, where
p_{j1}
is the next order term of
p_{j}
. Denote

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 0}:=(p_{10},p_{20},\dots,p_{N0}),\quad{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}:=(p_{11},p_{21},\dots,p_{N1}),\quad\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}:=(\dot{\xi}^{[1]},\dot{\xi}^{[2]},\dots,\dot{\xi}^{[N]}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}=\big(\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[1]},\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[2]},\dots,\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[N]}\big),\quad\mathcal{B}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}=\big(B_{\rm{in}}^{[1]},B_{\rm{in}}^{[2]},\dots,B_{\rm{in}}^{[N]}\big).

Recall the ansatz (
3.2
). We will solve
{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}
,
\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}
in the following spaces respectively,

\displaystyle B_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}}:=\big\{(p_{11},p_{21},\dots,p_{N1})\ \big|\ |p_{j1}(t)|+(T-t)|\dot{p}_{j1}(t)|\leq|\ln T|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lambda_{*}(t),\ t\in[0,T),\ j=1,2,\dots,N\big\},

\displaystyle B_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}}:=\big\{(\dot{\xi}^{[1]},\dot{\xi}^{[2]},\dots,\dot{\xi}^{[N]})\ \big|\ |\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|\leq C_{\xi}\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon_{\xi}}(t),\ t\in[0,T),\ j=1,2,\dots,N\big\}.

Set
\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)=q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\int_{t}^{T}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(s)ds
. Given
{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}\in B_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}\in B_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}}
, we have

p_{j}={\rm DC}_{j}[Z_{*}]\lambda_{*}\big(1+O(|\ln T|^{-\frac{1}{2}})\big),\quad|p_{j}|\sim\lambda_{*},\quad|\dot{p}_{j}|\sim(T-t)^{-1}\lambda_{*}.

Recalling
p_{j}=\lambda_{j}e^{i\gamma_{j}}
,
\lambda_{j}=|p_{j}|
,
\gamma_{j}=\arctan({\rm{Im}}(p_{j})/{\rm{Re}}(p_{j}))
, it follows that

|{\partial}_{t}(p_{j}^{c_{3}}|p_{j}|^{c_{4}})|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{c_{3}+c_{4}}(T-t)^{-1}\mbox{ \ for \ }c_{3},c_{4}\in\mathbb{R},\quad|\dot{\gamma}_{j}|=|p_{j}|^{-2}|{\rm{Im}}(\dot{p}_{j}){\rm{Re}}(p_{j})-{\rm{Im}}(p_{j}){\rm{Re}}(\dot{p}_{j})|\lesssim(T-t)^{-1}.

Hence, the ansatz (
3.2
) holds.
Direct calculation concludes the properties of
\lambda_{j},\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\gamma_{j}
in Theorem
1
.

Step 3.

To find a solution to the outer problem (

), it suffices to solve the following fixed-point problem:

\displaystyle\Phi_{\rm{out}}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$(2)$}}}}:=\Gamma_{\Phi,U_{*}}**\mathcal{G}+\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{n=1}^{3}c_{mn2}(\Gamma_{\Phi,U_{*}}*\vartheta_{mn}),\ \mathcal{G}=\mathcal{G}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}],\ c_{mn2}=c_{mn2}\big[\Phi,U_{*},\mathcal{G}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]\big]

)
under the parameter assumptions (

,

\tilde{p}_{j}:=\mathcal{P}\big[{\rm DC}_{j}[\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]]\big]

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[\tilde{p}_{j}](t)={\rm DC}_{j}[\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]](t)+\mathcal{R}_{0}\big[{\rm DC}_{j}[\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]]\big](t),\quad t\in[0,T],

\displaystyle\tilde{p}_{j}={\rm DC}_{j}\big[\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]\big](T)\big(1+O(|\ln T|^{-1})\big)\lambda_{*},

We define a mapping

\mathcal{T}_{p_{j1}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]:=\tilde{p}_{j}-p_{j0},\quad\mathcal{T}_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}}:=(\mathcal{T}_{p_{11}},\mathcal{T}_{p_{21}},\dots,\mathcal{T}_{p_{N1}}).

By (

), (

Orthogonality equation (

) gives a mapping of

.

By (

), (
4.37
),
\int_{0}^{\infty}w_{\rho_{j}}(\rho_{j})\cos w(\rho_{j})\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{-2\rho_{j}(\rho_{j}^{2}-1)}{(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{3}}d\rho_{j}=0
, and

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|\lambda_{j}\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{l}_{j,1}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}](\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}\Big|\lesssim\lambda^{\Theta+\alpha\beta}_{*}+\lambda^{\alpha}_{*}.

By (

),

\big|\lambda_{j}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},1}(\rho_{j},t)\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho_{j})\rho_{j}d\rho_{j}\big|\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}^{2}\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}
.
By (

),
|\lambda_{j}^{-1}c_{*1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))|\lesssim R_{0}^{-1}=\lambda_{*}^{\delta_{0}/6}
.
In order for

, we take

\nu-\delta_{0}>1/2,\quad 0<2\epsilon_{\xi}<\min\left\{\Theta+\alpha\beta,\alpha,2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1,\delta_{0}/6\right\}.

By (

), (
7.15
), (
7.16
), it follows that

(\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}},\mathcal{T}_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}},\mathcal{T}_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}})

maps

to itself.

By Proposition
6.3
, since the right-hand side of the outer problem is in the weighted-

space, one can obtain more regularity for

compared with the norm

.

By (

) in Proposition
5.1
,
\partial_{t}\mathcal{T}_{{\mathbf{p}}_{j1}}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}]
has Hölder continuity.

Recall the terms in the right-hand side of (
5.3
). By (

), (

) in the proof of Proposition

,

has quantitative Hölder continuity from the time Hölder continuity of the re-gluing outer problem
\psi_{o,1}
.
Together with the time continuity in the norms of the inner and outer problems in (
4.28
) and (
4.34
), it follows that
\mathcal{T}_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}}
is Hölder continuous, and thus the Schauder fixed-point theorem gives a fixed point for
(\mathcal{T}_{\rm{o}},\mathcal{T}_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}},\mathcal{T}_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}})

in
B_{\rm{out}}\times B_{{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}}\times B_{\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}}
. Namely, we find a solution
\big(\Phi_{\rm{out}}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}],{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 0}+{\mathbf{p}}_{\cdot 1}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}],\dot{\bm{\xi}}^{[\cdot]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]\big)
of (
4.16
) and (
5.1
).

Step 4.

Denote the right-hand side of the non-orthogonal inner problem (
4.42
) as

F_{\rm{no}}:=\lambda_{j}^{2}\Big(\tilde{l}_{j,0}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]\Big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}+\Big((\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},0}\Big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}+\mathbf{R}_{0}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},\lambda_{j},\gamma_{j}]+\Big(\int_{0}^{2}\eta(r)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}^{2}(r)rdr\Big)^{-1}\Big(c_{*0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))\eta(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)\Big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}.

By (
4.39
),

\big|\lambda_{j}^{2}\big(\tilde{l}_{j,0}^{\#}[\Phi_{\rm{out}}]\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big|\lesssim\big(\lambda_{*}^{1+\Theta+\alpha\beta}+\lambda_{*}^{1+\alpha}\big)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{\alpha-2}
.
By (
7.3
),
\big|\big((\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j},0}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-3}
.
Under the parameter assumption (
5.12
), by Proposition
5.1
,
\mathbf{R}_{0}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},\lambda_{j},\gamma_{j}]
given in (
4.43
) satisfies

\big|\mathbf{R}_{0}[\Phi_{\rm{out}},\lambda_{j},\gamma_{j}]\big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}(T-t)^{\tilde{m}+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}}|\ln(T-t)|^{-\varpi}\eta(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)
.
By (
7.6
),

\big|\big(c_{*0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\tau_{j}(t))\eta(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big|\lesssim R_{0}^{1-\ell_{0}}\ln R_{0}\lambda_{*}\eta(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|)
.
Since
F_{\rm{no}}
is in mode
0
, under the restrictions of parameters

(7.17)

\displaystyle\Theta+\alpha\beta<1,\quad 1+\Theta+\alpha\beta-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},\quad 1+\alpha-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

\displaystyle 2\beta<\nu-\delta_{0}<1,\quad\tilde{m}+(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha/2<1,\quad 1+\tilde{m}+(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha/2-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

\displaystyle\delta_{0}(\ell_{0}-1)<6,\quad\delta_{0}(\ell_{0}-1)/6+1-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

Proposition
8.2
gives a mapping
\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j2]$}}}}={\mathcal{T}}_{00}^{2C_{\lambda}R}[F_{\rm{no}}]
for (
4.42
) satisfying

(7.18)

|\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j2]$}}}}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1},\quad\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j2]$}}}}\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0.

We sum up (
4.41
) and (
4.42
) together and set

\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]:=\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j1]$}}}}+\Phi_{\rm in}^{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j2]$}}}},\quad\mathbf{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]:=(\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[1]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}],\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[2]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}],\dots,\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[N]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]).

\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]
is the inverse mapping of (
4.17
) since (
5.1
) holds. By (
7.6
) and (
7.18
), it holds that

(7.19)

|\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l},\quad\mathcal{T}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0.

Step 5.
Recall
\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
4.18
).
By (
3.48
), (
7.4
), and the estimate of
\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
in (
7.3
), we have

(7.20)

|\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-3}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2-l}\mbox{ \ for \ }|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2C_{\lambda}R,

where for the last step, we require

(7.21)

\nu+\beta l-\delta_{0}-1<0,\quad\delta_{0}<\nu.

Claim:
Given
y_{*},\tau_{*}
satisfying
|y_{*}|\leq 2C_{\lambda}R(t(\tau_{*}))\ll\tau_{*}^{1/2}
and
\tilde{\rho}=|y_{*}|/9
, it holds that

(7.22)

\big[\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(y_{*}+\tilde{\rho}z,\tau_{*}+\tilde{\rho}^{2}s)}\big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}(t(\tau_{j}))|_{\tau_{j}=\tau_{*}}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2-l}

provided

(7.23)

\delta_{0}<\nu,\quad 0<l<1,\quad\nu-\delta_{0}<\min\{\Theta+1-\beta,1-\beta l\},\quad 0<\beta<1/2.

Proof of (
7.22
).

In this proof, for brevity, we denote
(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})=(y_{*}+\tilde{\rho}z,\tau_{*}+\tilde{\rho}^{2}s)
with variables
(z,s)\in Q_{2}^{-}(0)
and abuse
\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})
to denote
\lambda_{*}(t(\tau_{j}))|_{\tau_{j}=\tau_{*}}
. Obviously,
|\tilde{z}|\sim|y_{*}|
,
\tilde{s}\sim\tau_{*}
.

In
y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
variable,
\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\eta\big(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}/(\lambda_{*}R)\big)
. By Lemma
6.1

(2)
and (
4.28
) for
\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, one has

\displaystyle\langle y_{*}\rangle\big([\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\big)+[\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\langle y_{*}\rangle\big([\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\big)

\displaystyle+[\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\|\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-l}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}.

Similar to (
7.3
), by Lemma
6.1
, provided
\delta_{0}<\nu
, we have

[\mathcal{H}_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-3-2l}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2-l}.

\bullet

\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}
estimates about the coupling terms from the outer problem in
\mathcal{H}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

(7.24)

(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\tilde{L}_{W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}[Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm{out}}]=(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\big(|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Pi_{W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm{out}})-2\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}((Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm{out}})\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big).

By (
E.7
) and (
4.27
), for
\lambda_{j}=\lambda_{j}(t(\tau_{j}))
,
\xi_{j}=\xi_{j}(t(\tau_{j}))
, we have

(7.25)

\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\ln\tau_{*}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1-\beta}(\tau_{*})+|\ln T|^{-2}\tau_{*}^{-1}(\ln\tau_{*})^{4}+\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})|y_{*}|.

By (
4.34
) and Lemma
6.1

(2)
, we get

(7.26)

\displaystyle[\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})\tilde{\rho}\sim\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})|y_{*}|,

\displaystyle[(\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}})(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim(\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+\alpha\beta}(\tau_{*})+\lambda_{*}^{\alpha}(\tau_{*}))|y_{*}|^{\alpha}.

We will give
\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}
estimates of two typical terms in (
7.24
), and the remaining two terms can be handled similarly. By (
7.25
) and (
7.26
), we estimate

\displaystyle\Big[(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Big(W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\big(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big)\Big)W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-4}\big(\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+[\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-4}\big(\ln\tau_{*}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1-\beta}(\tau_{*})+|\ln T|^{-2}\tau_{*}^{-1}(\ln\tau_{*})^{4}+\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})|y_{*}|\big)\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2-l}

provided
0<l<1
,
\nu-\delta_{0}<\min\{\Theta+1-\beta,1\}
.
By (
4.34
) and (
7.26
), one has

\displaystyle\Big[(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\Big\{\Big[W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Big(Q_{-\gamma_{j}}\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\big(\Phi_{\rm{out}}(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big)\Big)\Big]\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2}\big(1+[(\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}})(\lambda_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t(\tau_{j}))\big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2}\big(1+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+\alpha\beta}(\tau_{*})|y_{*}|^{\alpha}\big)\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}(\tau_{*})\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2-l}

provided
0<\beta<1/2
,
\nu-\delta_{0}<1-\beta l
.

\bullet

\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}
estimates about

\lambda_{j}^{2}\big(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+e^{i\theta_{j}}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}
,
where
M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
,
M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
are defined in (
3.46
) and (
3.43
), respectively. Back to the vector form, for
k=0,1
, by (
2.6
) and (
2.5
), we have

\displaystyle\big(e^{ik\theta_{j}}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}=\Big[\cos(k\theta_{j}){\rm Re}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\sin(k\theta_{j}){\rm Im}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\Big]E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+\Big[\cos(k\theta_{j}){\rm Im}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sin(k\theta_{j}){\rm Re}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\Big]E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\rm Re}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big[\cos(k\theta_{j})E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+\sin(k\theta_{j})E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\Big]+{\rm Im}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big[\cos(k\theta_{j})E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\sin(k\theta_{j})E^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}\Big]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\rm Re}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\cos((k-1)\theta_{j})+\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\cos(k\theta_{j})\cos\theta_{j}\\
\sin((k-1)\theta_{j})+\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\cos(k\theta_{j})\sin\theta_{j}\\
-\cos(k\theta_{j})\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\end{bmatrix}+{\rm Im}M_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}\sin((k-1)\theta_{j})-\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\sin(k\theta_{j})\cos\theta_{j}\\
\cos((k-1)\theta_{j})-\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\sin(k\theta_{j})\sin\theta_{j}\\
\sin(k\theta_{j})\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\end{bmatrix}.

For mode
1
,

\lambda_{j}^{2}\big(e^{i\theta_{j}}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}=\frac{2\lambda_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big(-\dot{\xi}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}-1+\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\cos^{2}\theta_{j}\\
\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\cos\theta_{j}\sin\theta_{j}\\
-\cos\theta_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\end{bmatrix}+\dot{\xi}_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\sin\theta_{j}\cos\theta_{j}\\
1-\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\sin^{2}\theta_{j}\\
\sin\theta_{j}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\end{bmatrix}\Big).

Then

\big[\big(\lambda_{j}^{2}\big(e^{i\theta_{j}}M_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big)(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})\big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1+\epsilon_{\xi}}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\epsilon}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-2-l},

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we require
\nu-\delta_{0}<1+\epsilon_{\xi}-\beta l
.

For mode
0
,

(7.27)

\lambda_{j}^{2}(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}=\lambda_{j}^{2}\Big({\rm Re}M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\cos\theta_{j}+\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\cos\theta_{j}\\
-\sin\theta_{j}+\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\sin\theta_{j}\\
-\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\end{bmatrix}+{\rm Im}M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\sin\theta_{j}\\
\cos\theta_{j}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\Big).

The vanishing in
M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
3.46
) as
\rho_{j}\to 0^{+}
makes the error (
7.27
) involving
\cos\theta_{j}
,
\sin\theta_{j}
smooth at the origin. By (
3.46
) and Lemma
6.1
,

\big[\big(\lambda_{j}^{2}(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big)(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})\big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-3}\big(m_{01}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\lambda_{*}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-3},

where
m_{01}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y_{*},\tau_{*})
,
m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y_{*},\tau_{*})
are defined as

\displaystyle m_{01}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=m_{01}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y_{*},\tau_{*}):=\Big\|\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)K_{0}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}

\displaystyle+\Big\|\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}K_{0\zeta_{j}}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\Big\|\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}^{2}K_{0\zeta_{j}\zeta_{j}}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))},

\displaystyle m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y_{*},\tau_{*}):=\Big[\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)K_{0}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}

\displaystyle+\Big[\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}K_{0\zeta_{j}}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}+\Big[\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}^{2}K_{0\zeta_{j}\zeta_{j}}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds\Big|_{(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j})=(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})}\Big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))},

with
\zeta_{j}=\zeta_{j}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j},s)=\frac{\lambda_{j}^{2}(t(\tau_{j}))(1+|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}
.
By (
3.18
) and (
3.21
), we have
m_{01}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lesssim 1
.
To estimate
m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, we consider the following more general form, which recovers all the terms in
m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
. Set

g_{0}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\tau_{j}):=\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds,\quad\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j}):=c_{0}\zeta_{j}^{-1}(1-e^{-d_{0}\zeta_{j}})+\sum\limits_{k=1}^{n}c_{i}\zeta_{j}^{k-1}e^{-d_{k}\zeta_{j}},

where
c_{k},d_{k}
are complex constants and
\mathrm{Re}(d_{k})>0
for
k=0,1,\dots,n
. It is easy to see that

(7.28)

\left|\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})\right|+\left|\zeta_{j}{\partial}_{\zeta_{j}}\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})\right|+\left|\zeta_{j}^{2}{\partial}_{\zeta_{j}\zeta_{j}}\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})\right|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}\leq 1\}}+\zeta_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta_{j}>1\}}.

Since

\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}g_{0}=\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)}{t(\tau_{j})-s}{\partial}_{\zeta_{j}}\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})\frac{2\lambda_{j}^{2}(t(\tau_{j}))y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{t(\tau_{j})-s}ds=\int_{-T}^{t(\tau_{j})}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)}{t(\tau_{j})-s}\zeta_{j}{\partial}_{\zeta_{j}}\mathcal{K}(\zeta_{j})\frac{2y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{1+|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}}ds,

by (
7.28
) and (
3.21
), we have
|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}g_{0}|\lesssim 1
. By Lemma
6.1

(2)
, then

[g_{0}(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim 1
and
m_{02}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lesssim 1
. Thus,

\big[\big(\lambda_{j}^{2}(M_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})_{\mathbb{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big)(\tilde{z},\tilde{s})\big]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\lambda_{*}\langle y_{*}\rangle^{-3}.

We complete the proof of (
7.22
).
∎

By (
7.19
), (
7.20
), (
7.22
),
Proposition
6.2
, and the scaling argument, due to the small quantity
\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon}
, we have
\mathbf{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]\in\mathcal{B}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}
. Since
p_{j},~\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
satisfy (
3.2
),
\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
is Hölder continuous, and
\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l,\varsigma_{\rm{in}}}
,
\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}

\lesssim 1
, then
\mathcal{H}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
is Hölder continuous. Applying the Schauder estimate to (
4.17
) and by changing time variable from
t
to
\tau_{j}
, we have
\mathbf{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]\in C^{2+c,(2+c)/2}(\mathcal{D}_{2C_{\lambda}R})
with a small constant
c\in(0,1)
. By the Schauder fixed-point theorem, we can find a fixed point of
\mathbf{T}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}[\mathbf{\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}}]
in
\mathcal{B}_{\rm{in}}^{[\cdot]}
.

Step 6.

Combining restrictions (
4.32
), (
6.7
), (
E.37
), (
D.1
) for the outer problem, parameter assumptions in Proposition
5.1
, (
5.12
), (
7.16
) for reduced equations, and (
4.29
), (
7.5
), (
7.17
), (
7.21
), (
7.23
) for the inner problems, we need to solve the following system of inequalities of
parameters:

\displaystyle\nu-\delta_{0}>1/2,\quad 0<\Theta<\beta,\quad\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad 3\beta<1+\Theta,\quad\beta(l+1)-1+\nu-\delta_{0}-\Theta>0,

\displaystyle\Theta+2\beta-1<0,\quad 0<\delta_{0}<\nu<1,\quad 2\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad 0<\alpha<1,\ \Theta<\alpha/2,

\displaystyle 0<\sigma_{0}<\beta,\ \beta-\sigma_{0}-\frac{\alpha}{2}<0,\ 1-\sigma_{0}-(1+\alpha)(1-\beta)<0,\ \Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,

\displaystyle 0<A_{\rm{o,h}}<\min\{\Theta+(1-\beta)(1-\alpha),1-2\sigma_{0}-\alpha(1-\beta),1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\},

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}\in(0,1/2),\quad\Theta\in(0,\flat),\quad\varpi-1-2\Theta<0,\quad\tilde{m}<\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta),

\displaystyle 0<2\epsilon_{\xi}<\min\{\Theta+\alpha\beta,\alpha,2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1,\delta_{0}/6\},\quad 0<\varsigma_{\rm{in}}<1,

\displaystyle\delta_{0}<6\beta,\quad\ell_{0}\in(1,3),\quad 2-\ell_{0}\leq-l,\quad\nu+\beta l-1<\delta_{0}/6,\quad 0<l<1,

\displaystyle 1+\Theta+\alpha\beta-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},\quad 1+\alpha-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

\displaystyle 2\beta<\nu-\delta_{0},\quad\tilde{m}+(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha/2<1,\quad 1+\tilde{m}+(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha/2-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

\displaystyle\delta_{0}(\ell_{0}-1)/6+1-2\beta>\nu-\delta_{0},

where the constant

\flat>0
is given in Proposition
5.1
.
With the assistance of Mathematica, sound choices satisfying all the restrictions are given below, and the proof of Theorem
1
is completed.

\displaystyle 0<\Theta<\min\Big\{\frac{15}{2479},\flat\Big\},\quad\beta=\frac{15+\Theta}{60},\quad\sigma_{0}=\frac{1}{50},\quad\alpha=\frac{9}{10},\quad\ell_{0}=l+2,

\displaystyle 3\Theta<\delta_{0}<\frac{1}{40}(-27+108\beta+120\Theta),\quad 1-2\beta+\delta_{0}+\Theta<\nu<\frac{1}{3}(3-6\beta+4\delta_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{10}{9}\Big(-\frac{11}{10}+\frac{11\beta}{5}-2\delta_{0}+2\nu-2\Theta\Big)<\alpha_{0}<\frac{1}{2},\quad\frac{1}{2}\Big(-\frac{29}{10}-\frac{9\alpha_{0}}{10}+4\beta-2\delta_{0}+2\nu\Big)<\tilde{m}<-\frac{9}{10}+\frac{9\beta}{10}+\Theta,

\displaystyle\max\Big\{0,-1+\frac{1-\nu+\delta_{0}+\Theta}{\beta},-1+\frac{6}{\delta_{0}}(\nu-\delta_{0}+2\beta-1)\Big\}<l<\min\Big\{1,\frac{1}{\beta}\big(\frac{\delta_{0}}{6}+1-\nu\big)\Big\},

\displaystyle 0<A_{\rm{o,h}}<\min\{\Theta+(1-\beta)(1-\alpha),1-2\sigma_{0}-\alpha(1-\beta),1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\},\quad\varpi<1+2\Theta,

\displaystyle 0<2\epsilon_{\xi}<\min\{\Theta+\alpha\beta,\alpha,2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1,\delta_{0}/6\},\quad 0<\varsigma_{\rm{in}}<1.

7.2.
Proof of Corollary
1.1

In this subsection, based on the weighted spaces for the solution constructed, we shall show the convergence results in Corollary
1.1
.
Throughout this subsection, we adopt the usual Sobolev norm for the mapping between Euclidean spaces.
M>0
is an arbitrary constant. Given a function
f(x,t)
, we use
f(t)
to denote
f(x,t)
for simplicity. A function
f(t)\rightarrow 0
means that
f(t)
converges to
0
under some norms as
t\to T
.

Recall (
4.1
). The solution in Theorem
1
has the form

(7.29)

\displaystyle u(x,t)=U_{*}+\Phi_{\rm per},\quad\Phi_{\rm per}:=AU_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*},

where
U_{*}
is the multi-bubble profile defined in (
3.3
), and
A
is given in (
4.4
). Since

|\Phi|\ll 1,\quad\||U_{*}(\cdot,t)|-1\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*},\quad|A|\lesssim\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}

by (
3.4
), (
4.5
), we obtain

\|\Phi_{\rm per}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(0,T))}\ll 1.

It is straightforward to get

\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}=U_{*}\nabla_{x}A+A\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\nabla_{x}\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\nabla_{x}U_{*}-U_{*}\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*}),

where we denote
U_{*}\nabla_{x}f=(U_{*}{\partial}_{x_{1}}f,U_{*}{\partial}_{x_{2}}f)
for a scalar function
f
. Applying (
E.26
) to
U_{*}\nabla_{x}A
;
(
4.5
), (
E.1
), (
E.28
) to
A\nabla_{x}U_{*}-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\nabla_{x}U_{*}
;
(
E.16
), (
E.23
) to
\nabla_{x}\Phi-U_{*}\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})
, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big\{\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big[1+\big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R\big)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}\Big]

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big\}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}.

Here,
\big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R\big)\|\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R
. Thus,

(7.30)

\Phi_{\rm per}\in L^{\infty}\big((0,T);H^{1}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^{2})\big),\quad\lambda_{*}(t)\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon}(t)

with a small constant
\epsilon>0
. We define

(7.31)

u_{*}(x):=\Phi_{\rm per}(x,T)+W(\infty).

By definition, we have

u(x,t)-u_{*}(x)-\sum_{j=1}^{N}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Big[W\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{j}}\Big)-W(\infty)\Big]=\Phi_{\rm per}(x,t)-\Phi_{\rm per}(x,T).

Claim: For
\Phi
given in (
4.1
),

(7.32)

\Phi(x,T)=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,T)\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)+\Phi_{\rm out}(x,T),

(7.33)

\|\Phi(t)-\Phi(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\rightarrow 0,\quad\|\nabla_{x}\Phi(t)-\nabla_{x}(\Phi(T))\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0.

Combining (
E.7
), (
E.9
), and (
3.23
), we have

(7.34)

\|\Phi(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})},\quad\|\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi(T)\right)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim 1.

Proof of (
7.32
) and (
7.33
).

For
\Phi_{\rm out}
solved in
B_{\rm{out}}
defined in (
4.35
), we have

(7.35)

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm out}(t)-\Phi_{\rm out}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R+(T-t),

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(t)-\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm out}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}+(T-t)^{\alpha/2}.

For
\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{\rm in}
solved in
B_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
defined in (
4.30
), since
0<l<1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{\rm in}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}},

\displaystyle\int_{B_{M}}|\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{\rm in}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)|^{2}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\int_{B_{M}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-2l}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}+2}\int_{|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq C(M)\lambda_{*}^{-1}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-2l}dy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}+2l},

(7.36)

\displaystyle\int_{B_{M}}\Big|\nabla_{x}\Big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Big)\Big|^{2}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-2}\int_{B_{M}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-2l-2}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}.

In particular,
\Phi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{\rm in}(T)=0
, which implies (
7.32
).
It is easy to get

(7.37)

\|\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)-\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\rightarrow 0,\quad\|\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)-\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\rightarrow 0.

Next, we consider
\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}
defined in (
3.11
) with
\mu=3
. Recalling (
3.17
), we denote

\mathfrak{A}:=\frac{a+ib}{4},\quad\zeta_{j}(t):=\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|^{2}+\lambda_{j}(t)^{2}}{t-s},\quad\zeta_{j}(T)=\frac{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}}{T-s}.

By (
3.2
), we have
|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\ll\lambda_{*}
.
Recall (
3.11
), (
3.16
), (
2.3
) and
\mu=3
