,

(7.38)

\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)=\frac{-2|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}\Big[\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds,0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}},

(7.39)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)-\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{j})^{-1}\Big|\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds-\int_{-T}^{T}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds\Big|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}-\frac{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|}\bigg|\Big|\int_{-T}^{T}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds\Big|.

We will estimate the above term by term. Denote
\tilde{r}_{j}:=|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|
. Then
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{j}\sim\tilde{r}_{j}+\lambda_{*}
, and

(7.40)

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}-\frac{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{|x-\theta\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-(1-\theta)q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}(|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{j})}{[|x-\theta\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-(1-\theta)q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\theta\lambda_{j}]^{4}}d\theta\lesssim(|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{*})\int_{0}^{1}(\tilde{r}_{j}+\theta\lambda_{*})^{-2}d\theta.

By (
3.21
), we have

\Big|\int_{-T}^{T}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds\Big|=\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\Big|\int_{-T}^{T}\frac{\dot{p}_{j}(s)}{T-s}\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)\zeta_{j}(T)^{-1}ds\Big|\lesssim\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}.

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds-\int_{-T}^{T}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[\int_{-T}^{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}+\int_{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}+\int_{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{t}\Big]\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds-\int_{t}^{T}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds,

where for the first part,

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{-T}^{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds\Big|\lesssim\int_{-T}^{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)||\zeta_{j}(T)-\zeta_{j}(t)|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|\Big[\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\Big)+\frac{\tilde{r}_{j}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}{t-s}\Big]ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\ln\Big(\frac{(t+T)\big[T-\big(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\big)_{+}\big]}{2T\big[t-\big(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\big)_{+}\big]}\Big)+(\tilde{r}_{j}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{*}^{2})\ln\Big(\frac{t+T}{t-\big(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\big)_{+}}\Big).

For the second part,

\Big|\int_{(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds\Big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2}.

For the third part,
if
\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{T-t}
, we have

\Big|\int_{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds\Big|\lesssim\tilde{r}_{j}^{2};

if
\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}
,
t>T/2
, by similar calculations as in the first part, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\int_{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{t-(T-t)}+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\Big)\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}^{t-(T-t)}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|\Big[\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\Big)+\frac{\tilde{r}_{j}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}{t-s}\Big]ds+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\frac{|\ln T|}{\ln^{2}(T-t)}+(\tilde{r}_{j}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|+\lambda_{*}^{2})\ln\Big(\frac{t-(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{T-t}\Big)+\lambda_{*},

where we used
\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\sim(T-t)(T-s)^{-2}
for
s\leq t-(T-t)
.

For the last part,
by (
3.2
),

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\dot{p}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(T)}\big)ds\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[\Big(\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\int_{t}^{T-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}}\frac{|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|}{T-s}ds+\int_{T-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}}^{T}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}(s)|ds\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{T-t}\}}+\lambda_{*}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{T-t}\}}+\lambda_{*}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}\}}.

In sum, collecting the above estimates, we conclude that for
t>T/2
,

\displaystyle\big|\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)-\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\big(2T(t+T)^{-1}\big)+\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\Big(\frac{T-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)+\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t+T}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{T-t}\}}\tilde{r}_{j}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}\}}\Big[\tilde{r}_{j}\frac{|\ln T|}{\ln^{2}(T-t)}+\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t-(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{T-t}\Big)+\lambda_{*}(T-t)^{-1/2}\Big].

Here, if
t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\Big(\frac{T-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)=\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\Big(1+\frac{T-t}{\lambda_{*}^{2}+\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{j}|\ln(T-t)|\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}<\sqrt{T-t}\}}+\tilde{r}_{j}^{-1}(T-t)\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}\geq\sqrt{T-t}\}}\lesssim\sqrt{T-t}|\ln(T-t)|,

since
\frac{T-t}{2(\sqrt{T-t})^{2}}\leq\frac{T-t}{\lambda_{*}^{2}+\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}}\leq\frac{T-t}{\lambda_{*}^{2}}
if
\tilde{r}_{j}<\sqrt{T-t}
. Thus,

(7.41)

\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\Big(\frac{T-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\leq t-\lambda_{*}^{2}\}}\sqrt{T-t}|\ln(T-t)|+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}>t-\lambda_{*}^{2}\}}\tilde{r}_{j}\ln(T/t).

Similarly,

(7.42)

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t+T}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\leq t-\lambda_{*}^{2}\}}\lambda_{*}|\ln(T-t)|+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}>t-\lambda_{*}^{2}\}}\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t+T}{t}\Big),

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}\}}\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t-(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{T-t}\Big)=\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{T-t}<\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{t}\}}\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}}{T-t}\Big)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\max\{\sqrt{T-t},\sqrt{t}\}\}}\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t}{T-t}\Big).

It follows that for all
\tilde{r}_{j}^{2}\leq M
, we have

(7.43)

\big|\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)-\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)\big|\rightarrow 0\mbox{ \ uniformly as \ }t\rightarrow T,

From (
7.35
), (
7.36
), (
7.37
), and (
7.43
), we obtain the first part of (
7.33
).

Using (
7.38
), we have

{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)=-2f_{1}(t)\Big[\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\big(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}\big)ds,0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}+f_{2}(t)\Big[\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}(t)e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}ds,0\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}},

where

\displaystyle f_{1}(t):=

\displaystyle\frac{2(x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1})\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}+\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}(-1)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)^{-2}3|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|(x_{1}-\xi_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})

\displaystyle+\frac{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}}(-1)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{-3/2}(x_{1}-\xi_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}),

\displaystyle f_{2}(t):=

\displaystyle\frac{-2|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\big[x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(x_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]}{\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{3}+\lambda_{j}^{3}\big)\big(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}2\mathfrak{A}\frac{x_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}}{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+\lambda_{j}^{2}}.

It is easy to get
|f_{1}(t)|+|f_{2}(t)|\lesssim(\tilde{r}_{j}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}
.
Similar to (
7.40
), we have

|f_{1}(t)-f_{1}(T)|+|f_{2}(t)-f_{2}(T)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\int_{0}^{1}(\tilde{r}_{j}+\theta\lambda_{*})^{-3}d\theta.

\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)(1-e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)})ds
has been dealt with in the estimate of (
7.39
).

\int_{-T}^{t}\dot{p}_{j}(s)\zeta_{j}(t)e^{-\mathfrak{A}\zeta_{j}(t)}ds
can be handled similarly. In sum, for
t>T/2
,

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)-{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)|\lesssim\ln(1+\tilde{r}_{j}^{-1}\lambda_{*})

\displaystyle\quad+(\tilde{r}_{j}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}\Big\{\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\big(2T(t+T)^{-1}\big)+\tilde{r}_{j}\ln\Big(\frac{T-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)+\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t+T}{t-(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{*}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}\leq\sqrt{T-t}\}}\tilde{r}_{j}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{j}>\sqrt{T-t}\}}\Big[\tilde{r}_{j}\frac{|\ln T|}{\ln^{2}(T-t)}+\lambda_{*}\ln\Big(\frac{t-(t-\tilde{r}_{j}^{2})_{+}}{T-t}\Big)+\lambda_{*}(T-t)^{-1/2}\Big]\Big\}.

And
{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)
can be dealt with similarly. Using (
7.41
), (
7.42
), and
\int_{\tilde{r}_{j}\leq M}(\tilde{r}_{j}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}dx\lesssim\ln(M+2)+|\ln\lambda_{*}|
,
(\tilde{r}_{j}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}\tilde{r}_{j}\leq 1
, we get

(7.44)

\int_{B_{M}}\Big|\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,t)-\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,T)\Big|^{2}dx\to 0.

Applying (
7.35
) to
\Phi_{\rm out}
, (
7.36
) to
\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, and (
7.37
), (
3.23
), (
7.43
), (
7.44
) to
\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}
, we conclude the second part of (
7.33
).
∎

Recall
U_{*}
given in (
3.3
). The pointwise limit as
t
goes to
T
is given by

U_{*}(T)=\begin{cases}[0,0,1]^{\mathsf{tr}}=U_{\infty},&\mbox{ \ if \ }x\not\in\{q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\ |\ j=1,2,\dots,N\}\\
[0,0,-1]^{\mathsf{tr}},&\mbox{ \ if \ }x\in\{q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\ |\ j=1,2,\dots,N\}.\end{cases}

U_{*}(t)
does not converge in
L_{\rm{loc}}^{\infty}
since
U_{*}(T)
is not continuous. Instead,

(7.45)

\|U_{*}(\cdot,t)-U_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}(\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)\})}\to 0.

By (
E.15
), one has

(7.46)

\displaystyle\Phi(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)=0,\quad j=1,\dots,N,\mbox{ \ and then \ }((\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*})(T)=(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})U_{\infty},

\displaystyle\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}-((\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*})(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq\|(\Phi\cdot(U_{*}-U_{\infty}))U_{*}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle+\|(\Phi\cdot U_{\infty})(U_{*}-U_{\infty})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\|[(\Phi-\Phi(T))\cdot U_{\infty}]U_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0,

where we used (
E.15
), (
7.45
), (
7.33
) in the last step.

\displaystyle\|{\partial}_{x_{i}}[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}]-{\partial}_{x_{i}}[((\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*})(T)]\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|(\Phi\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*})U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|[{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi\cdot(U_{*}-U_{\infty})]U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}

(7.47)

\displaystyle+\|({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi\cdot U_{\infty})(U_{*}-U_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|[({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi(T))\cdot U_{\infty}]U_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0,

where for the last step, we used
\|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\leq C(M)\lambda_{*}|\ln\lambda_{*}|^{1/2}
and applied (
E.1
), (
E.15
) to
\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|(\Phi\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*})U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}
; (
E.16
), (
7.45
) to
\|[{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi\cdot(U_{*}-U_{\infty})]U_{*}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi\cdot U_{\infty})(U_{*}-U_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}
; (
7.33
) to
\|[({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi(T))\cdot U_{\infty}]U_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}
.

Recall
A
defined in (
4.4
) and
\Phi(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)=0
. It is straightforward to get

\displaystyle A(T)=[1-|\Phi(T)|^{2}+(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})^{2}]^{1/2}-1,\quad A(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)=0,\ j=1,2,\dots,N,\quad(AU_{*})(T)=A(T)U_{\infty},

(7.48)

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}(A(T))=-\frac{\nabla_{x}\big[\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\big]}{2(1+A(T))},\quad\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}=|\Phi(T)|^{2}-(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})^{2}.

Note that

|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2}=|\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}|^{2}=|\Phi|^{2}-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}+(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}(|U_{*}|^{2}-1).

By (
E.15
), (
7.33
), and (
7.45
), we have

\displaystyle\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(t)-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq\|\Phi(t)\cdot\left(U_{*}(t)-U_{*}(T)\right)\|_{L^{\infty}(\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|>3\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)\})}

\displaystyle+\|\Phi(t)\cdot\left(U_{*}(t)-U_{*}(T)\right)\|_{L^{\infty}(\cup_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)\})}+\|\left(\Phi(t)-\Phi(T)\right)\cdot U_{*}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0.

We can handle the convergence of the other terms involved in
A
directly. Then

(7.49)

\|A(t)-A(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0.

Similarly, using (
4.5
), (
E.15
), (
7.45
), and (
7.49
), we have

(7.50)

\displaystyle\|(AU_{*})(t)-(AU_{*})(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq\|A(t)(U_{*}(t)-U_{*}(T))\|_{L^{\infty}(\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|>3\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)\})}

\displaystyle+\|A(t)(U_{*}(t)-U_{*}(T))\|_{L^{\infty}(\cup_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}(t)R(t)\})}+\|(A(t)-A(T))U_{*}(T)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0.

Using
(AU_{*})(T)=A(T)U_{\infty}
, we write

\displaystyle{\partial}_{x_{i}}((AU_{*})(t)-(AU_{*})(T))=(U_{*}(t)-U_{\infty}){\partial}_{x_{i}}A(t)+A(t){\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*}(t)+U_{\infty}{\partial}_{x_{i}}(A(t)-A(T)),\quad i=1,2

and consider the following three terms

\displaystyle I_{1}:=\int_{B_{M}}|\nabla_{x}A(x,t)|^{2}|U_{*}(x,t)-U_{\infty}|^{2}dx,\quad I_{2}:=\int_{B_{M}}|A(x,t)|^{2}|\nabla_{x}U_{*}(x,t)|^{2}dx,

\displaystyle I_{3}:=\int_{B_{M}}|\nabla_{x}(A(x,t)-A(x,T))|^{2}dx.

Using (
E.26
), (
7.45
), and
\|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\leq C(M)\lambda_{*}|\ln\lambda_{*}|^{1/2}
, we have
I_{1}\rightarrow 0
.
By (
4.5
), (
E.15
), (
E.1
), and
\|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\leq C(M)\lambda_{*}|\ln\lambda_{*}|^{1/2}
, we have
I_{2}\rightarrow 0
.
For
I_{3}
, using
\nabla_{x}(A(T))
in (
7.48
) and
\nabla_{x}A
given in (
4.11
), we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}A-\nabla_{x}(A(T))|\lesssim\Big|\Big[(1+A)^{2}\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})+\nabla_{x}\big(\big|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big|^{2}\big)+2(1+A)\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\Big](1+A(T))

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\Big[\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\Big]\Big[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big|(1+A)^{2}\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})(1+A(T))+2(1+A)\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)(1+A(T))

\displaystyle+\Big[\nabla_{x}\Big(\big|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big|^{2}\Big)-\nabla_{x}\Big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\Big)\Big](1+A(T))+\nabla_{x}\Big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\Big)(A(T)-A)

\displaystyle+\nabla_{x}\Big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\Big)(1+A)(1-|U_{*}|^{2})-\nabla_{x}\Big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\Big)(1-|U_{*}|^{2})(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\Big|.

\bullet
By (
E.3
), we have

\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\to 0
in
L^{2}_{\rm loc}
.

\bullet
By (
3.4
), (
E.23
), (
E.19
), and (
E.3
),

|\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)|=|(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1/2}.

\bullet
Note that

\nabla_{x}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})=2\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*}).

From (
7.48
), we have

\displaystyle\big|{\partial}_{x_{i}}\big(\big|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big|^{2}\big)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\big)\big|\leq 2|\Phi\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi-\Phi(T)\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi(T))|+\big|(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}{\partial}_{x_{i}}\big(|U_{*}|^{2}\big)\big|

\displaystyle+2\big|\big(|U_{*}|^{2}-1\big)(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\big|+2\big|-(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})[{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi(T))\cdot U_{\infty}]\big|.

By (
7.33
) and (
7.34
),

\|\Phi\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi-\Phi(T)\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi(T))\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\rightarrow 0.

By (
E.15
) and (
E.3
),

\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}{\partial}_{x_{i}}(|U_{*}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\lesssim\||\Phi|^{2}|{\partial}_{x_{i}}(|U_{*}|^{2})|\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0.

By (
3.4
), (
E.19
), and (
E.23
),

\|(|U_{*}|^{2}-1)(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim\lambda_{*}\|(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0.

Finally,

\displaystyle\|-(\Phi\cdot U_{*}){\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})+(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})[{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi(T))\cdot U_{\infty}]\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle\|[(\Phi(T)-\Phi)\cdot U_{*}]{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|[\Phi(T)\cdot(U_{\infty}-U_{*})]{\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}

\displaystyle+\|(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})(\Phi\cdot{\partial}_{x_{i}}U_{*})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}+\|(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})[{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi\cdot(U_{\infty}-U_{*})]\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}

\displaystyle+\|(\Phi(T)\cdot U_{\infty})[({\partial}_{x_{i}}(\Phi(T))-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Phi)\cdot U_{\infty}]\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0,

where we used (
7.33
), (
E.23
); (
7.34
), (
7.45
); (
E.15
), (
E.1
); (
E.16
), (
7.45
); (
7.33
) in order for the last step. In sum,

\|{\partial}_{x_{i}}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})-{\partial}_{x_{i}}(|(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)(T)|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0.

\bullet
By (
7.48
) and (
7.34
),

(7.51)

\|\nabla_{x}(|(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)(T)|^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\lesssim 1.

Combining (
7.49
) and (
3.4
), we have

\|\nabla_{x}\big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\big)(A(T)-A)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\|\nabla_{x}\big(\big|\big(\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\big)(T)\big|^{2}\big)(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*})\left(1-|U_{*}|^{2}\right)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\to 0.

As a result, we conclude
I_{3}\to 0
. In sum,

(7.52)

\|\nabla_{x}((AU_{*})(t)-(AU_{*})(T))\|_{L^{2}(B_{M})}\to 0.

Consequently, applying (
7.31
), (
7.48
), (
7.46
), (
7.34
), (
7.51
) to
u_{*}
, and (
7.33
), (
7.46
), (
7.47
), (
7.50
), (
7.52
) to
\Phi_{\rm per}(t)-\Phi_{\rm per}(T)
, we attain

(7.53)

\displaystyle u_{*}(x)=A(x,T)U_{\infty}+\Phi(x,T)-(\Phi(x,T)\cdot U_{\infty})U_{\infty}+U_{\infty}\in H^{1}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^{2})\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),

\displaystyle\Phi_{\rm per}(t)-\Phi_{\rm per}(T)\rightarrow 0\mbox{ \ in \ }H^{1}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^{2}){\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}.

Next, we will prove weak-
*
convergence. Obviously,
|\nabla_{x}u|^{2}=|\nabla_{x}U_{*}|^{2}+2\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}+|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}|^{2}
,

|\nabla_{x}u_{*}|^{2}=|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}(x,T)|^{2}
,
|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}=8\lambda_{j}^{-2}\big(\lambda_{j}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1\big)^{-2}
.
Given a function
f\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
continuous at
q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, by dominated convergence theorem and
8\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(|z|^{2}+1)^{-2}dz=8\pi
, then

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(x,t)|^{2}f(x)dx=8\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(|z|^{2}+1)^{-2}f(\lambda_{j}z+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})dz\to 8\pi f(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}).

Given a constant
C_{1}>0
, by (
7.30
),

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\big(\lambda_{j}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1\big)^{-1}|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm per}(x,t)|\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq C_{1}\}}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon}|\ln\lambda_{*}|.

For
j\neq k
, by splitting
\mathbb{R}^{2}
into three parts
\{x\ |\ |x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}
,
\{x\ |\ |x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}
, and
\{x\ |\ \min\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|,|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\}>d_{q}\}
when estimating, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\big(\lambda_{j}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+1\big)^{-1}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\big(\lambda_{k}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{2}+1\big)^{-1}dx\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2}|\ln\lambda_{*}|.

Together with (
7.53
), given a function
f\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
with compact support and continuous at
q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
,
j=1,2,\dots,N
, we have

(7.54)

\lim_{t\to T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}f(x)|\nabla u(x,t)|^{2}dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}f(x)|\nabla u_{*}(x)|^{2}dx+\sum_{j=1}^{N}8\pi f(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}),

which is the weak-
*
convergence of the Radon measure. We complete the proof of Corollary
1.1
.

8.
Linear theory for the inner problems

In this section, we will establish the linear theory for the inner problem (
4.17
) in different modes. Since this section is rather independent of the other parts, we abuse the notation a bit and use
R
for more general cases.
Recall (
4.26
), in the time variable
\tau_{j}
, (
4.17
) is the usual parabolic system. Since the inner problems for
j=1,2,\dots,N
all have the same structure, we omit the subscripts or superscripts “
j
”, “
[j]
” in this section for brevity, and all spatial derivatives are about
y
. Consider

(8.1)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi\right)+H&&\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\\
&\Psi(y,\tau)\cdot W(y)=H(y,\tau)\cdot W(y)=0&&\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\end{aligned}\right.

where

(8.2)

L_{\rm{in}}\mathbf{f}:=\Delta\mathbf{f}+|\nabla W|^{2}\mathbf{f}-2\nabla\left(W\cdot\mathbf{f}\right)\cdot\nabla W+2\left(\nabla W\cdot\nabla\mathbf{f}\right)W,

\mathcal{D}_{R}:=\left\{(y,\tau)\ |\ \tau\in(\tau_{0},\infty),y\in B_{R}\right\},\quad B_{R}:=\left\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\ |\ |y|<R(\tau)\right\},\quad\tau_{0}\geq 2.

We call a function
f(\tau)
defined in
(\tau_{0},\infty)
of
algebraic power type
if
C^{-1}f_{*}(\tau)\leq f(\tau)\leq Cf_{*}(\tau)
, where
C\geq 1
is a constant, and

f_{*}(\tau)=c_{0}\tau^{c_{1}}\langle\ln\tau\rangle^{c_{2}}\langle\ln\langle\ln\tau\rangle\rangle^{c_{3}}\cdots
with finite multiplicity,
c_{0}>0
possibly depends on
\tau_{0}
,
c_{i}\in\mathbb{R}
,
i=1,2,\dots
, and then we define

(8.3)

{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[f]:=c_{1}.

Denote
{\mathbf{AP}}
as the set of algebraic power type functions. Obviously, for any
f_{1},f_{2}\in{\mathbf{AP}}
,
c\in\mathbb{R}
, we have
f_{1}f_{2},f_{1}/f_{2},f_{1}^{c}\in{\mathbf{AP}}
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{1}f_{2}]=\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{1}]+\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{2}]
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}[1/f_{1}]=-\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{1}]
, and
\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{1}^{c}]=c\mathbf{P}_{1}[f_{1}]
.
For
\tau\geq\tau_{0}\geq 9
and
f\in{\mathbf{AP}}
,
C_{f}^{-1}f(\tau)\leq f(s)\leq C_{f}f(\tau)
for all
\tau\leq s\leq 2\tau

with a constant
C_{f}>1
. If we assume, in addition,
\ln f\in{\mathbf{AP}}
, then
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\ln f]=0
.

Throughout this section, unless otherwise stated, we always assume that constants,
O(\cdot)
,
\lesssim
,
\sim
are independent of
\tau_{0}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}
for
|k|\geq 2
(used for mode
k
,
|k|\geq 2
), and

(8.4)

\displaystyle\tau_{0}\geq 9,\quad v(\tau),R(\tau),R_{0}(\tau)\in{\mathbf{AP}},\quad 0\leq{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[R_{0}]\leq{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[R]<1/2,\quad 2R_{0}(\tau)\leq R(\tau),

\displaystyle\inf_{s\geq\tau_{0}}R_{0}(s)\gg 1,\quad R^{2}\big(\ln R+(\ln\tau)^{m}\big)\leq C_{1}\tau,\quad R_{0}\in C^{1}(\tau_{0},\infty),\quad|R_{0}^{\prime}|=O(R_{0}^{-1})

for any
m\geq 0
with a constant
C_{1}>0
depending on
m
. Obviously, if
R_{0}^{\prime}=O(\tau^{-1}R_{0})
, then
|R_{0}^{\prime}|=O(R_{0}^{-1})
.

Suppose that
\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}(y,\tau)
,
H_{\mathbb{C}}(y,\tau)
defined by (
2.16
) have the following Fourier expansion respectively,

(8.5)

\displaystyle\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}(y,\tau)=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\psi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta},\quad\psi_{k}(\rho,\tau):=\Psi_{\mathbb{C},k}(\rho,\tau)=(2\pi)^{-1}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}(\rho e^{is},\tau)e^{-iks}ds,

\displaystyle H_{\mathbb{C}}(y,\tau)=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}h_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta},\quad h_{k}(\rho,\tau):=H_{\mathbb{C},k}(\rho,\tau)=(2\pi)^{-1}\int_{0}^{2\pi}H_{\mathbb{C}}(\rho e^{is},\tau)e^{-iks}ds,

where
\Psi_{\mathbb{C},k}
and
H_{\mathbb{C},k}
are defined in (
2.18
) and

y=\rho e^{i\theta},\quad\rho=|y|,\quad\theta=\arctan(y_{2}/\penalty y_{1}).

Using (
2.17
), we denote

\Psi_{k}(y,\tau):=\big(\psi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}},\quad H_{k}(y,\tau):=\big(h_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ for \ }k\in\mathbb{Z}.

It is easy to see that

(8.6)

|\Psi_{k}|=|\psi_{k}|,\quad|H_{k}|=|h_{k}|.

For
\ell\in{{\mathbb{R}}}
and
v(\tau)>0
and vectorial complex-valued function
{\mathbf{f}}
, we introduce the weighted topology

(8.7)

\|{\mathbf{f}}\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}:=\sup\limits_{(y,\tau)\in{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathcal{R}}}(v(\tau)\langle y\rangle^{-\ell})^{-1}|{\mathbf{f}}(y,\tau)|

with a scalar function
\mathcal{R}=\mathcal{R}(\tau)
. By (
8.6
) and (
8.5
), we have

(8.8)

\|\psi_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}=\|\Psi_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}},\quad\|h_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}=\|H_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\lesssim\|H\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}},\quad|\Psi(y,\tau)|\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}|\Psi_{k}(y,\tau)|.

For the convergence in (
8.8
) when summing up, we have to make the dependence on
k
very clear in the estimates of mode
k
,
|k|\geq 2
.

8.1.
Complex-valued form of the inner linear equation

The following lemma bridges the inner problem in the parabolic system form and the complex-scalar form.

Lemma 8.1
.

For
L_{\rm{in}}
defined in (
8.2
) and
\Psi\cdot W=0
, we have

(8.9)

\left[\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi\right)\right]\cdot W=0,

(8.10)

\left[\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi\right)\right]_{\mathbb{C}}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{\mathbb{C}},

where

\mathcal{L}_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}:=\Big[{\partial}_{\rho\rho}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}+i\frac{2\cos w(\rho)}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}+\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\Big]\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}.

Then (
8.1
) is equivalent to the complex-valued equation

(8.11)

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}+H_{\mathbb{C}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R}.

Under the Fourier expansion (
8.5
), then

(8.12)

\mathcal{L}_{\rm{in}}\big[e^{ik\theta}\psi_{k}\big]=e^{ik\theta}\mathcal{L}_{k}\psi_{k},

where

(8.13)

\mathcal{L}_{k}f:={\partial}_{\rho\rho}f+\frac{{\partial}_{\rho}f}{\rho}+V_{k}(\rho)f,\quad V_{k}(\rho):=-\frac{(k+1)^{2}\rho^{4}+(2k^{2}-6)\rho^{2}+(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}.

It follows that

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{k}\right)+H_{k}

is equivalent to

(8.14)

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{k}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{k}\psi_{k}+h_{k}.

Proof.

Set

\Psi(y,\tau)=\varphi_{1}(y,\tau)E_{1}(y)+\varphi_{2}(y,\tau)E_{2}(y),\mbox{ \ that is, \ }\Psi_{\mathbb{C}}=\varphi_{1}+i\varphi_{2}.

By (
2.8
), one has

\displaystyle\Delta(\varphi_{1}E_{1})=(\Delta\varphi_{1})E_{1}+2\Big({\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}{\partial}_{\rho}E_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}{\partial}_{\theta}E_{1}\Big)+\varphi_{1}\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}+\frac{{\partial}_{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{{\partial}_{\theta\theta}}{\rho^{2}}\Big)E_{1}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(\Delta\varphi_{1})E_{1}-2{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}w_{\rho}W+\frac{2}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}\cos wE_{2}

\displaystyle+\varphi_{1}\Big[-w_{\rho\rho}W-w_{\rho}^{2}E_{1}-\frac{1}{\rho}w_{\rho}W-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\cos w(\sin wW+\cos wE_{1})\Big]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big[\Delta\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{1}\Big(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\Big)\Big]E_{1}+\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}E_{2}+\Big[-2w_{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{1}\Big(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\Big)\Big]W

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{1}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{1}\Big)E_{1}+\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}E_{2}+\Big(-2w_{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}-\frac{2\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{1}\Big)W,

where we used
w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}-\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}=0
for the last equality. Similarly,

\displaystyle\Delta(\varphi_{2}E_{2})=\Delta(\varphi_{2})E_{2}-2\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2}(\sin wW+\cos wE_{1})-\varphi_{2}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}E_{2}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta}\varphi_{2}E_{1}+\Big(\partial_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{2}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{2}\Big)E_{2}-\frac{2\sin w}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta}\varphi_{2}W.

Thus

(8.15)

\displaystyle\Delta\Psi=

\displaystyle\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{1}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{1}-\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2}\Big)E_{1}

\displaystyle+\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{2}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{2}+\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}\Big)E_{2}

\displaystyle+\Big(-2w_{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}-\frac{2\sin w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2}-\frac{2\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{1}\Big)W.

By (
2.8
),

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{\rho}W=w_{\rho}E_{1},\quad{\partial}_{\theta}W=\sin wE_{2},\quad{\partial}_{\rho}\Psi=({\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1})E_{1}+({\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{2})E_{2}-\varphi_{1}w_{\rho}W,

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{\theta}\Psi=({\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1})E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2})E_{2}+\varphi_{1}\cos wE_{2}-\varphi_{2}(\sin wW+\cos wE_{1}),

then we have

(8.16)

2(\nabla W\cdot\nabla\Psi)W=2\Big({\partial}_{\rho}W\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}\Psi+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}W\cdot{\partial}_{\theta}\Psi\Big)W=\Big(\frac{2\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\varphi_{1}+2w_{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{2\sin w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2}\Big)W.

Then plugging (
2.5
), (
8.15
), (
8.16
) into (
8.2
) and using (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi\right)=

\displaystyle\Big\{{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{1}+\Big[\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\Big]\varphi_{1}-\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{2}\Big\}\left(aE_{1}-bE_{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\Big\{{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\varphi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta\theta}\varphi_{2}+\Big[\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\Big]\varphi_{2}+\frac{2\cos w}{\rho^{2}}{\partial}_{\theta}\varphi_{1}\Big\}\left(aE_{2}+bE_{1}\right),

which implies (
8.9
), (
8.10
) and the equivalence between (
8.1
) and (
8.11
).
Finally, (
8.12
) and (
8.14
) are derived directly.
∎

The linearly independent kernels
\mathcal{Z}_{k,1},\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}
of

\mathcal{L}_{k}
in (
8.13
) satisfying the Wronskian
W[\mathcal{Z}_{k,1},\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}]=\rho^{-1}
are given as follows:

(8.17)

\begin{cases}\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(\rho)=\frac{\rho^{2}}{\rho^{2}+1},\quad\mathcal{Z}_{-1,2}(\rho)=\frac{4\rho^{2}(\rho^{2}\ln(\rho)-1)-1}{4\rho^{2}(\rho^{2}+1)},&k=-1,\\
\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)=\frac{\rho}{\rho^{2}+1},\quad\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(\rho)=\frac{\rho^{4}+4\rho^{2}\ln(\rho)-1}{2\rho(\rho^{2}+1)},&k=0,\\
\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho)=\frac{1}{\rho^{2}+1},\quad\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(\rho)=\frac{\rho^{4}+4\rho^{2}+4\ln(\rho)}{4(\rho^{2}+1)},&k=1,\\
\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)=\frac{\rho^{1-k}}{\rho^{2}+1},\quad\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)=\frac{\rho^{k-1}}{\rho^{2}+1}(\frac{\rho^{4}}{2k+2}+\frac{\rho^{2}}{k}+\frac{1}{2k-2}),&k\neq-1,0,1.\end{cases}

It is straightforward to get

\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\sim\rho^{1-k}\mathbf{1}_{\{0<\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-1-k}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}},\quad\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\sim k^{-1}\big(\rho^{k-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{0<\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{k+1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big)\mbox{ \ for \ }k\neq-1,0,1.

Recall (
2.9
) and (
8.5
) and notice
\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)=-\frac{1}{2}\rho w_{\rho}
. Then
for mode
0
,

(8.18)

\left(h_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{0,1}+i\left(h_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{0,2}=\rho w_{\rho}h_{0}(\rho,\tau)=-2\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)h_{0}(\rho,\tau).

Notice
\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho)=-\frac{1}{2}w_{\rho}
. For mode
1
,

\displaystyle(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{1,1}={\rm Re}(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})w_{\rho}\cos\theta+{\rm Im}(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})w_{\rho}\sin\theta,

\displaystyle(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{1,2}={\rm Re}(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})w_{\rho}\sin\theta-{\rm Im}(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})w_{\rho}\cos\theta,

whose equivalent complex form is given by

(8.19)

(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{1,1}-i(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\cdot Z_{1,2}=w_{\rho}h_{1}(\rho,\tau)=-2\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho)h_{1}(\rho,\tau).

For a radial complex-valued function
f(\rho)
, the quadratic form of
\mathcal{L}_{k}
in
B_{R}
is defined as

(8.20)

Q_{R,k}(f,f)=2\pi\int_{0}^{R}\Big[|{\partial}_{\rho}f|^{2}+\frac{(k+1)^{2}\rho^{4}+(2k^{2}-6)\rho^{2}+(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{|f|^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\Big]\rho d\rho.

By
[
120
, Lemma 4.2]
,
Q_{R,k}(f,f)\geq 0
for all
f\in C^{2}(B_{R})\cap C(\bar{B}_{R})
with
f=0
on
{\partial}B_{R}
, and
Q_{R,k}(f,f)=0
implies
f\equiv 0
. Define the norms

\|f\|_{X(B_{R})}=\Big[2\pi\int_{0}^{R}\Big(|{\partial}_{\rho}f|^{2}+\frac{|f|^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\Big)\rho d\rho\Big]^{1/2},

\|f\|_{H^{1}_{0}(B_{R})}=\Big(2\pi\int_{0}^{R}|{\partial}_{\rho}f|^{2}\rho d\rho\Big)^{1/2},\ \|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}=\Big(2\pi\int_{0}^{R}|f|^{2}\rho d\rho\Big)^{1/2}.

Set
X_{0}(B_{R})=\{f(\rho)\ \big|\ f(R)=0,\quad\|f\|_{X(B_{R})}<\infty\}
.

8.2.
Energy estimates

We use the method in
[
15
, Lemma 7.2]

to analyze the first eigenvalue of
Q_{R,k}
.

Lemma 8.2
.

Let

\lambda_{R,k}=\inf\limits_{f\in X_{0}(B_{R})\backslash\{0\}}\frac{Q_{R,k}(f,f)}{\|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}\mbox{ \ for \ }k\neq 1,\quad\lambda_{R,1}=\inf\limits_{f\in H_{0}^{1}(B_{R})\backslash\{0\}}\frac{Q_{R,1}(f,f)}{\|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}\mbox{ \ for \ }k=1.

\lambda_{R,k}
is attained by a real-valued function in
X_{0}(B_{R})
for
k\neq 1
and
H_{0}^{1}(B_{R})
for
k=1
.
For
R
large,

\lambda_{R,0}\sim(R^{2}\ln R)^{-1},\quad\lambda_{R,1}\sim R^{-4},\quad\lambda_{R,-1}\gtrsim(R^{2}\ln R)^{-1},\quad\lambda_{R,k}\gtrsim|k|^{2}R^{-2}\mbox{ \ for \ }|k|\geq 2.

Proof.

For any complex-valued function
f=f_{1}+if_{2}
with
f_{1}=\mathrm{Re}f
,
f_{2}=\mathrm{Im}f
,

\frac{Q_{R,k}(f,f)}{\|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}=\frac{Q_{R,k}(f_{1},f_{1})+Q_{R,k}(f_{2},f_{2})}{\|f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}+\|f_{2}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}\geq\min\bigg\{\frac{Q_{R,k}(f_{1},f_{1})}{\|f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}},\frac{Q_{R,k}(f_{2},f_{2})}{\|f_{2}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}\bigg\}.

Thus for
k\neq 1
,

\lambda_{R,k}=\inf\bigg\{\frac{Q_{R,k}(f,f)}{\|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}}\ |\ f\in X_{0}(B_{R})\backslash\{0\},\mbox{ $f$ is real-valued}\bigg\}.

The same argument can be applied to
\lambda_{R,1}
.

Hereafter, we focus on real-valued functions. We choose a sequence
f_{n}\in X_{0}(B_{R})
if
k\neq 1
(
f_{n}\in H_{0}^{1}(B_{R})
if
k=1
) with
\|f_{n}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}=1
and
\lambda_{R,k}+1\geq Q_{R,k}(f_{n},f_{n})\rightarrow\lambda_{R,k}
. By the form of
Q_{R,k}
given in (
8.20
), we have

\int_{0}^{R}({\partial}_{\rho}f_{n})^{2}\rho d\rho\lesssim\lambda_{R,k}+1
.
The Sobolev compact embedding theorem implies
f_{n}\rightarrow f_{\infty}
in
L^{2}(B_{R})
up to a subsequence.

For
k\neq 1
,

Q_{R,k}(f_{n},f_{n})=2\pi\int_{0}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}f_{n})^{2}+\frac{(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{f_{n}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{(k+1)^{2}\rho^{2}+(2k^{2}-6)}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}f_{n}^{2}\bigg]\rho d\rho.

Up to a subsequence, we have

\int_{0}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}f_{\infty})^{2}+\frac{(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{f_{\infty}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho\leq\liminf\limits_{n\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}f_{n})^{2}+\frac{(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{f_{n}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho,

\int_{0}^{R}\frac{(k+1)^{2}\rho^{2}+(2k^{2}-6)}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}f_{\infty}^{2}\rho d\rho=\lim\limits_{n\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{R}\frac{(k+1)^{2}\rho^{2}+(2k^{2}-6)}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}f_{n}^{2}\rho d\rho.

Moreover,

\int_{0}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}f_{\infty})^{2}+\frac{(k-1)^{2}}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{f_{\infty}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho\sim C(R,k)\int_{0}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}f_{\infty})^{2}+\frac{f_{\infty}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho.

Thus

Q_{R,k}(f_{\infty},f_{\infty})\leq\lambda_{R,k},\quad\|f_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}=1,\quad f_{\infty}\in X_{0}(B_{R}),

which implies that the minimum
\lambda_{R,k}
is attained by
f_{\infty}
.

For
k=1
,
similarly, we choose a subsequence such that
f_{n}\rightharpoonup f_{\infty}
in
H^{1}_{0}(B_{R})
,
f_{n}\rightarrow f_{\infty}
in
L^{2}(B_{R})
.

\int_{0}^{R}({\partial}_{\rho}f_{\infty})^{2}\rho d\rho\leq\liminf\limits_{n\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{R}({\partial}_{\rho}f_{n})^{2}\rho d\rho,\quad\int_{0}^{R}\frac{4(\rho^{2}-1)}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}f_{\infty}^{2}\rho d\rho=\lim\limits_{n\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{R}\frac{4(\rho^{2}-1)}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}f_{n}^{2}\rho d\rho.

Then

Q_{R,1}(f_{\infty},f_{\infty})\leq\lambda_{R,1},\quad\|f_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}=1,\quad f_{\infty}\in H_{0}^{1}(B_{R}),

and thus
\lambda_{R,1}
is attained by
f_{\infty}
.

Next, we will use the Lagrange multiplier for the real-valued minimum function
f_{\infty}
to estimate
\lambda_{R,k}
,
k=-1,0,1
. To avoid confusion, we denote
w_{k}
as the eigenfunction corresponding to the eigenvalue
\lambda_{R,k}
for every mode
k
with the normalization
\|w_{k}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}=1
.

For
k=0
,

\mathcal{L}_{0}w_{0}=-\lambda_{R,0}w_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R},\quad w_{0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}B_{R}.

w_{0}
is given by

w_{0}(\rho)=\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}(-\lambda_{R,0}w_{0}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(s)sds+\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,0}w_{0}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(s)sds-A_{R,0}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho),

where
A_{R,0}=(\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(R))^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(R)\int_{0}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,0}w_{0}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(s)sds
.
For
0\leq\rho\leq 1
,

|\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(s)sds|\lesssim\rho^{-1}\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}(B_{\rho})}\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{\rho})}\lesssim 1,

|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{R}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(s)sds|\leq|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{1}^{R}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(s)sds|+|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{1}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(s)sds|\lesssim R^{2},

|A_{R,0}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)|\lesssim|A_{R,0}|\lesssim\lambda_{R,0}R^{2}(\ln R)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Thus
\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}\lesssim\lambda_{R,0}R^{2}(\ln R)^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
For
\rho\geq 1
,

\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(s)sds\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\leq\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\lesssim R^{2}(\ln R)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{R}w_{0}(s)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(s)sds\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\lesssim\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\|\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\lesssim R^{2}(\ln R)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|A_{R,0}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\|_{L^{2}(B_{R}\backslash B_{1})}\lesssim\lambda_{R,0}R^{2}\ln R.

In sum, when
R
is large, we have
1=\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\lesssim\lambda_{R,0}R^{2}\ln R
.

On the other hand, when
R
is large,

\|\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}\sim\ln R
,

\displaystyle Q_{R,0}(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1},\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1})\sim\bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{R}{2}}+\int_{\frac{R}{2}}^{R}\bigg)\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}))^{2}+\frac{\rho^{4}-6\rho^{2}+1}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1})^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\frac{R}{2}}^{\infty}\left[({\partial}_{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1})^{2}+\frac{\rho^{4}-6\rho^{2}+1}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{(\mathcal{Z}_{0,1})^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]\rho d\rho+\int_{\frac{R}{2}}^{R}\bigg[({\partial}_{\rho}(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}))^{2}+\frac{\rho^{4}-6\rho^{2}+1}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1})^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg]\rho d\rho\sim R^{-2},

where we used
\mathcal{L}_{0}\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}=0
. Then we have

\lambda_{R,0}\lesssim(R^{2}\ln R)^{-1}
.

For
k=1
, similarly,

\mathcal{L}_{1}w_{1}=-\lambda_{R,1}w_{1}\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R},\quad w_{1}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}B_{R},

w_{1}(\rho)=\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}(-\lambda_{R,1}w_{1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(s)sds+\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,1}w_{1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(s)sds-A_{R,1}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(\rho),

where
A_{R,1}=(\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(R))^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(R)\int_{0}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,1}w_{1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(s)sds
. For
R
large, we have
1=\|w_{1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\lesssim\lambda_{R,1}R^{4}
,
\|\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}\sim 1
,
Q_{R,1}(\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1},\eta_{\frac{R}{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1})\sim R^{-4}
by
\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}=0
, and then
\lambda_{R,1}\lesssim R^{-4}
.

For
k=-1
, similarly,

\mathcal{L}_{-1}w_{-1}=-\lambda_{R,-1}w_{-1}\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R},\quad w_{-1}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}B_{R},

w_{-1}(\rho)=\mathcal{Z}_{-1,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}(-\lambda_{R,-1}w_{-1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(s)sds+\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,-1}w_{-1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{-1,2}(s)sds-A_{R,-1}\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(\rho),

where
A_{R,-1}=(\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(R))^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{-1,2}(R)\int_{0}^{R}(-\lambda_{R,-1}w_{-1}(s))\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(s)sds
. For
R
large, we have
1=\|w_{-1}\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\lesssim\lambda_{R,-1}R^{2}\ln R
.

For
|k|\geq 2
,
Q_{R,k}(f,f)\gtrsim 2\pi|k|^{2}\int_{0}^{R}\frac{|f|^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\rho d\rho\geq|k|^{2}R^{-2}\|f\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}
.
∎

Lemma 8.3
.

Consider

(8.21)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{k}\phi_{k}+h(\rho,\tau)\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\\
\phi_{k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R},\quad\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})}\end{cases}

with
R
given in (
8.4
). Denote
\Phi_{k}(y,\tau)=(\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
. Then (
8.21
) is equivalent to

(8.22)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\Phi_{k}=(a-bW\wedge)(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{k})+(h(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\\
\Phi_{k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R},\quad\Phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

Suppose that
\|h(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}\lesssim g(\tau)
,

(8.23)

\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}e^{c\int^{s}\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}(z)dz}(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}(s))^{-1}g(s)ds\lesssim C(c)e^{c\int^{\tau}\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}(z)dz}\min\left\{\tau,(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}\right\}(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}g(\tau)

for any fixed constant
c>0
, a constant
C(c)>0
depending on
c
and

\tilde{\lambda}_{R,0}=(R^{2}\ln R)^{-1},\quad\tilde{\lambda}_{R,1}=R^{-4},\quad\tilde{\lambda}_{R,-1}=(R^{2}\ln R)^{-1},\quad\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}=R^{-2}\mbox{ \ for \ }|k|\geq 2,

then, we have the estimates

(8.24)

\|\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}\lesssim\big[\min\big\{\tau,(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}\big\}(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}g(\tau)\big]^{1/2}.

In particular, when
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
, then
\|h(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}\lesssim\big(\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}\big)^{2}
, and

(8.25)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{0}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R},\quad\|\phi_{1}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R},

\displaystyle\|\phi_{-1}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R},\quad\|\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}\mbox{ \ for \ }|k|\geq 2,

where

(8.26)

\theta_{R,\ell}:=\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>1\\
(\ln R)^{\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=1\\
R^{1-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<1.\end{cases}

For
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, if
\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k},g(\tau)\in{\mathbf{AP}}
, and
either
Case 1:
{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}]>-1
,
or
Case 2:
{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}]<-1
,
{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}(s))^{-1}g(s)]>-1
holds, then (
8.23
) is true.

Proof.

Lemma
8.1
connects (
8.21
) and (
8.22
). The theory of parabolic systems guarantees the existence and uniqueness of the solution. By continuity argument, it suffices to assume that
h
is smooth. Multiplying
\bar{\phi}_{k}
to (
8.21
) and integrating by parts, we have

\int_{B_{R}}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}\bar{\phi}_{k}+(a-ib)Q_{R,k}(\phi_{k},\phi_{k})=\int_{B_{R}}h\bar{\phi}_{k}.

We take the real part for both parts and use
\phi_{k}=0
on
{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R}
, then

\frac{1}{2}{\partial}_{\tau}\int_{B_{R}}|\phi_{k}|^{2}+aQ_{R,k}(\phi_{k},\phi_{k})=\int_{B_{R}}\mathrm{Re}\left(h\bar{\phi}_{k}\right).

By Lemma
8.2
, we have

{\partial}_{\tau}\int_{B_{R}}|\phi_{k}|^{2}+c\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}\int_{B_{R}}|\phi_{k}|^{2}\leq 2\int_{B_{R}}|h||\phi_{k}|

for a fixed constant
c>0
. By Young’s inequality, we have

{\partial}_{\tau}\int_{B_{R}}|\phi_{k}|^{2}+c\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}\int_{B_{R}}|\phi_{k}|^{2}\lesssim(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}\int_{B_{R}}|h|^{2}\lesssim(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}g(\tau).

Since
\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0
in
B_{R(\tau_{0})}
, by (
8.23
), we have (
8.24
).

When
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
, then
\|h(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}(B_{R})}^{2}\lesssim\big(\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}\big)^{2}
. By (
8.24
),
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
, the application of
W_{p}^{2,1}
estimate given in
[
32
]
twice to (
8.22
), and the Sobolev embedding theorem, we have

\|\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim\big\{\big[\min\big\{\tau,(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}\big\}(\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k})^{-1}\big]^{1/2}\theta_{R,\ell}+1\big\}v(\tau)\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{R}.

Using
R^{2}\ln R\leq C_{1}\tau
in (
8.4
), we have (
8.25
).
∎

Lemma 8.4
.

Given an integer
k\geq 0
, consider

\partial_{\tau}\phi=(a-ib)\Big(\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi-\frac{k^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\phi\Big)+h(\rho,\tau),\quad\phi(\rho,\tau_{0})=g(\rho),

where
h(\rho,\tau)
,
g(\rho)
are some functions with sufficient space-time decay. Then using
\Gamma_{d}^{\natural}
defined in (
2.1
) gives a solution
\phi
of the form

(8.27)

\phi(\rho,\tau)=\rho^{k}\Gamma_{2k+2}^{\natural}**\big(|y|^{-k}h(|y|,s)\big)(\rho,\tau,\tau_{0})+\rho^{k}\Gamma_{2k+2}^{\natural}*\big(|y|^{-k}g(|y|)\big)(\rho,\tau,\tau_{0}).

Proof.

Set
\phi(\rho,\tau)=\rho^{k}\psi(\rho,\tau)
.
Then

(8.28)

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi=(a-ib)\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\psi+\frac{2k+1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi\Big)+\rho^{-k}h(\rho,\tau),\quad\psi(\rho,\tau_{0})=\rho^{-k}g(\rho),

which can be regarded as the heat equation in
{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2k+2}
. Then
\psi
is given by

\psi(\rho,\tau)=\Gamma_{2k+2}^{\natural}**\big(|y|^{-k}h(|y|,s)\big)(\rho,\tau,\tau_{0})+\Gamma_{2k+2}^{\natural}*\big(|y|^{-k}g(|y|)\big)(\rho,\tau,\tau_{0}),

which satisfies (
8.28
) in weak sense and pointwise sense except at
\rho=0
. (
8.27
) follows.
∎

8.3.
Mode
k
,
|k|\geq 2

In order to analyze the case that the right-hand side of the equation has singularity at
y=0
, given
\mathcal{R}=\mathcal{R}(\tau)
, we introduce the norm

(8.29)

\|h\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}:=\sup\limits_{(y,\tau)\in{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathcal{R}}}v(\tau)^{-1}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 1\}}|y|^{\ell_{1}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|>1\}}|y|^{\ell}\big)|h(y,\tau)|.

We use the notation
\|h\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{\infty}
if
\mathcal{R}(\tau)=\infty
. Obviously,

(8.30)

\|h\|_{v,0,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\sim\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}};\quad\|h\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\lesssim\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell_{1}>0;\quad\|h\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\gtrsim\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\mathcal{R}}\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell_{1}<0.

Lemma 8.5
.

Consider

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{k}\right)+H_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},

where
H_{k}=\big(h_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{k}\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{R}<\infty
. Suppose (
8.4
),
\ell_{1}\in[0,1.9]
,
\ell\in(1,3)
,
\frac{3}{2}+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)R^{4-\ell}]>0
, then
there exists a solution
\Psi_{k}={\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{R}[H_{k}]
as a mapping linear in
H_{k}
with the estimate

(8.31)

|\Psi_{k}|\lesssim|k|^{-1-(0.05)^{2}}v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\ln(|y|+2)\|H_{k}\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{R}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},

where “
\lesssim
” is independent of
k
.
Moreover,
\Psi_{k}\cdot W=0
and
e^{-ik\theta}\left(\Psi_{k}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Proof.

For brevity, denote
\|h_{k}\|=\|h_{k}\|_{v,\ell_{1},\ell}^{R}
in this proof.
Assume
h_{k}(\rho,\tau)=0
in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R}^{c}
.
Consider

\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}G_{k}\right)=H_{k},\mbox{ \ where \ }G_{k}=\big(g_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}.

By Lemma
8.1
, it is equivalent to considering

\left(a-ib\right)\mathcal{L}_{k}g_{k}=h_{k},

where
g_{k}
is given by

(8.32)

g_{k}(\rho,\tau)=(a+ib)\begin{cases}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr+\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr&\mbox{ \ if \ }k\leq-2\\
-\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr-\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr&\mbox{ \ if \ }k\geq 2.\end{cases}

We will estimate the upper bound of
g_{k}
.
For
k\leq-2
,
\rho\leq 1
,

(8.33)

\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k-1}\int_{0}^{\rho}r^{2-k-\ell_{1}}dr\sim|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell_{1}}

for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 4
.

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim\rho^{1-k}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)|k|^{-1}\Big(\int_{\rho}^{1}r^{k-\ell_{1}}dr+\int_{1}^{\infty}r^{k+2-\ell}dr\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\rho^{1-k}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\Big[\frac{\rho^{k-\ell_{1}+1}}{\ell_{1}-(k+1)}+\frac{1}{\ell-(k+3)}\Big]\lesssim C_{1}(\ell)|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell_{1}}

for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 4
and
1<\ell\leq 5
, where
C_{1}(\ell)\rightarrow\infty
as
\ell\rightarrow 1
due to
k=-2
.

For
k\leq-2
,
\rho\geq 1
,

(8.34)

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k+1}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}r^{2-k-\ell_{1}}dr+\int_{1}^{\rho}r^{-\ell-k}dr\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k+1}\Big(\frac{1}{3-k-\ell_{1}}+\frac{\rho^{1-k-\ell}}{1-k-\ell}\Big)\lesssim C_{2}(\ell)|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell}

for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 4
and
0\leq\ell<3
, where
C_{2}(\ell)\rightarrow\infty
as
\ell\rightarrow 3
due to
k=-2
.

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim\rho^{-1-k}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}|k|^{-1}r^{k+2-\ell}dr\lesssim C_{3}(\ell)|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell}

for
1<\ell\leq 4
, where
C_{3}(\ell)\rightarrow\infty
as
\ell\rightarrow 1
due to
k=-2
.

In sum, for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 4
,
1<l<3
,
k\leq-2
,

(8.35)

\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell_{1}-2,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim C_{4}(\ell)|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|,

where
C_{4}(\ell)\rightarrow\infty
as
\ell\rightarrow 1
or
3
.

For
k\geq 2
,
\rho\leq 1
,
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 3
,
0\leq\ell\leq 4
,

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim k^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k-1}\Big(\int_{1}^{\infty}r^{-k-\ell}dr+\int_{\rho}^{1}r^{2-k-\ell_{1}}dr\Big)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle k^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k-1}\Big(\frac{1}{k+\ell-1}+\frac{1-\rho^{3-k-\ell_{1}}}{3-k-\ell_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\ell_{1}\neq 3-k\}}+(-\ln\rho)\mathbf{1}_{\{\ell_{1}=3-k\}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\begin{cases}k^{-1}\rho^{k-1}\big(k^{-1}+\frac{\rho^{3-k-\ell_{1}}}{k+\ell_{1}-3}\big)\sim k^{-2}\rho^{2-\ell_{1}},&k\geq 4\\
\rho^{2}\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&k=3,~\ell_{1}=0\\
\rho^{2}\big(1+\frac{\rho^{-\ell_{1}}-1}{\ell_{1}}\big)\lesssim\rho^{2-\ell_{1}}\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&k=3,~0<\ell_{1}\leq 3\\
\rho\big(1+\frac{1-\rho^{1-\ell_{1}}}{1-\ell_{1}}\big)\lesssim\rho\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&k=2,~0\leq\ell_{1}<1\\
\rho\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&k=2,~\ell_{1}=1\\
\rho\big(1+\frac{\rho^{1-\ell_{1}}-1}{\ell_{1}-1}\big)\lesssim\rho^{2-\ell_{1}}\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&k=2,~1<\ell_{1}\leq 3,\end{cases}

where we used
\rho^{t}-1=t\rho^{ct}\ln\rho
for some
c\in[0,1]
.

For the other part,

\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{1-k}\int_{0}^{\rho}k^{-1}r^{k-\ell_{1}}dr\sim k^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell_{1}}

for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 2.9
.
For
k\geq 2
,
\rho\geq 1
,

\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim k^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{k+1}\int_{\rho}^{\infty}r^{-k-\ell}dr\sim k^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell},

when
0\leq\ell\leq 4
.

\Big|\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\Big|\lesssim k^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-1-k}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}r^{k-\ell_{1}}dr+\int_{1}^{\rho}r^{k+2-\ell}dr\Big)\lesssim k^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell},

when
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 2.9
,
0\leq\ell\leq 4
.

In sum, for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 2.9
,
0\leq\ell\leq 4
,

(8.36)

\begin{cases}\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell_{1}-2,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim k^{-2}\|h_{k}\|,&k\geq 4\\
\|g_{3}\|_{v,\epsilon+\ell_{1}-2,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim C(\epsilon)\|h_{k}\|,&k=3\\
\|g_{2}\|_{v,\epsilon+(\ell_{1}-1)_{+}-1,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim C(\epsilon)\|h_{k}\|,&k=2,\end{cases}

where
\epsilon>0
could be an arbitrarily small constant, and
C(\epsilon)
is a constant depending on
\epsilon
.

Combining (
8.35
), (
8.36
), (
8.30
), for
0\leq\ell_{1}\leq 1.9
,
1<\ell<3
, we have

(8.37)

\|G_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}=\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|\mbox{ \ for \ }|k|\geq 2,

where
C(\ell)\rightarrow\infty
as
\ell\rightarrow 1
or
3
and
C(\ell)
will vary from line to line.
Consider

(8.38)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\Phi_{k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{k}\right)+G_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\\
\Phi_{k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\quad\Phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

To find a solution
\Phi_{k}
with the form
\Phi_{k}=\left(\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, by Lemma
8.1
, it suffices to consider

(8.39)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}=\left(a-ib\right)\mathcal{L}_{k}\phi_{k}+g_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\\
\phi_{k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\quad\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

The existence and uniqueness follow by the theory of parabolic systems.

Using
{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[R]<1/2
in (
8.4
), we have
{\mathbf{P}}_{1}[\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}]>-1
with
\tilde{\lambda}_{R,k}=R^{-2}
,
|k|\geq 2
. Then (
8.23
) is true.
Thus, applying Lemma
8.3
to (
8.39
), we have

(8.40)

\|\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{2R(\tau)})}\lesssim v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}.

To improve the spatial decay of
\phi_{k}
, we reformulate the equation (
8.39
) into the following form

(8.41)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}=(a-ib)\big[{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\phi_{k}+\frac{{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{k}}{\rho}-\frac{(k+1)^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\phi_{k}\big]+\tilde{g}_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{2R},\\
\phi_{k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{2R},\quad\phi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where
\tilde{g}_{k}(\rho,\tau):=(a-ib)\frac{(4k+8)\rho^{2}+4k}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\phi_{k}+g_{k}
.
Set
\phi_{*k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)
. Then (
8.41
) is equivalent to

(8.42)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{*k}=(a-ib)\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\phi_{*k}+e^{i(k+1)\theta}\tilde{g}_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{2R},\\
\phi_{*k}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{2R},\quad\phi_{*k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

Complex-valued equation (
8.42
) can be regarded as a real-valued parabolic system in a varying-time domain in
\mathbb{R}^{2+1}
. Combining
[
33
, Theorem 3.2]
and
[
100
, Lemma 2.26 and Remark 2.27]
,
there exists a fundamental solution
\Gamma_{2}(x,y,\tau,s)
for the homogeneous part of (
8.42
) with the estimate

|\Gamma_{2}(x,y,\tau,s)|\leq C\left(\tau-s\right)^{-1}e^{-\frac{\kappa|x-y|^{2}}{\tau-s}}

for some constants
C,\kappa>0
. Then, by scaling argument, we have

(8.43)

\left|\nabla_{y}\Gamma_{2}(x,y,\tau,s)\right|\lesssim\left(\tau-s\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{\kappa_{1}|x-y|^{2}}{\tau-s}}

for a constant
\kappa_{1}>0
.
Then
\phi_{*k}
can be written as

(8.44)

\phi_{*k}(y,\tau)=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{B_{2R(s)}}\Gamma_{2}(y,z,\tau,s)e^{i(k+1)\theta(z)}\tilde{g}_{k}(|z|,s)dzds,

where
\theta(z)=\arctan(z_{2}/z_{1})
.

For utilizing the special form of
e^{i(k+1)\theta}\tilde{g}_{k}
, we set
\tilde{g}_{k}=0
in
\mathcal{D}_{2R}^{c}
and want to find
\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)
satisfying

(8.45)

\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\tilde{g}_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2}.

Set
\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\tilde{p}_{k}(\rho,\tau)
. Then

{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\tilde{p}_{k}+\rho^{-1}{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k}-(k+1)^{2}\rho^{-2}\tilde{p}_{k}=\tilde{g}_{k}.

Set
\tilde{p}_{k}=\rho^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}(\rho,\tau)
. It is equivalent to

{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}+\left(2|k+1|+1\right)\rho^{-1}{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}=\rho^{-|k+1|}\tilde{g}_{k}.

We take
\tilde{p}_{k,1}
as

\tilde{p}_{k,1}(\rho,\tau)=-\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}rr^{-|k+1|}\tilde{g}_{k}(r,\tau)drdu.

Notice

|\tilde{g}_{k}|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 2R(\tau)\}}\big[|k|\big(\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-4}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big)|\phi_{k}|+v(\tau)\langle\rho\rangle^{2-\ell}\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\big].

Then

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{p}_{k,1}\right|\lesssim\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}r\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 2R(\tau)\}}

\displaystyle\times\Big[|k|\Big(r^{-2-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 1\}}+r^{-4-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}\Big)|\phi_{k}|+v(\tau)\Big(r^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 1\}}+r^{2-\ell-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}\Big)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\Big]drdu.

Here, by Lemma
A.3
, one has

\displaystyle\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 2R(\tau)\}}rv(\tau)\Big(r^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 1\}}+r^{2-\ell-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}\Big)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}drdu

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(\ell)v(\tau)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\begin{cases}|k|^{-2}\left(\rho^{2-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{4-\ell-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\right)&\mbox{ \ for \ }k\leq-4\mbox{ or }k\geq 2\\
R(\tau)\left(\langle\ln\rho\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{1-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\right)&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-3\\
R^{2}(\tau)\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{1-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\right)&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-2,\end{cases}

where we used

\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 2R(\tau)\}}r^{2-\ell-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}\lesssim\begin{cases}R(\tau)r^{-1-\ell}&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-3\\
R^{2}(\tau)r^{-1-\ell}&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-2\end{cases}
.
By (
8.40
) and Lemma
A.3
,

\displaystyle\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}r\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 2R(\tau)\}}|k|\left(r^{-2-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 1\}}+r^{-4-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}\right)|\phi_{k}|drdu

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(\ell)v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}(\tau)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\begin{cases}|k|^{-1}\left(\rho^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-2-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\right)&\mbox{ \ for \ }k\leq-4\mbox{ or }k\geq 2\\
\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-4}\langle\ln\rho\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-3\\
\rho^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}&\mbox{ \ for \ }k=-2.\end{cases}

|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}|
can be bounded by (
A.3
) in Lemma
A.3
similarly. As a result, for
\rho\leq 2R(\tau)
,

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\rho|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}|+|\tilde{p}_{k,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(\ell)v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}(\tau)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\begin{cases}|k|^{-1}\big(\rho^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-1-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big),&k\leq-4\mbox{ or }k\geq 2\\
\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}},&k=-3\\
\rho^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}},&k=-2.\end{cases}

Notice
\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\rho^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}(\rho,\tau)
. Then

(8.46)

\displaystyle\big|\nabla\tilde{P}_{k}\big|=\Big(\big|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{P}_{k}\big|^{2}+\rho^{-2}\big|{\partial}_{\theta}\tilde{P}_{k}\big|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}=\Big(\big||k+1|\rho^{|k+1|-1}\tilde{p}_{k,1}+\rho^{|k+1|}{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}\big|^{2}+\rho^{-2}|k+1|^{2}\big|\rho^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}\big|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k+1|\rho^{|k+1|-1}\big(|\tilde{p}_{k,1}|+|k+1|^{-1}\rho|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{p}_{k,1}|\big)\lesssim C(\ell)v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}(\tau)\big(\rho^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}.

By
\phi_{*k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)
, (
8.44
) and (
8.45
), we have

\phi_{k}(y,\tau)=-e^{-i(k+1)\theta}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{B_{2R(s)}}\nabla_{z}\Gamma_{2}(y,z,\tau,s)\cdot\nabla\tilde{P}_{k}(z,s)dzds.

By (
8.43
) and (
8.46
), then

\left|\phi_{k}\right|\lesssim C(\ell)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{B_{2R(s)}}\left(\tau-s\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{\kappa_{1}|y-z|^{2}}{\tau-s}}v(s)R^{5-\ell}(s)\big(|z|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 1\}}+|z|^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|>1\}}\big)|z|dzds.

By similar estimates as
[
132
, Lemma A.1]
and Lemma
A.1
in
\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
provided
\frac{3}{2}+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(t)R^{4-\ell}]>0
, we have

(8.47)

\left|\phi_{k}\right|\lesssim C(\ell)v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}|y|^{-1}\ln(|y|+2)\|g_{k}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\mbox{ \ for \ }1\leq|y|<2R.

Combining (
8.40
), (
8.47
), and (
8.37
), we have

(8.48)

|\Phi_{k}|=|\phi_{k}|\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-2}v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\ln(|y|+2)\|h_{k}\|\mbox{ \ for \ }|y|<2R.

To get the pointwise estimate of
|D^{2}\Phi_{k}|
, we need to calculate the
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}
semi-norm of
G_{k}=\big(g_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
. Recall (
2.6
) and (
8.32
). We consider the following typical term in
G_{k}
.

(8.49)

f_{1}(y,\tau)=f_{11}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\cos(\theta),\ f_{11}(\rho,\tau):=\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\frac{\rho^{2}-1}{\rho^{2}+1}\mbox{ \ for \ }k\leq-2.

We assume
(x,t)\in Q_{2}^{-}(0,\tau)
,
r\in(0,1)
, and arbitrary points
(y,s),(z,s)\in Q_{r}^{-}(x,t)
. Obviously,
s\sim\tau
. If
r\geq|x|/2
, then
|y|,|z|\leq|x|+r\leq 3r
. By (
8.33
), (
8.34
), and
2-\ell_{1}>0
, we have

|f_{1}(y,s)-f_{1}(z,s)|\lesssim|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)r^{2-\ell_{1}}.

It is direct to see that

\displaystyle\partial_{\rho}(f_{11}(\rho,\tau))=\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\frac{4\rho}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}_{k,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(r)h_{k}(r,\tau)rdr\frac{\rho^{2}-1}{\rho^{2}+1}+\mathcal{Z}_{k,2}(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{k,1}(\rho)h_{k}(\rho,\tau)\rho\frac{\rho^{2}-1}{\rho^{2}+1}.

By (
8.33
), (
8.34
), and (
8.17
), we have

(8.50)

\big|\partial_{\rho}f_{11}(\rho,\tau)\big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\big(\rho^{1-\ell_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{0<\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{1-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big).

Note that

\displaystyle e^{ik\theta(y)}f(\theta(y))-e^{ik\theta(z)}f(\theta(z))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{ik\theta(s_{1}y+(1-s_{1})z)}(\nabla\theta)(s_{1}y+(1-s_{1})z)\cdot(y-z)\big[ikf\big(\theta(s_{1}y+(1-s_{1})z)\big)+f^{\prime}\big(\theta(s_{1}y+(1-s_{1})z)\big)\big]

for some
s_{1}\in[0,1]
.
When
|f(\theta_{1})|\leq C_{1},|f^{\prime}(\theta_{1})|\leq C_{1}
with a constant
C_{1}
for all
\theta_{1}\in[-\pi/2,\pi/2]
, then

(8.51)

\big|e^{ik\theta(y)}f(\theta(y))-e^{ik\theta(z)}f(\theta(z))\big|\leq\frac{C_{1}(|k|+1)}{|s_{1}y+(1-s_{1})z|}|y-z|.

If
r<|x|/2
, then
|x|/2\leq|y|,~|z|\leq 3|x|/2
. It follows that

\displaystyle|f_{1}(y,s)-f_{1}(z,s)|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\big|\big(f_{11}(|y|,s)-f_{11}(|z|,s)\big)e^{ik\theta(y)}\cos(\theta(y))+f_{11}(|z|,s)\big(e^{ik\theta(y)}\cos(\theta(y))-e^{ik\theta(z)}\cos(\theta(z))\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)|x|^{1-\ell_{1}}\big||y|-|z|\big|+|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)|x|^{2-\ell_{1}}(|k|+1)|x|^{-1}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)|x|^{1-\ell_{1}}r=|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)|x|^{1.9-\ell_{1}}|x|^{-0.9}r\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)r^{0.1},

where we used
1.9-\ell_{1}\geq 0
,
|x|\lesssim 1
, and
r<|x|/2
for the last step.

In sum,
|\omega|_{f_{1}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q_{2}^{-}(0,\tau))\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)r^{0.1}
. The other terms in
G_{k}(y,\tau)
could be handled similarly and we deduce

(8.52)

|\omega|_{G_{k}}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q_{2}^{-}(0,\tau))\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)r^{0.05}.

We will not use
[G_{k}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0,\tau))}\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)
to deduce the pointwise estimate of
|D^{2}\Phi_{k}|
directly since
\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}|k|^{-1}
is divergent.

For any
(z,s)\in Q_{r}^{-}(x,t)
, we have
(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\in Q_{r\rho_{*}}^{-}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}x,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}t)
. Thus,

\displaystyle\fint_{Q_{r}^{-}(x,t)}\fint_{B_{r}(x)}\big|f(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)-f(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\big|dzdyds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\fint_{Q_{r\rho_{*}}^{-}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}x,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}t)}\fint_{B_{r\rho_{*}}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}x)}\big|f(y_{1},s_{1})-f(z_{1},s_{1})\big|dz_{1}dy_{1}ds_{1}.

Then, for
t_{*}>\tau_{0}
,
|x_{*}|\leq 1
,
\rho_{*}\in(0,1/100]
, by (
8.52
), we have

|\omega|_{G_{k}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)}^{\mathsf{x}}(r,Q_{2}^{-}(0))\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(t_{*})\rho_{*}^{0.05}r^{0.05}.

Applying Proposition
6.2
to (
8.38
) with
\rho_{*}=1/(100|k|^{0.05})
, we get

(8.53)

|D^{2}\Phi_{k}(x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-1-(0.05)^{2}}v(t_{*})R^{5-\ell}(t_{*})\|h_{k}\|\mbox{ \ for \ }t_{*}>\tau_{0},\ |x_{*}|\leq 1.

Given
t_{*}>\tau_{0}
,
1\leq|x_{*}|\leq 3R(t_{*})/2
,
\rho_{*}\leq|x_{*}|/100
, for any
(x,t)\in Q_{2}^{-}(0)
,
r\in(0,1)
, and
(y,s),(z,s)\in Q_{r}^{-}(x,t)
, we have
|x_{*}+\rho_{*}y|\sim|x_{*}+\rho_{*}z|\sim|x_{*}|
,
t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s\sim t_{*}
. By (
8.48
), (
8.37
), then

\displaystyle\rho_{*}^{-2}\|\Phi_{k}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim C(\ell)\rho_{*}^{-2}|k|^{-2}v(t_{*})R^{5-\ell}(t_{*})\langle x_{*}\rangle^{-1}\ln(|x_{*}|+2)\|h_{k}\|,

\displaystyle\|G_{k}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-2}v(t_{*})\langle x_{*}\rangle^{2-\ell}\|h_{k}\|.

To estimate
[G_{k}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}
, we still use the representative term
f_{1}
in (
8.49
) to show the general process of analysis. By (
8.50
), and (
8.34
), (
8.51
), we have

\displaystyle\big|f_{1}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)-f_{1}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big|\big[f_{11}(|x_{*}+\rho_{*}y|,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)-f_{11}(|x_{*}+\rho_{*}z|,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\big]e^{ik\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y)}\cos(\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y))

\displaystyle+f_{11}(|x_{*}+\rho_{*}z|,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)\big[e^{ik\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y)}\cos(\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}y))-e^{ik\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z)}\cos(\theta(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z))\big]\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(t_{*})|x_{*}|^{1-\ell}\rho_{*}r.

It follows that
[f_{1}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(t_{*})|x_{*}|^{1-\ell}\rho_{*}
. The other terms in
G_{k}
can be handled similarly. Then

[G_{k}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim|k|^{-1}\|h_{k}\|v(t_{*})|x_{*}|^{1-\ell}\rho_{*}.

Applying Proposition
6.2
to (
8.38
) with
\rho_{*}=|x_{*}|/(100|k|^{\frac{1}{3}})
, we have

(8.54)

|(D^{2}\Phi_{k})(x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-\frac{4}{3}}v(t_{*})R^{5-\ell}(t_{*})\langle x_{*}\rangle^{-3}\ln(|x_{*}|+2)\|h_{k}\|\mbox{ \ for \ }t_{*}>\tau_{0},\ 1\leq|x_{*}|\leq 3R(t_{*})/2.

Combining (
8.48
), (
8.53
), (
8.54
), and the interpolation inequality, we have

(8.55)

\langle y\rangle^{2}|D^{2}\Phi_{k}|+\langle y\rangle|D\Phi_{k}|+|\Phi_{k}|\lesssim C(\ell)|k|^{-1-(0.05)^{2}}v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\ln(|y|+2)\|h_{k}\|\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{3R/2}.

We take
\Psi_{k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{k}\right)
, which is the exact strong solution we look for. (
8.55
) deduces (
8.31
). Recalling
\Phi_{k}=\left(\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
and applying Lemma
8.1
, we have
\left(\Psi_{k}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}=e^{ik\theta}(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{k}\phi_{k}(\rho,\tau)
.
∎

Since the
\Psi_{k}
given in Lemma
8.5
loses some power of
R
when
|y|
is small, we will construct
\Psi_{k}
with a better estimate by another gluing procedure.

Proposition 8.1
.

Consider

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{k}\right)+H_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},

where
H_{k}=\big(h_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
. Suppose (
8.4
),
\ell\in(1,3)
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>-1
, then there exists a solution
\Psi_{k}={\mathcal{T}}_{k}^{R}[H_{k}]
as a mapping linear in
H_{k}
with the estimate

|\Psi_{k}|\lesssim|k|^{-1-(0.05)^{2}}v(\tau)\|H_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}\begin{cases}\langle y\rangle^{2-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell\in(1,2)\cup(2,3)\\
\ln(|y|+2)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\end{cases}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},

where “
\lesssim
” is independent of
k
. Moreover,
\Psi_{k}\cdot W=0
and
e^{-ik\theta}\left(\Psi_{k}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Remark 8.1
.

The restriction
\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>-1
is not optimal. To improve the lower bound of
\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]
, we need to modify Lemma
A.3
to catch the property of
\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 4R\}}
.

Proof.

Denote
\|h_{k}\|=\|h_{k}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}
and
take
h_{k}=0
in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R}^{c}
. In order to find a solution
\Psi_{k}
with the form
\Psi_{k}=\big(\psi_{k}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
by Lemma
8.1
, it is equivalent to considering

(8.56)

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{k}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{k}\psi_{k}+h_{k}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\quad\psi_{k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})}.

Set
\psi_{k}=\eta_{R_{0}}(\rho)\psi_{i,k}(\rho,\tau)+\psi_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)
, where
\eta_{R_{0}}(\rho)=\eta(\rho/R_{0})
and
R_{0}
is a large constant independent of
\tau_{0}
,
k
. To find a solution for (
8.56
), it suffices to consider the following inner-outer system

(8.57)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{o,k}=(a-ib)\Big[{\partial}_{\rho\rho}\psi_{o,k}+\frac{{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{o,k}}{\rho}-\frac{(k+1)^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\psi_{o,k}\Big]+J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2}\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\\
&\psi_{o,k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(8.58)

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{i,k}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{k}\psi_{i,k}+K[\psi_{o,k}]\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}},\quad\psi_{i,k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})},

where we denote

\displaystyle J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}]=(a-ib)\left(1-\eta_{R_{0}}\right)\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\psi_{o,k}+A_{0}[\psi_{i,k}]+(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{k},\quad\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)=\frac{(4k+8)\rho^{2}+4k}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}\rho^{2}},

\displaystyle K[\psi_{o,k}]=(a-ib)\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\psi_{o,k}+h_{k},\quad A_{0}[\psi_{i,k}]=(a-ib)\Big[\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}\Big)\psi_{i,k}+2{\partial}_{\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{i,k}\Big].

Set
\Psi_{i,k}(y,\tau)=(\psi_{i,k}e^{ik\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, that is,
\psi_{i,k}=e^{-ik\theta}(\Psi_{i,k}\cdot E_{1}+i\Psi_{i,k}\cdot E_{2})
. By Lemma
8.1
, (
8.58
) is equivalent to

(8.59)

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{i,k}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{i,k}+\big(K[\psi_{o,k}]e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}},\quad\Psi_{i,k}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})}.

The linear theories of (
8.57
) and (
8.59
) are given by Lemma
8.4
and Lemma
8.5
, respectively. We reformulate (
8.57
) and (
8.59
) into the following form

(8.60)

\displaystyle\psi_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)=

\displaystyle\rho^{|k+1|}\Big[\Gamma_{2|k+1|+2}^{\natural}**\Big(|z|^{-|k+1|}J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\Big)\Big](\rho,\tau,\tau_{0}),

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,k}(y,\tau)=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\big[\big(K[\psi_{o,k}]e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\big].

We will solve
(\psi_{o,k},\Psi_{i,k})
for (
8.60
) by the contraction mapping theorem.
Since
\big|\big(h_{k}e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\big|\leq v(\tau)\langle y\rangle^{-\ell}\|h_{k}\|
, provided
\ell\in(1,3)
,
\frac{3}{2}+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>0
, by Lemma
8.5
and the scaling argument,

\big|{\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\big[\big(h_{k}e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\big]\big|\leq D_{i}w_{i,k,1}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{k}\|,\quad\big|\nabla{\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\big[\big(h_{k}e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\big]\big|\leq D_{i}w_{i,k,2}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{k}\|,

where
D_{i}\geq 1
is a large constant independent of
k
,

w_{i,k,1}(\rho,\tau):=|k|^{-1-(0.05)^{2}}v(\tau)R_{0}^{5-\ell}\ln R_{0}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3},\quad w_{i,k,2}(\rho,\tau):=v(\tau)R_{0}^{5-\ell}\ln R_{0}\big(\rho^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-4}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big).

Here the weight
\rho^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}
is due to the forthcoming estimate (
8.69
). Denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{i,k}:=\

\displaystyle\Big\{F(y,\tau)\in C^{1}\big(B_{2R_{0}}\backslash\{0\},\mathbb{R}^{3}\big)\ \big|\ F(y,\tau)=\big(f(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}\big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ for some radial scalar function}

(8.61)

\displaystyle~f(\rho,\tau)\mbox{ \ and \ }|F(y,\tau)|\leq 2D_{i}w_{i,k}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{k}\|,\ |\nabla F(y,\tau)|\leq 2D_{i}w_{i,k,2}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{k}\|\Big\}.

For any
\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\in\mathcal{B}_{i,k}
, denote
\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}=e^{-ik\theta}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\cdot E_{1}+i\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\cdot E_{2})
. We will find a solution
\psi_{o,k}=\psi_{o,k}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]
of (
8.57
) by the contraction mapping theorem. Let us estimate
J[\psi_{o,k},\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]
term by term. By (
2.8
),

(8.62)
