|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}|=\big|e^{-ik\theta}\big(\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}E_{1}+E_{1}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}+i\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}E_{2}+iE_{2}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\big)\big|\lesssim|\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}|\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}|.

Since
\tilde{\Psi}_{i,k}\in\mathcal{B}_{i,k}
, for
\tilde{\ell}<\ell
, we have

\displaystyle|A_{0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]|+|(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{k}|\lesssim D_{i}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 2R_{0}\}}v(\tau)R_{0}^{-\ell}\ln R_{0}\|h_{k}\|+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\geq R_{0}\}}v(\tau)\rho^{-\ell}\|h_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle D_{i}\ln R_{0}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\geq R_{0}\}}\lesssim D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\geq R_{0}\}},

(8.63)

\displaystyle\rho^{-|k+1|}\big(\big|A_{0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]\big|+\big|(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{k}\big|\big)\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}\lesssim D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-|k+1|-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 4R\}}.

For
k=-3,-2
, provided
\frac{3-\tilde{\ell}}{2}+1+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>0
,
1<\tilde{\ell}<\min\{\ell,3\}
, by Lemma
A.2
, we have

\displaystyle\rho^{|k+1|}\big|\Gamma_{2|k+1|+2}^{\natural}\big|**\Big\{|z|^{-|k+1|}\Big[\big|A_{0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]\big|+\big|(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{k}\big|\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\big(\rho^{|k+1|}R_{0}^{2-|k+1|-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq R_{0}\}}+\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>R_{0}\}}\big)

(8.64)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\big(\rho\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\big).

The spatial decay rate near
\rho=0
is restricted by the case
k=-2
.

For
|k+1|
large, since the direct pointwise estimate of
\big|\Gamma_{2|k+1|+2}^{\natural}\big|**[\cdot]
will lead to an upper bound with a multiplicity of a constant with exponential growth in
|k+1|
(with the form
a^{-|k+1|}
), which is a disaster for the convergence of summation. Instead, we search for another strategy. Set
\psi_{o,k}^{*}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\psi_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)
. Then

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{o,k}^{*}=(a-ib)\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\psi_{o,k}^{*}+e^{i(k+1)\theta}J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2}\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\quad\psi_{o,k}^{*}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2},

and
\psi_{o,k}^{*}
is given by

\psi_{o,k}^{*}(y,\tau)=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Gamma_{2}^{\natural}(y-z,\tau-s)e^{i(k+1)\theta(z)}J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}](|z|,s)\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}dzds.

Similar to (
8.45
), we will find
\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)
satisfying

\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}](|y|,\tau)\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 4R(\tau)\}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2}.

Set
\tilde{P}_{k}(y,\tau)=e^{i(k+1)\theta}\rho^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}(\rho,\tau)
, where we take
\tilde{p}_{k,1}
as

\tilde{p}_{k,1}(\rho,\tau)=-\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}rr^{-|k+1|}J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}](r,\tau)\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 4R(\tau)\}}drdu.

Then

\psi_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)
in (
8.60
) can be rewritten as
\psi_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)=e^{-i(k+1)\theta}\psi_{o,k}^{*}(y,\tau)
with

\displaystyle\psi_{o,k}^{*}(y,\tau)=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Gamma_{2}^{\natural}(y-z,\tau-s)\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{P}_{k}(z,s)dzds=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{2},z}\Gamma_{2}^{\natural}(y-z,\tau-s)\tilde{P}_{k}(z,s)dzds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(a-ib)^{-1}[4\pi(\tau-s)]^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{|y-z|^{2}}{4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}}\frac{|y-z|^{2}-4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}{4(a-ib)^{2}(\tau-s)^{2}}e^{i(k+1)\theta(z)}|z|^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}(|z|,s)dzds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(a-ib)^{-1}[4\pi(\tau-s)]^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Big[e^{-\frac{|y-z|^{2}}{4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}}\frac{|y-z|^{2}-4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}{4(a-ib)^{2}(\tau-s)^{2}}

\displaystyle-e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}}\frac{|z|^{2}-4(a-ib)(\tau-s)}{4(a-ib)^{2}(\tau-s)^{2}}\Big]e^{i(k+1)\theta(z)}|z|^{|k+1|}\tilde{p}_{k,1}(|z|,s)dzds,

where we used
\int_{0}^{2\pi}e^{i(k+1)\theta}d\theta=0
for the last equality. By the last two equalities above, we have

(8.65)

|\psi_{o,k}|\lesssim\min\big\{F_{1}[|\tilde{p}_{k,1}(|y|,\tau)|],~F_{2}[|\tilde{p}_{k,1}(|y|,\tau)|]\big\},

where for any
f(y,\tau)
, we denote

\displaystyle F_{1}[f]=F_{1}[f](y,\tau):=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{a|y-z|^{2}}{8(\tau-s)}}|z|^{|k+1|}f(z,s)dzds,

\displaystyle F_{2}[f]=F_{2}[f](y,\tau):=|y|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-5/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{a|uy-z|^{2}}{8(\tau-s)}}|z|^{|k+1|}f(z,s)dzdsdu.

By (
8.63
), we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{p}_{k,1}|\lesssim M_{1}+M_{2},\mbox{ \ where \ }M_{1}:=\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}rr^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq r\leq 4R(\tau)\}}|\tilde{V}_{k}(r)\psi_{o,k}(r,\tau)|drdu,

\displaystyle M_{2}:=\rho^{-2|k+1|}\int_{0}^{\rho}u^{2|k+1|-1}\int_{u}^{\infty}r\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq r\leq 4R(\tau)\}}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)r^{-|k+1|-\tilde{\ell}}drdu.

For
k\leq-4
or
k\geq 2
, by Lemma
A.3
(with
f(x)=|x|^{-|k+1|}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 1\}}+|x|^{-|k+1|-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>1\}}
), and
\tilde{\ell}\in(1,3)
, we have

\rho^{|k+1|}M_{2}\lesssim D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\||k|^{-2}v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}}\big).

For
\tilde{\ell}\in(2,3)
, using
\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}}=\rho^{2}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-\tilde{\ell}}\big)
, provided
1+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>0
, by similar convolution estimates in
\mathbb{R}^{4}
as Lemma
A.2
and
[
132
, Lemma A.2]
, we have

(8.66)

F_{1}[M_{2}]\lesssim D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\||k|^{-2}v(\tau)\langle\rho\rangle^{2-\tilde{\ell}}.

For
\tilde{\ell}\in(1,3)
, using
\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}}=\rho^{3}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{-1}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-1-\tilde{\ell}}\big)
, provided
1+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>0
, by similar convolution estimates in
\mathbb{R}^{5}
as Lemma
A.2
and
[
132
, Lemmas A.1, A.2]
, we have

(8.67)

\displaystyle F_{2}[M_{2}]\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\||k|^{-2}v(\tau)|y|\int_{0}^{1}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{u|y|\leq 1\}}+|uy|^{1-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{u|y|>1\}}\big)du

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\||k|^{-2}v(\tau)\bigg(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\begin{cases}\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}},&\tilde{\ell}\in(1,2)\\
\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&\tilde{\ell}=2\\
1,&\tilde{\ell}\in(2,3)\end{cases}\bigg).

By (
8.64
), (
8.65
), (
8.66
) and (
8.67
), we solve
\psi_{o,k}
for
|k|\geq 2
in the space

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{o,k}:=\big\{f(\rho,\tau)\ |\ |f(\rho,\tau)|\leq D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|w_{o,k}(\rho,\tau)\big\},

\displaystyle\mbox{ \ where \ }

\displaystyle w_{o,k}(\rho,\tau):=|k|^{-2}v(\tau)\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\begin{cases}\rho^{2-\tilde{\ell}},&\tilde{\ell}\in(1,2)\cup(2,3)\\
\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&\tilde{\ell}=2\end{cases}\Big)

and
D_{o}
is a sufficiently large constant.
For any
\tilde{\psi}_{o,k}\in\mathcal{B}_{o,k}
,

\displaystyle\big|\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 4R\}}\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\tilde{\psi}_{o,k}\big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-2-\tilde{\ell}}\langle\ln\rho\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 4R\}}

(8.68)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(R_{0}^{-2}\ln R_{0})D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-\tilde{\ell}}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 4R\}}.

Compared with (
8.63
), since
R_{0}^{-2}\ln R_{0}
is small, it follows that

\rho^{|k+1|}\Gamma_{2|k+1|+2}^{\natural}**\big(|z|^{-|k+1|}J[\tilde{\psi}_{o,k},\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{o,k}.

We can deduce the contraction mapping property in the same way.
Now we have found a solution
\psi_{o,k}=\psi_{o,k}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]\in\mathcal{B}_{o,k}
. Let us estimate the following term in
{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}}
.

(8.69)

\big|\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\psi_{o,k}\big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\ln R_{0}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{-1}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-2-\tilde{\ell}}\big)\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}}.

Provided
2+\tilde{\ell}>\ell
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>-3/2
, by Lemma
8.5
and the scaling argument,

\displaystyle\Big|{\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\Big\{\Big[e^{ik\theta}(a-ib)\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\psi_{o,k}\Big]_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\Big\}\Big|\lesssim|k|^{-1}D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\ln R_{0}\|h_{k}\|w_{i,k}(\rho,\tau),

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla{\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\Big\{\Big[e^{ik\theta}(a-ib)\tilde{V}_{k}(\rho)\psi_{o,k}\Big]_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\Big\}\Big|\lesssim D_{o}D_{i}R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\ln R_{0}\|h_{k}\|w_{i,k,2}(\rho,\tau).

In sum, we take
1<\tilde{\ell}<\min\{\ell,3\}
,
\ell\in(1,3)
. By the small quantity
R_{0}^{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)/2}\ln R_{0}
, we have

{\mathcal{T}}_{kr}^{2R_{0}}\Big[\Big(K[\psi_{o,k}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}]]e^{ik\theta}\Big)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\Big]\in\mathcal{B}_{i,k}.

The contraction property can be deduced in the same way, and thus, we find a solution
\Psi_{i,k}=\Psi_{i,k}[h_{k}]\in\mathcal{B}_{i,k}
. Finally we find a solution
(\psi_{o,k},\Psi_{i,k})
for (
8.57
) and (
8.59
).

Substituting the right-hand side
h_{k}
by
c_{1}h_{k}^{(1)}
,
c_{2}h_{k}^{(2)}
,
c_{1}h_{k}^{(1)}+c_{2}h_{k}^{(2)}
respectively, where
c_{1},c_{2}
are arbitrary constants and
h_{k}^{(1)}
,
h_{k}^{(2)}
are in the same topology as
h_{k}
, then making subtraction and repeating process above,
we deduce that
\Psi_{i,k}[h_{k}]
and
\psi_{o,k}[h_{k}]
are linear mappings about
h_{k}
. So does
\psi_{k}
.

We will regard
D_{o}
,
D_{i}
, and
R_{0}
as general constants hereafter. Recalling (
8.63
), (
8.68
),
2+\tilde{\ell}>\ell
, we have

|J[\psi_{o,k},\psi_{i,k}]|\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}\lesssim\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\rho^{-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 4R\}}
.
Similar to (
8.64
), (
8.66
), (
8.67
), for
\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
, the spatial decay of
\psi_{o,k}
can be improved to

(8.70)

|\psi_{o,k}|\lesssim|k|^{-2}\|h_{k}\|v(\tau)\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\begin{cases}\rho^{2-\ell},&\ell\in(1,2)\cup(2,3)\\
\langle\ln\rho\rangle,&\ell=2\end{cases}\Big).

By the upper bounds of
\psi_{o,k}
in (
8.70
) and
\Psi_{i,k}
in (
8.61
), we get the upper bound of
\Psi_{k}
in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R}
.
∎

8.4.
Mode
0

Proposition 8.2
.

Consider

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{0}\right)+H_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\\
\Psi_{0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where
H_{0}=\left(h_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
. Suppose
\ln R\in\mathbf{AP}
, (
8.4
),
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
with
\theta_{R,\ell}
given in (
8.26
), then there exists a linear mapping
\Psi_{0}={\mathcal{T}}_{00}^{R}[H_{0}]
with the estimate

|\Psi_{0}|\lesssim\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 1\}}|y|+\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|>1\}}|y|^{-1}\big)\begin{cases}R^{2}\ln R&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>1\\
R^{2}(\ln R)^{\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=1\\
R^{3-\ell}\ln R&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<1.\end{cases}

Moreover,
\Psi_{0}\cdot W=0
and
\left(\Psi_{0}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Proof.

Denote
\|h_{0}\|=\|h_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}
.
In order to find a solution with the form
\Psi_{0}=\left(\psi_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, by Lemma
8.1
, it is equivalent to considering

(8.71)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{0}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{0}\psi_{0}+h_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\\
\psi_{0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

By (
8.4
), (
8.23
) is true. Then Lemma
8.3
gives
\|\psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h_{0}\|
. To improve the spatial decay, we reformulate (
8.71
) into the following form

(8.72)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{0}=(a-ib)\big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\psi_{0}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{0}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\psi_{0}\big)+\tilde{h}_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\\
\psi_{0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R},\quad\psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where
\tilde{h}_{0}:=(a-ib)\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\psi_{0}+h_{0}
.
Set
\psi_{0}=\rho\psi_{*0}
. Then (
8.72
) is equivalent to

(8.73)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{*0}=(a-ib)\Delta_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\psi_{*0}+|y|^{-1}\tilde{h}_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{R},\\
\psi_{*0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R},\quad\psi_{*0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where we abuse the symbol
\mathcal{D}_{R}=\left\{(y,\tau)\ |\ y\in\mathbb{R}^{4},\ |y|\leq R(\tau)\right\}
as the corresponding time-varying domain in
\mathbb{R}^{4}
and similarly
{\partial}\mathcal{D}_{R}
,
B_{R(\tau_{0})}
.
By the same argument for deducing (
8.44
), the fundamental solution for (
8.73
) is given by
\Gamma_{4}(x,y,t,s)
with the bound

|\Gamma_{4}(x,y,\tau,s)|\lesssim(\tau-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{\kappa|x-y|^{2}}{\tau-s}}\mbox{ \ for a constant \ }\kappa>0.

Provided
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
, for
\rho\leq R(\tau)
, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{0}|=\rho|\psi_{*0}|\lesssim\rho\big|\Gamma_{4}**\big(|z|^{-1}|\tilde{h}_{0}|\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq R(s)\}}\big)\big|\lesssim\|h_{0}\|\rho\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}(\tau-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{\kappa|y-z|^{2}}{\tau-s}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big[(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(s)\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 1\}}|z|^{-1}+\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-5}\Big)+v(s)\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-1-\ell}\Big]dzds

(8.74)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-1}\big)\|h_{0}\|,

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we used the following calculation.
By Lemma
A.1
,

(8.75)

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}(\tau-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{\kappa|y-z|^{2}}{\tau-s}}(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(s)\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-5}dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 1\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|y|\leq R\}}|y|^{-2}\big)

provided
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
.
By
[
132
, Lemma A.1]
,

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}(\tau-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{\kappa|y-z|^{2}}{\tau-s}}(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(s)\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 1\}}|z|^{-1}dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\tau^{-2}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{16\tau}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(s)ds+R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 1\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|y|\leq R\}}|y|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{16\tau}}\big).

To get the upper bound in (
8.75
), we require
\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(s)ds\lesssim\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)
.

If
\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>-1
, it holds since
\tau R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\lesssim\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\Leftrightarrow R^{2}\lesssim\tau
, which is true. If
\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]=-1
, it suffices to make
\tau(\ln\tau)^{m}R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\lesssim\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\Leftrightarrow R^{2}(\ln\tau)^{m}\lesssim\tau

for some large constant
m\geq 0
, which is true by (
8.4
).
If
\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]<-1
, since
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
, we only need to ensure
\tau_{0}(R^{2}\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(\tau_{0})\lesssim\tau_{0}^{2}(\ln R\theta_{R,\ell}v)(\tau_{0})\Leftrightarrow R^{2}(\tau_{0})\lesssim\tau_{0}
, which is true.

The estimate including
\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-1-\ell}
is deduced by
[
132
, Lemma A.1]
, due to the property of
\theta_{R,\ell}
, we only need to consider
\ell<3
. We omit the details.
∎

In contrast to (
8.17
)
4
for mode
k
,
|k|\geq 2
, the elliptic operator in mode
0

admits a
bounded
kernel function with
decay
, and as a consequence, the decay information of the right-hand side might get lost when deriving estimates. In fact, decay of the solution can be recovered if an orthogonality condition is imposed. The linear theory of mode
0
with the orthogonality condition is given below.

Lemma 8.6
.

Consider

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{0}\right)+H_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},

where
H_{0}
is defined in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R_{*}}
with
R\leq R_{*}\leq\infty
,
H_{0}=\left(h_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}<\infty
with
\ell\in(1,3)
and the orthogonality condition

(8.76)

\int_{0}^{R_{*}(\tau)}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\tau\in(\tau_{0},\infty)

holds. Suppose
\ln R\in\mathbf{AP}
, (
8.4
),
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}R^{3-\ell}v(\tau)]>0
, then there exists a solution
\Psi_{0}={\mathcal{T}}_{0r}^{R}[H_{0}]
as a linear mapping in
H_{0}
with the estimate

(8.77)

\langle y\rangle|\nabla\Psi_{0}|+|\Psi_{0}|\lesssim v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\ln R\langle y\rangle^{-3}\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R}.

Moreover,
\Psi_{0}\cdot W=0
and
\left(\Psi_{0}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Proof.

Denote
\|h_{0}\|=\|h_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}
and assume
h_{0}=0
in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R_{*}}^{c}
. We consider

\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}G_{0}\right)=H_{0}\mbox{ \ where \ }G_{0}=\left(g_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}.

By Lemma
8.1
, it is equivalent to
\left(a-ib\right)\mathcal{L}_{0}g_{0}=h_{0}
,
where
g_{0}
is given by

g_{0}(\rho,\tau)=\left(a+ib\right)\Big(\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr-\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(r)rdr\Big).

It follows that

\partial_{\rho}g_{0}(\rho,\tau)=\left(a+ib\right)\Big(\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}_{0,2}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr-\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}_{0,1}(\rho)\int_{0}^{\rho}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,2}(r)rdr\Big).

By (
8.17
), the orthogonality condition (
8.76
), if
1<\ell<3
, for all
\rho\in(0,\infty)
, we have

(8.78)

\rho|\partial_{\rho}g_{0}|+|g_{0}|\lesssim\|h_{0}\|v(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}\rho^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{2-\ell}\big),\quad\|G_{0}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}=\|g_{0}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}\lesssim\|h_{0}\|.

Similar to the computations on the
\mathsf{DMO_{x}}
semi-norm of
G_{k}
in Lemma
8.5
(but simpler since we do not need its dependence on
k
now), we have

[G_{0}]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0,\tau))}\lesssim\|h_{0}\|v(\tau),\quad[G_{0}(x_{*}+\rho_{*}z,t_{*}+\rho_{*}^{2}s)]_{\mathsf{|DMO|_{x}}(Q_{2}^{-}(0))}\lesssim\|h_{0}\|v(t_{*})|x_{*}|^{1-\ell}\rho_{*}

for
t_{*}>\tau_{0}
,
1\leq|x_{*}|\leq 3R(t_{*})/2
,
\rho_{*}=|x_{*}|/100
.
Next, let us consider

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\Phi_{0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{0}\right)+G_{0}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\\
\Phi_{0}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{2R},\quad\Phi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R(\tau_{0})}.\end{cases}

Provided
\ln R\in\mathbf{AP}
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}[\tau^{2}R^{3-\ell}v(\tau)]>0
, by (
8.78
) and Proposition
8.2
, there exists a solution
\Phi_{0}=\Phi_{0}[G_{0}]
with the form
\Phi_{0}=\left(\phi_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
for some scalar function
\phi_{0}
and the estimate

|\Phi_{0}(y,\tau)|\lesssim v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\ln R\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|G_{0}\|_{v,\ell-2}^{\infty}.

By Proposition
6.1
(for
|y|\leq 1
), Proposition
6.2
(for
|y|>1
), and the interpolation inequality, then

(8.79)

\langle y\rangle^{2}|D^{2}\Phi_{0}|+\langle y\rangle|D\Phi_{0}|+|\Phi_{0}|\lesssim v(\tau)R^{5-\ell}\ln R\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|h_{0}\|\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathcal{D}_{3R/2}.

We take the desired strong solution as
\Psi_{0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{0}\right)
. Combining (
8.79
) and the scaling argument, we conclude (
8.77
). Applying
\Phi_{0}=(\phi_{0}(\rho,\tau))_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
and Lemma
8.1
, we have
\left(\Psi_{0}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{0}\phi_{0}
.
∎

Proposition 8.3
.

Consider

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{0}\right)+H_{0}+\left(c_{0}(\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},

where
H_{0}
is defined in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R_{*}}
with
R\leq R_{*}\leq\infty
,

H_{0}=\left(h_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}<\infty
. Suppose (
8.4
),
\ln R_{0}\in\mathbf{AP}
,

\ell\in(1,3)
,

\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>(\ell-5)/2
,
then there exists a solution
(\Psi_{0},c_{0})=({\mathcal{T}}_{0}^{R}[H_{0}],c_{0}[H_{0}])
as a linear mapping in
H_{0}
with the estimates

\langle y\rangle|\nabla\Psi_{0}|+|\Psi_{0}|\lesssim\ln R_{0}v(\tau)\left(R_{0}^{5-\ell}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 2R_{0}\}}+\langle y\rangle^{2-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|>2R_{0}\}}\right)\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}},

c_{0}(\tau)=-\Big(\int_{0}^{2}\eta(r)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}^{2}(r)rdr\Big)^{-1}\Big(\int_{0}^{R_{1}}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr+c_{*0}[H_{0}](\tau)\Big),

where
2R_{0}\leq R_{1}\leq R_{*}
,
c_{*0}[H_{0}]
is a scalar function linearly depending on
H_{0}
and satisfies

|c_{*0}[H_{0}](\tau)|\lesssim R_{0}^{1-\ell}\ln R_{0}v(\tau)\|H_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}
.
Moreover,
\Psi_{0}\cdot W=0
and
\left(\Psi_{0}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Proof.

Denote
\|h_{0}\|=\|h_{0}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{*}}
and
set
h_{0}=0
in
{\mathcal{D}}_{R_{*}}^{c}
. By Lemma
8.1
, to find a solution
\Psi_{0}
with the form
\Psi_{0}=\left(\psi_{0}(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, it is equivalent to

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{0}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{0}\psi_{0}+h_{0}+c_{0}(\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\psi_{0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})}.

Set
\psi_{0}=\eta_{R_{0}}(\rho)\psi_{i,0}(\rho,\tau)+\psi_{o,0}(\rho,\tau)
, where
\eta_{R_{0}}(\rho)=\eta(\rho/R_{0})
. In order to find a solution
\psi_{0}
, it suffices to consider the following inner-outer system

(8.80)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{o,0}=(a-ib)\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\psi_{o,0}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{o,0}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\psi_{o,0}\Big)+J[\psi_{o,0},\psi_{i,0}]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2}\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\\
&\psi_{o,0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(8.81)

{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{i,0}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{0}\psi_{i,0}+K[\psi_{o,0}]+c_{0}(\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}},\quad\psi_{i,0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R_{0}(\tau_{0})},

where

\displaystyle J[\psi_{o,0},\psi_{i,0}]=(a-ib)(1-\eta_{R_{0}})\tilde{V}_{0}(\rho)\psi_{o,0}+A_{0}[\psi_{i,0}]+(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{0},\quad\tilde{V}_{0}(\rho)=\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}},

\displaystyle K[\psi_{o,0}]=(a-ib)\tilde{V}_{0}(\rho)\psi_{o,0}+h_{0},\quad A_{0}[\psi_{i,0}]=(a-ib)\Big[\Big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}+\frac{{\partial}_{\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}}{\rho}\Big)\psi_{i,0}+2{\partial}_{\rho}\eta_{R_{0}}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{i,0}\Big]-\psi_{i,0}{\partial}_{\tau}\eta_{R_{0}}.

Set
\Psi_{i,0}(y,\tau)=(\psi_{i,0})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, that is,
\psi_{i,0}=\Psi_{i,0}\cdot E_{1}+i\Psi_{i,0}\cdot E_{2}
. By Lemma
8.1
, (
8.81
) is equivalent to

(8.82)

{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{i,0}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{i,0}+\left(K[\psi_{o,0}]+c_{0}(\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{2R_{0}},\quad\Psi_{i,0}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{2R_{0}(\tau_{0})}.

For
2R_{0}\leq R_{1}\leq R_{*}
, in order for the orthogonality condition (
8.76
) to hold in (
8.82
), we take

c_{0}(\tau)=c_{0}[\psi_{o,0}](\tau):=C_{0,1}\int_{0}^{R_{1}}\Big[(a-ib)\tilde{V}_{0}(r)\psi_{o,0}(r,\tau)+h_{0}(r,\tau)\Big]\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr,\ C_{0,1}:=-\Big(\int_{0}^{2}\eta(r)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}^{2}(r)rdr\Big)^{-1}.

By Lemma
8.4
and Lemma
8.6
, we reformulate (
8.80
) and (
8.82
) into the following form formally

\displaystyle\psi_{o,0}(\rho,\tau)=

\displaystyle\rho\big[\Gamma_{4}^{\natural}**\big(|z|^{-1}J[\psi_{o,0},\psi_{i,0}]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\big)\big](\rho,\tau,\tau_{0}),

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,0}(y,\tau)=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathcal{T}}_{0r}^{2R_{0}}\left[\left(K[\psi_{o,0}]+c_{0}[\psi_{o,0}](\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\right].

We will solve this system using the contraction mapping theorem.

Denote
H_{I}:=\big[h_{0}+C_{0,1}\big(\int_{0}^{R_{1}}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr\big)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\big]_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
. It is easy to have
\|H_{I}\|_{v,\ell}^{R_{1}}\lesssim\|h_{0}\|
. In view of Lemma
8.6
, if
(H_{I})_{\mathbb{C}}
satisfies the orthogonality condition (
8.76
) (with
R_{*}=R_{1}
) and
\ln R_{0}\in\mathbf{AP}
,
2+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]+(3-\ell)\mathbf{P}_{1}[R_{0}]>0
, we have the estimate

(8.83)

\langle y\rangle|\nabla{\mathcal{T}}_{0r}^{2R_{0}}[H_{I}](y,\tau)|+|{\mathcal{T}}_{0r}^{2R_{0}}[H_{I}](y,\tau)|\leq D_{i}w_{i,0}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{0}\|,

where
D_{i}\geq 1
is a constant and

w_{i,0}(\rho,\tau):=v(\tau)R_{0}^{5-\ell}\ln R_{0}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3}
.
Denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{i,0}:=

\displaystyle\Big\{F(y,\tau)\in C^{1}\left(B_{2R_{0}},\mathbb{R}^{3}\right)\ |\ F(y,\tau)=\left(f(\rho,\tau)\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}\mbox{ \ for some radial scalar function}

\displaystyle f(\rho,\tau)\mbox{ \ and \ }\langle y\rangle|\nabla F(y,\tau)|+|F(y,\tau)|\leq 2D_{i}w_{i,0}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{0}\|\Big\}.

For any
\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\in\mathcal{B}_{i,0}
, denote
\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}=\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\cdot E_{1}+i\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\cdot E_{2}
.
We will find a solution
\psi_{o,0}=\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]
of (
8.80
) by the contraction mapping theorem. Let us estimate
J[\psi_{o,0},\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]
term by term.
By (
2.8
),

\displaystyle\big|{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}\big|=\big|\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}E_{1}+E_{1}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}+i\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}E_{2}+iE_{2}\cdot{\partial}_{\rho}\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}|\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+|\nabla\tilde{\Psi}_{i,0}|\lesssim D_{i}v(\tau)R_{0}^{5-\ell}\ln R_{0}\langle\rho\rangle^{-4}\|h_{0}\|.

Since
|R_{0}^{\prime}|=O(R_{0}^{-1})
in (
8.4
), we have

(8.84)

\displaystyle|A_{0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]|+\left|(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{0}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{i}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}\leq\rho\leq 2R_{0}\}}v(\tau)R_{0}^{-\ell}\ln R_{0}\|h_{0}\|+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\geq R_{0}\}}v(\tau)\rho^{-\ell}\|h_{0}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{i}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\geq R_{0}\}}v(\tau)\ln R_{0}\rho^{-\ell}\|h_{0}\|.

Provided (
8.4
),
\ln R_{0}\in\mathbf{AP}
,
1<\ell<3
, and

\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>(\ell-5)/2
, by Lemma
A.2
, we have

\Big|\rho\Gamma_{4}^{\natural}**\Big\{|z|^{-1}\Big[A_{0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]+(1-\eta_{R_{0}})h_{0}\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\Big\}\Big|\leq D_{o}D_{i}w_{o,0}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{0}\|,

where
D_{o}\geq 1
is a large constant,

w_{o,0}(\rho,\tau):=v(\tau)\ln R_{0}\big(\rho R_{0}^{1-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq R_{0}\}}+\rho^{2-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>R_{0}\}}\big)
.
Denote

(8.85)

\mathcal{B}_{o,0}:=\{f(\rho,\tau)\ |\ |f(\rho,\tau)|\leq 2D_{o}D_{i}w_{o,0}(\rho,\tau)\|h_{0}\|\mbox{ \ for \ }\rho\geq 0,\tau\geq\tau_{0}\}.

For any
\tilde{\psi}_{o,0}\in\mathcal{B}_{o,0}
, we estimate

\displaystyle|(1-\eta_{R_{0}})\tilde{V}_{0}(\rho)\tilde{\psi}_{o,0}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 4R\}}|\lesssim D_{o}D_{i}v(\tau)\ln R_{0}\rho^{-2-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}<\rho\leq 4R\}}\|h_{0}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{o}D_{i}v(\tau)\ln R_{0}\rho^{-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{R_{0}<\rho\leq 4R\}}\|h_{0}\|\big(\inf_{s\geq\tau_{0}}R_{0}(s)\big)^{-2}.

Compared with (
8.84
), due to the small quantity
\big(\inf_{s\geq\tau_{0}}R_{0}(s)\big)^{-2}
by
\inf_{s\geq\tau_{0}}R_{0}(s)\gg 1
in (
8.4
), we have

\rho\Gamma_{4}^{\natural}**\left(|z|^{-1}J[\tilde{\psi}_{o,0},\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4R(s)\}}\right)\in\mathcal{B}_{o,0}.

We can deduce the contraction mapping property in the same way.

Now we have found a solution
\psi_{o,0}=\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]\in\mathcal{B}_{o,0}
. It follows that for
\ell\in(1,3)
,

\Big\|\big|\tilde{V}_{0}(\rho)\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]\big|+C_{0,1}\Big|\int_{0}^{R_{1}}\tilde{V}_{0}(r)\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}](r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)dr\Big|\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)\Big\|_{v,\ell}^{2R_{0}}\lesssim D_{o}D_{i}\sup_{s\geq\tau_{0}}\big(R_{0}^{1-\ell}\ln R_{0}\big)(s)\|h\|.

Due to the choice of
c_{0}(\tau)
,
h_{II}:=K[\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]]+c_{0}[\psi_{o,0}[\tilde{\psi}_{i,0}]](\tau)\eta(\rho)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(\rho)
satisfies the orthogonality condition (
8.76
) with
R_{*}=R_{1}
. Since
\sup_{s\geq\tau_{0}}\big(R_{0}^{1-\ell}\ln R_{0}\big)(s)
provides small quantity by (
8.4
),
similar to (
8.83
), we have

{\mathcal{T}}_{0r}^{2R_{0}}[(h_{II})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}]\in\mathcal{B}_{i,0}.

The contraction property can be deduced in the same way. Therefore, we find a solution
\Psi_{i,0}=\Psi_{i,0}[h_{0}]\in\mathcal{B}_{i,0}
. Finally we find a solution
(\psi_{o,0},\Psi_{i,0})
for (
8.80
) and (
8.82
).
From the construction process,
\psi_{i,0}[h_{0}]
,
\psi_{o,0}[h_{0}]
and
c_{0}[h_{0}]
are linear mappings in
h_{0}
. So does
\psi_{0}
.

We will regard
D_{o}
,
D_{i}
as general constants hereafter. Since
\psi_{o,0}[h_{0}]\in\mathcal{B}_{o,0}
, then

c_{0}[h_{0}](\tau)=C_{0,1}\Big(\int_{0}^{R_{1}}h_{0}(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{0,1}(r)rdr+c_{*0}[h_{0}](\tau)\Big),

where
c_{*0}[h_{0}](\tau)
is a linear mapping in
h_{0}
, and
|c_{*0}[h_{0}](\tau)|\lesssim R_{0}^{1-\ell}\ln R_{0}v(\tau)\|h_{0}\|
.

Combining the upper bound of
\psi_{o,0}
and
\Psi_{i,0}
, we have

|\Psi_{0}(y,\tau)|\lesssim\ln R_{0}v(\tau)\big(R_{0}^{5-\ell}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 2R_{0}\}}+\langle y\rangle^{2-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|>2R_{0}\}}\big)\|h_{0}\|\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R}.

By the scaling argument, we conclude the validity of the proposition.
∎

Remark 8.2
.

The reason that we solve
\psi_{o,0}
in (
8.85
) with elaborated pointwise bound in
\rho>4R
is to give a uniform estimate of
c_{*0}[H_{0}]
when
R_{1}=R_{*}=\infty
. It will be more convenient when solving the reduced equations. The reasoning is the same for the refined estimates of
\psi_{o,1}
in Proposition
8.5
below.

8.5.
Mode
1

Proposition 8.4
.

Consider

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\Psi_{1}=\left(a-bW\wedge\right)\left(L_{\rm{in}}\Psi_{1}\right)+H_{1}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\\
\Psi_{1}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\Psi_{1}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where
H_{1}=\left(h_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta}\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|H_{1}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}<\infty
. Suppose
\ln R\in\mathbf{AP}
when
\ell=1
, (
8.4
),

(8.86)

\mbox{either Case 1: \ }\mathbf{P}_{1}[R]<1/4\mbox{ \ or Case 2: \ }\mathbf{P}_{1}[R]>1/4,\ 4\mathbf{P}_{1}[R]+2\mathbf{P}_{1}[\theta_{R,\ell}]+2\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(\tau)]>-1\mbox{ holds},

and
3+\min\{1/2,\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{2}]\}+\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{-2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
with
\theta_{R,\ell}
given in (
8.26
),
then there exists a linear mapping
\Psi_{1}={\mathcal{T}}_{10}^{R}[H_{1}]
with the estimate

|\Psi_{1}(y,\tau)|\lesssim\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\langle y\rangle^{-2}\|H_{1}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}.

Moreover,
\Psi_{1}\cdot W=0
and
e^{-i\theta}\left(\Psi_{1}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}
is radial in space.

Proof.

To find a solution
\Psi_{1}=\left(\psi_{1}(\rho,\tau)e^{i\theta}\right)_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
, by Lemma
8.1
, we consider

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\psi_{1}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{1}\psi_{1}+h_{1}=(a-ib)\big({\partial}_{\rho\rho}\psi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{1}-\frac{4}{\rho^{2}}\psi_{1}\big)+\tilde{h}_{1}\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\\
\psi_{1}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}{\mathcal{D}}_{R},\quad\psi_{1}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{R(\tau_{0})},\end{cases}

where
\tilde{h}_{1}=(a-ib)\frac{12\rho^{2}+4}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\psi_{1}+h_{1}
. Denote
\|h_{1}\|=\|h_{1}\|_{v,\ell}^{R}
. By (
8.86
) and Lemma
8.3
, we get
\|\psi_{1}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{R})}\lesssim\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h_{1}\|
.

To get spatial decay, by similar argument for (
8.74
), for
\rho\leq R
,

\displaystyle|\psi_{1}|\lesssim\rho^{2}\Big|\Gamma_{6}**\Big(|y|^{-2}|\tilde{h}_{1}|\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq R\}}\Big)\Big|\lesssim\|h_{1}\|\rho^{2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}(\tau-s)^{-3}e^{-\frac{\kappa|y-z|^{2}}{\tau-s}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\big(\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v\big)(s)\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 1\}}|z|^{-4}+\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-6}\Big)+v(s)\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-2-\ell}\Big]dzds

(8.87)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\|h_{1}\|\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-2}\langle\ln\rho\rangle\big),

where
|\Gamma_{6}(x,y,t,s)|\lesssim(t-s)^{-3}e^{-\frac{\kappa|x-y|^{2}}{t-s}}
with a constant
\kappa>0
, and we used
\ln R\in\mathbf{AP}
when
\ell=1
, (
8.4
),
3+\min\{1/2,\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{2}]\}+\mathbf{P}_{1}[R^{-2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)]>0
, Lemma
A.1
for the term
\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-6}
and
[
132
, Lemma A.1]
for the other two terms. Since
\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}
may not stay in
\mathbf{AP}
, we have used
\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
R^{2}
in the calculations separately instead. In the last step,
\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-2}\langle\ln\rho\rangle
is from the convolution related to
\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-6}
, and the other two terms give the bound
\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\rho^{-2}
.

Plugging the new upper bound of
|\psi|
into (
8.87
), the bound
\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-6}
can be improved to
\mathbf{1}_{\{1<|z|\leq R(s)\}}|z|^{-7}
, for
\rho\leq R
, we obtain
|\psi_{1}|\lesssim\min\{\tau^{\frac{1}{2}},R^{2}\}R^{2}\theta_{R,\ell}v(\tau)\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\|h_{1}\|
.
∎

Lemma 8.7
.

Proposition 8.5
.

8.6.
Mode
-1

To prepare for the linear theory, we first summarize the properties of the spectrum, Fourier basis
\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
, spectrum measure
\rho_{-1}(d\xi)
of
-\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}
in the next proposition, where

\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}:={\partial}_{\rho\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\rho^{-2}+V_{-1}(\rho)={\partial}_{\rho\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\rho^{-2}-\frac{4}{\rho^{2}}-\frac{-4}{\rho^{2}+1}+\frac{8}{(\rho^{2}+1)^{2}}.

Proposition 8.6

(
[
75
]
)
.

The spectrum of
-\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}
is
[0,\infty)
, which is also the essential spectrum. Denote the Fourier basis of
-\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}
as
\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
, where
\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
satisfies

-\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)=\xi\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\mbox{ \ for all \ }\xi\geq 0.

For
\rho\geq 0
,
\xi\geq 0
, we have

(8.100)

\displaystyle|\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|\lesssim\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho^{2}\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho^{2}\xi>1\}},

\displaystyle|\partial_{\rho}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|\lesssim\rho^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho^{2}\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho^{2}\xi>1\}}.

\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
has the expansion

(8.101)

\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)=\Phi^{-1}_{0}(\rho)+\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\infty}(-\rho^{2}\xi)^{j}\Phi_{j}(\rho^{2}),

which converges absolutely, where
\Phi_{0}^{-1}(\rho)=\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(1+\rho^{2})^{-1}
. It converges uniformly if
\rho\xi^{\frac{1}{2}}
remains bounded. Here
\Phi_{j}(u)\geq 0
are smooth functions of
u\geq 0
satisfying

\Phi_{j}(u)\leq\frac{1}{j!}\frac{u}{1+u}
,
|\Phi_{j}^{\prime}(u)|\leq\frac{21}{j!}
for
u\geq 0
,
j\geq 1
,
and
\Phi_{1}(u)\geq c_{1}\frac{u}{1+u}
for
u\geq 0
with a constant
c_{1}>0
.

The spectrum measure
\rho_{-1}(d\xi)
of
-\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}
is supported in
\xi\in[0,\infty)
and absolutely continuous on
\xi\geq 0
with density

\frac{d\rho_{-1}(\xi)}{d\xi}\sim\langle\xi\rangle^{2}
.

Proof.

Most of the estimates can be found in
[
75
, Propositions 5.1, 5.3, 5.4, 5.5]
. Derivative estimate (
8.100
)
2
can be derived similarly as in
[
75
]
. Indeed, by similar induction in
[
75
, pp. 32-33]
, we have
|\Phi_{j}^{\prime}(u)|\leq\frac{21}{j!}
for
u\geq 0
,
j\geq 1
. Acting
\partial_{\rho}
on (
8.101
), for
\rho^{2}\xi\leq 1
, we have
|\partial_{\rho}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|\lesssim\rho^{\frac{3}{2}}
. For the remote region
\rho^{2}\xi>1
, the use of
[
75
, Propositions 5.4, 5.5]
and the relation between the Weyl-Titchmarsh function and
\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
gives the estimate
|\partial_{\rho}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|\lesssim\xi^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}
.
∎

We emphasize that the assumption (
8.4
) is not required in the next proposition.

Proposition 8.7
.

Consider

{\partial}_{\tau}\Phi_{-1}=(a-bW\wedge)(L_{\rm{in}}\Phi_{-1})+H_{-1}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\quad\Phi_{-1}(\cdot,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{2},

where
\tau_{0}\geq 2
,
H_{-1}=(h(\rho,\tau)e^{-i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
,
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}<\infty
,
0\leq v(\tau)\in L_{\rm{loc}}^{\infty}([\tau_{0},\infty))
,
\ell>3/2
.
\Phi_{-1}={\mathcal{T}}_{-1}[H_{-1}]
is given as a linear mapping in
H_{-1}
by the convolution via the fundamental solution of the parabolic system. Moreover,
\Phi_{-1}(y,\tau)=(\phi_{-1}(\rho,\tau)e^{-i\theta})_{\mathbb{C}^{-1}}
and
\phi_{-1}
satisfies

(8.102)

{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{-1}=(a-ib)\mathcal{L}_{-1}\phi_{-1}+h\mbox{ \ for \ }(\rho,\tau)\in(0,\infty)\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\quad\phi_{-1}(\rho,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\rho\in(0,\infty)

with the estimate

\displaystyle|\phi_{-1}(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim\

\displaystyle\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\begin{cases}\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\ln\tau)^{2}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-1}\ln\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\ln\tau\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

(8.103)

\displaystyle+\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\ln\tau\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\ln\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2,\end{cases}

where we assume
v(s)=0
for
s\leq\tau_{0}
.
Moreover, if we suppose in addition
2<\ell<5/2
and the orthogonality condition

(8.104)

\int_{0}^{\infty}h(r,\tau)\mathcal{Z}_{-1,1}(r)rdr=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\tau>\tau_{0}

holds, then we have the estimate

(8.105)

|\phi_{-1}(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}\begin{cases}\langle\rho\rangle^{2-\ell}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\Big)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho>\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{cases}

Proof.

Similar to the argument in Lemma
8.3
, the theory of the parabolic system guarantee the existence of
\Phi_{-1}
and
\phi_{-1}
. And
\Phi_{-1},\nabla\Phi_{-1}\in L^{\infty}(\Omega\times(\tau_{0},\tau_{1}))
for any bounded domain
\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
\tau_{1}>\tau_{0}
. Using the argument in (
8.62
), we have that
\phi_{-1},\partial_{\rho}\phi_{-1}
is bounded in
(0,\rho_{1})\times(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
for any
\rho_{1}>0
and
\tau_{1}>\tau_{0}
.

We will give a representation formula for
\phi_{-1}
. First, besides
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}<\infty
, we assume

(8.106)

\displaystyle\mbox{$h(\rho,\tau)$ is smooth in spatial variable $\rho\in\mathbb{R}$ and for $\tau_{0}<\tau<\tau_{1}<\infty$,}

\displaystyle\mbox{$h(\rho,\tau)=0$ for $|\rho|\geq M_{\tau_{1}}$ with a constant $M_{\tau_{1}}>0$ depending on $\tau_{1}$.}

Take
\phi_{-1}=\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}f(\rho,\tau)
. Since
\mathcal{L}_{-1}(\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}f)=\big(\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\rho\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\rho^{-\frac{5}{2}}+V_{-1}(\rho)\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)f
, then

(8.107)

{\partial}_{\tau}f=(a-ib)\tilde{{\mathcal{L}}}_{-1}f+\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}h\mbox{ \ for \ }(\rho,\tau)\in(0,\infty)\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\quad f(\rho,\tau_{0})=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\rho\in(0,\infty).

Due to the assumption (
8.106
), given a fixed
\tau>\tau_{0}
,
\Phi_{-1}
,
\nabla\Phi_{-1}
have fast spatial decay as
|y|\to\infty
. So do
\phi_{-1}
,
\partial_{\rho}\phi_{-1}
,
f
, and
\partial_{\rho}f
. And
|f(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
|\partial_{\rho}f(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}
as
\rho\downarrow 0
. Combining these with (
8.100
), we are able to
multiply (
8.107
) by
\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)
and integrate by parts in
\rho\in(0,\infty)
to deduce

\partial_{\tau}\hat{f}(\xi,\tau)+(a-ib)\xi\hat{f}(\xi,\tau)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}h(\rho,\tau)\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)d\rho,\quad\hat{f}(\xi,\tau_{0})=0,

where we denote
\hat{f}(\xi,\tau):=\int_{0}^{\infty}f(\rho,\tau)\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)d\rho
. It follows that

\hat{f}(\xi,\tau)=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}e^{-(a-ib)\xi(\tau-s)}\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)dxds.

Using the distorted Fourier transform, we get the representation formula

\displaystyle\phi_{-1}=\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}f(\rho,\tau)=\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\hat{f}(\xi,\tau)\rho_{-1}(d\xi)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}e^{-(a-ib)\xi(\tau-s)}\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)dxds\rho_{-1}(d\xi)

(8.108)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-(a-ib)\xi(\tau-s)}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)\rho_{-1}(d\xi)dxds.

For general
h
satisfying
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}<\infty
, if the last integral in (
8.108
) is absolutely integrable, then (
8.108
) gives the representation formula of
\phi_{-1}
. Hereafter, without loss of generality, we assume
\|h\|_{v,\ell}^{\infty}=1
and will prove that (
8.108
) is absolutely integrable and give pointwise estimate of
\phi_{-1}
.

Estimate without orthogonality.
Using the property of
\rho_{-1}(d\xi)
in Proposition
8.6
, we have

|\phi_{-1}|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}v(s)\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}|\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)||\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)|x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}\langle\xi\rangle^{2}dxd\xi ds.

We will use the estimate of
|\Phi^{-1}|
in (
8.100
) repetitively. First, we consider

F(\xi):=\int_{0}^{\infty}|\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)|x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx=\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}+\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}\cdots:=F_{1}+F_{2}.

For
F_{1}
, one has

F_{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}x^{\frac{5}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-2}x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\ln\xi\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}\quad+\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\xi^{-2}.

For
F_{2}
, since
\ell>\frac{3}{2}
,
F_{2}\lesssim\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\xi^{-\frac{5}{4}}
,
and thus

(8.109)

F(\xi)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\ln\xi\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}\quad+\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\xi^{-\frac{5}{4}}.

Next, let us estimate

P(\rho,\tau,s):=\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}|\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi=\int_{0}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}+\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}\cdots:=P_{1}+P_{2}.

First, let us estimate
P_{1}
.
Note that
P_{1}\lesssim\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\int_{0}^{\rho^{-2}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi
.
For
\rho\geq 1
, since
\xi\leq\rho^{-2}\leq 1
, by Lemma
B.1
,

\displaystyle P_{1}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\rho^{-2}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\ln\xi\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{\frac{1}{2}-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}\langle\ln\rho\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

For
\rho<1
,

P_{1}\lesssim\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\big(\int_{0}^{1}+\int_{1}^{\rho^{-2}}\big)e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi
. By the same estimate above,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

Using (
8.109
) and direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\lesssim\int_{1}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{3}{4}}d\xi\sim(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}\int_{a(\tau-s)}^{\frac{a(\tau-s)}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-z}z^{\frac{3}{4}}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{7}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1.\end{cases}

Thus for
\rho<1
,

P_{1}\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

Next, let us estimate
P_{2}
. By (
8.100
),
P_{2}\lesssim\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi
.
For
\rho\leq 1
, by (
8.109
),

P_{2}\lesssim\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}d\xi\sim(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{a(\tau-s)}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-z}z^{-\frac{1}{2}}dz\lesssim\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}.\end{cases}

For
\rho>1
,
P_{2}\lesssim\big(\int_{1}^{\infty}+\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{1}\big)e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi
.
For the same reason as above, we have

\int_{1}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1.\end{cases}

By (
8.109
),
\ell>1/2
, and Lemma
B.1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}F(\xi)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\lesssim\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-\frac{5}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\ln\xi\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

Thus, for
\rho>1
,

P_{2}\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

Combining the estimates of
P_{1}
and
P_{2}
, we have the following estimates of
P
. For
\rho\leq 1
,

P\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

For
\rho>1
with
\ell\geq 1/2
,

P\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

Now we will use the upper bound of
P
to estimate
\phi_{-1}
. For
\rho\leq 1
, since we assume
\tau_{0}\geq 2
, then

\displaystyle|\phi_{-1}|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}\Big)v(s)P(\rho,\tau,s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bigg[\tilde{v}(\tau)\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}ds+\tilde{v}(\tau)\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}ds

\displaystyle+\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\begin{cases}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(a(\tau-s))\rangle ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-1}ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\tilde{v}(\tau)\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}+\rho^{2}\begin{cases}\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)(\ln\tau)^{2}+\tau^{-1}\ln\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\ln\tau+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2,\end{cases}

where we denote
\tilde{v}(\tau):=\sup\limits_{s\in[\tau/2,\tau]}v(s)
.
For
1<\rho\leq(\tau/2)^{1/2}
,

\displaystyle|\phi_{-1}|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\Big)v(s)P(\rho,\tau,s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tilde{v}(\tau)\begin{cases}\rho^{2-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\ln\rho\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}+\begin{cases}\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\int_{\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\langle\ln z\rangle z^{-1}dz+\tau^{-1}\ln\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\ln(\frac{\tau}{2\rho^{2}})+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)(\langle\ln\rho\rangle+\int_{\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\langle\ln z\rangle z^{-1}dz)+\tau^{-1}\ln\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\langle\ln(\frac{\tau}{2\rho^{2}})\rangle+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

For
(\tau/2)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

\displaystyle|\phi_{-1}|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\Big(\int_{\frac{\tau}{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\Big)+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\Big]v(s)P(\rho,\tau,s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tilde{v}(\tau)\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\ln\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}v(s)ds\begin{cases}\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{-1}\langle\ln\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}\Big]\lesssim\begin{cases}\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\ln\tau\rangle\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tau^{-1}\langle\ln\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

For
\rho\geq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

|\phi_{-1}|\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}\Big)v(s)P(\rho,\tau,s)ds\lesssim\tilde{v}(\tau)\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\begin{cases}\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{if \ }\ell<2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\ln\tau\rangle+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\ln\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{if \ }\ell=2\\
\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{if \ }\ell>2.\end{cases}

In sum, we have proved that (
8.108
) is absolutely integrable and (
8.103
) holds.

Estimate with orthogonality.
Recalling the estimates of
|\phi_{-1}|
in four cases above, we have

(8.110)

\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big|\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-(a-ib)\xi(\tau-s)}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)\rho_{-1}(d\xi)dxds\Big|\lesssim\tilde{v}(\tau)\Big(\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}+\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}\Big).

For the other part, we denote

\tilde{\phi}_{-1}:=\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big|\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-(a-ib)\xi(\tau-s)}\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)\rho_{-1}(d\xi)dxds\Big|.

By the orthogonality condition (
8.104
), we have

\tilde{F}(\xi,s):=\Big|\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx\Big|=\Big|\Big(\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}+\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}\Big)\Big(\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)-\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}\Big)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx\Big|.

We will use Proposition
8.6
repetitively. Firstly, for
\ell<4
, we have

\Big|\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\Big(\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)-\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}\Big)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx\Big|\lesssim v(s)\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}x^{2}\xi x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx\lesssim v(s)\big(\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\big).

Secondly,

\Big|\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}\Phi^{-1}(x,\xi)x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx\Big|\lesssim v(s)\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}x^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx\sim v(s)\big(\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{-\frac{5}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\big),

where we require
\ell>3/2
to guarantee the integrability.

Thirdly, by (
8.104
) and
\ell>2
, we have
\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx=-\int_{0}^{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx
. Then

\Big|\int_{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\infty}\frac{x^{\frac{5}{2}}}{1+x^{2}}x^{\frac{1}{2}}h(x,s)dx\Big|\lesssim v(s)\big(\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}\big),

and thus

(8.111)

\tilde{F}(\xi,s)\lesssim v(s)(\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi\leq 1\}}+\xi^{-\frac{5}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\xi>1\}}).

Next, using
\frac{d\rho_{-1}(\xi)}{d\xi}\sim\langle\xi\rangle^{2}
on
\xi\geq 0
in Proposition
8.6
, we will estimate

\tilde{P}(\rho,\tau,s):=\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}|\Phi^{-1}(\rho,\xi)|\tilde{F}(\xi,s)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi=\int_{0}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}+\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}\cdots:=\tilde{P}_{1}+\tilde{P}_{2}.

Let us estimate
\tilde{P}_{1}
. For
\rho\geq 1
, by Proposition
8.6
, (
8.111
),
\ell>0
, and Lemma
B.1
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle v(s)\int_{0}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\sim v(s)\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle v(s)\big[\rho^{\frac{1}{2}-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\}}+\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>\rho^{2}\}}\big].

For
\rho<1
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\tilde{F}(\xi,s)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\sim\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}+\int_{1}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}\Big)e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\tilde{F}(\xi,s)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle v(s)\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}d\xi+\int_{1}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{3}{4}}d\xi\Big)\lesssim v(s)\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{7}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}

since

\int_{0}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}d\xi\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq 1\}}+(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>1\}}

by
\ell>0
and Lemma
B.1
, and

\int_{1}^{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{3}{4}}d\xi\sim(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}\int_{a(\tau-s)}^{\frac{a(\tau-s)}{\rho^{2}}}e^{-z}z^{\frac{3}{4}}dz\lesssim\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{7}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1.\end{cases}

Next, we will use Proposition
8.6
and (
8.111
) to estimate
\tilde{P}_{2}
. For
\rho\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle v(s)\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\xi^{-\frac{5}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\sim v(s)\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}d\xi

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle v(s)(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{a(\tau-s)}{\rho^{2}}}^{\infty}e^{-z}z^{-\frac{1}{2}}dz\lesssim v(s)(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\}}+e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>\rho^{2}\}}\Big).

For
\rho>1
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{2}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{1}+\int_{1}^{\infty}\Big)e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\tilde{F}(\xi,s)\langle\xi\rangle^{2}d\xi\lesssim v(s)\Big(\int_{\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-\frac{5}{4}}d\xi+\int_{1}^{\infty}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}d\xi\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle v(s)\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq 1\}}+(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\}}+(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2\rho^{2}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>\rho^{2}\}}

\displaystyle+(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq 1\}}+(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>1\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle v(s)\Big[(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s\leq 1\}}+(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\}}+(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{a(\tau-s)}{4\rho^{2}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau-s>\rho^{2}\}}\Big],

where we used
\ell>1/2
and Lemma
B.1
to estimate
\int_{1/\rho^{2}}^{1}e^{-a\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-\frac{5}{4}}d\xi
. Combining the estimates of
\tilde{P}_{1}
,
\tilde{P}_{2}
, since
\ell\geq 1/2
, we have

\tilde{P}(\rho,\tau,s)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq 1\}}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{7}{4}}&\mbox{if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}\ +\ \mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>1\}}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}.\end{cases}

Finally, we will estimate
\tilde{\phi}_{-1}
. Obviously,

\tilde{\phi}_{-1}\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}\tilde{P}(\rho,\tau,s)ds
.
For
\rho\leq 1
, since
\ell>2
,

\tilde{\phi}_{-1}\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)\rho^{\frac{5}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds\lesssim\rho^{2}\Big(\tilde{v}(\tau)+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\Big).

For
1<\rho\leq(\tau/2)^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

2<\ell<5/2
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}_{-1}\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\Big)\tilde{P}(\rho,\tau,s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\tilde{v}(\tau)\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds+\Big(\int_{\frac{\tau}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\Big)v(s)\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds\Big]\lesssim\tilde{v}(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds.

For
(\tau/2)^{\frac{1}{2}}<\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
,
\ell<5/2
,

\tilde{\phi}_{-1}\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\Big(\int_{\frac{\tau}{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\Big)+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\Big]\tilde{P}(\rho,\tau,s)ds\lesssim\tilde{v}(\tau)\rho^{2-\ell}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds.

For
\rho>\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}
,
\ell<5/2
,

\tilde{\phi}_{-1}\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\tilde{v}(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\Big).

Combining (
8.110
) with all the estimates of
\tilde{\phi}_{-1}
above, we conclude the estimate (
8.105
).
∎

Appendix A
Pointwise estimates for heat and Laplace equations

Recall
algebraic power type
(
\mathbf{AP}
) defined at the very beginning of Section
8
.

Lemma A.1
.

Suppose
n>2
,
v(t),l_{1}(t),l_{2}(t)\in\mathbf{AP}
,
b\in{{\mathbb{R}}}
,
l_{1}(t)\leq l_{2}(t)\leq C_{*}t^{\frac{1}{2}}
with a constant
C_{*}>0
,

\begin{cases}\mathbf{P}_{1}[t^{\frac{n}{2}}v(t)l_{2}^{2-b}(t)]>0&\mbox{ \ if \ }b\leq n\\
\mathbf{P}_{1}[t^{\frac{n}{2}}v(t)l_{2}^{2-n}(t)l_{1}^{n-b}(t)]>0&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>n,\end{cases}

given constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
and
f(x,t)=v(t)|x|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(t)\leq|x|\leq l_{2}(t)\}}
or
v(t)(|x|+l_{1}(t))^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq l_{2}(t)\}}
, then for all
|x|\leq l_{2}(t)
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(t-s)^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-c_{1}\big(\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{t-s}\big)^{c_{2}}}f(y,s)dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle v(t)\begin{cases}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<2\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2\\
l_{1}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }|x|\leq l_{1}(t)\\
\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<2\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{|x|})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2\\
|x|^{2-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<b<n\\
|x|^{2-n}\langle\ln(\frac{|x|}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=n\\
|x|^{2-n}l_{1}^{n-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>n\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }l_{1}(t)<|x|\leq l_{2}(t),\end{cases}

where “
\lesssim
” is independent of
t_{0}
.

Lemma A.2
.

Suppose
2<b<n
,
v(t),l_{1}(t),l_{2}(t)\in\mathbf{AP}
,
l_{1}(t)\leq l_{2}(t)\leq C_{*}t^{\frac{1}{2}}
with a constant
C_{*}>0
,
\frac{n}{2}-\frac{b}{2}+1+\mathbf{P}_{1}[v(t)]>0
,
given constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
and
f(x,t)=v(t)|x|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(t)\leq|x|\leq l_{2}(t)\}}
or
v(t)(|x|+l_{1}(t))^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq l_{2}(t)\}}
, then for all
(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{n}\times(t_{0},\infty)
, we have

\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(t-s)^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-c_{1}\big(\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{t-s}\big)^{c_{2}}}f(y,s)dyds\lesssim v(t)\big(l_{1}^{2-b}(t)\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq l_{1}(t)\}}+|x|^{2-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>l_{1}(t)\}}\big),

where “
\lesssim
” is independent of
t_{0}
.

Proof of Lemmas
A.1
and
A.2
.

The proof is a direct application of the analog of
[
132
, Lemma A.1]
. We omit details.
∎

The estimates in Lemmas
A.1
and
A.2
do not show the dependence on parameters clearly.
To get estimates with precise dependence on
k
in the linear theory of mode
k
,
|k|\geq 2
in Subsection
8.3
, we need the following lemma.

Lemma A.3
.

Consider
-\Delta u=f(x)
in
\mathbb{R}^{n}\backslash\{0\}
,
where
n\geq 3
,
f(x)=f(|x|)
is radial with the upper bound
|f(x)|\lesssim|x|^{-l_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 1\}}+|x|^{-l}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>1\}}
,
l_{1}<n
,
l>2
.
u
is given by

(A.1)

u(x)=\frac{1}{(n-2)|S^{n-1}|}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}|x-y|^{2-n}f(y)dy,

where
|S^{n-1}|
is the volume of the unit sphere
S^{n-1}
. Then

(A.2)

u(x)=u(|x|)=|x|^{2-n}\int_{0}^{|x|}a^{n-3}\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)dbda,\quad{\partial}_{|x|}u=-|x|^{1-n}\int_{0}^{|x|}f(a)a^{n-1}da.

When
f(x)=|x|^{-l_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 1\}}+|x|^{-l}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>1\}}
, we have

(A.3)

{\partial}_{|x|}u=\frac{-|x|^{1-l_{1}}}{n-l_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 1\}}-\bigg(\frac{|x|^{1-n}}{n-l_{1}}+\begin{cases}\frac{|x|^{1-l}-|x|^{1-n}}{n-l}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l<n\\
|x|^{1-n}\ln|x|&\mbox{ \ if \ }l=n\\
\frac{|x|^{1-n}-|x|^{1-l}}{l-n}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l>n\end{cases}\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>1\}};

for
|x|\leq 1
,

u(x)=\frac{1}{(l-2)(n-2)}+\begin{cases}\frac{1}{(2-l_{1})(n-2)}-\frac{|x|^{2-l_{1}}}{(2-l_{1})(n-l_{1})}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}<2\\
\frac{-\ln|x|}{n-2}+\frac{1}{(n-2)^{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}=2\\
\frac{|x|^{2-l_{1}}}{(l_{1}-2)(n-l_{1})}-\frac{1}{(l_{1}-2)(n-2)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l_{1}<n,\end{cases}

for
|x|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle u(x)=\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{n-2}\Big(\frac{1}{l-2}+\frac{1}{n-l_{1}}\Big)+\begin{cases}\frac{|x|^{2-l}-|x|^{2-n}}{(l-2)(n-l)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}\ln|x|}{n-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l=n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}-|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(l-n)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l>n\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(n-l)}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})}-\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l)}&\mbox{if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}\ln|x|}{n-2}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{n-2}(\frac{1}{n-2}+\frac{1}{n-l_{1}})&\mbox{if \ }l=n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(l-n)}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})}-\frac{|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(l-n)}&\mbox{if \ }l>n\end{cases}=\begin{cases}\frac{|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(n-l)}+\frac{(l_{1}-l)|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})(n-l)}&\mbox{if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}\ln|x|}{n-2}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{n-2}(\frac{1}{n-2}+\frac{1}{n-l_{1}})&\mbox{if \ }l=n\\
\frac{(l-l_{1})|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})(l-n)}-\frac{|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(l-n)}&\mbox{if \ }l>n.\end{cases}

In particular,
for
|x|\geq 1
,

u(x)\geq\begin{cases}\frac{|x|^{2-l}}{(l-2)(n-2)}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}\ln|x|}{n-2}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{n-2}(\frac{1}{n-2}+\frac{1}{n-l_{1}})&\mbox{ \ if \ }l=n\\
\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(l-2)(n-2)}+\frac{|x|^{2-n}}{(n-2)(n-l_{1})}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l>n.\end{cases}

Proof.

l_{1}<n
and
l>2
ensure the integrability of (
A.1
). Since
f(x)=f(|x|)
, it is easy to see that
u
is radial. Due to the upper bound of
f(|x|)
, by the removable singularity theorem for harmonic functions (It is used for the case
2\leq l_{1}<n
) and maximum principle, we have the formula of
u(x)
in (
A.2
). And the deduction of
{\partial}_{|x|}u
in (
A.2
) is straightforward.

When
f(r)=r^{-l_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 1\}}+r^{-l}\mathbf{1}_{\{r>1\}}
with
r=|x|
, we only present the calculation of
u(x)
.
{\partial}_{|x|}u
is similar. For
a\geq 1
,
l>2
, then
\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)db=\frac{a^{2-l}}{l-2}
.
For
0<a\leq 1
,

\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)db=\int_{1}^{\infty}b^{1-l}db+\int_{a}^{1}b^{1-l_{1}}db=\frac{1}{l-2}+\begin{cases}\frac{1}{2-l_{1}}(1-a^{2-l_{1}})&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}<2\\
-\ln a&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}=2\\
\frac{1}{l_{1}-2}(a^{2-l_{1}}-1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}>2.\end{cases}

For
0<r\leq 1
,

(A.4)

\int_{0}^{r}a^{n-3}\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)dbda=\frac{r^{n-2}}{(l-2)(n-2)}+\begin{cases}\frac{r^{n-2}}{(2-l_{1})(n-2)}-\frac{r^{n-l_{1}}}{(2-l_{1})(n-l_{1})}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}<2\\
\frac{r^{n-2}(-\ln r)}{n-2}+\frac{r^{n-2}}{(n-2)^{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}=2\\
\frac{r^{n-l_{1}}}{(l_{1}-2)(n-l_{1})}-\frac{r^{n-2}}{(l_{1}-2)(n-2)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l_{1}<n,\end{cases}

where
l_{1}<n
guarantees the integrability around
0
.
For
r\geq 1
, since

\int_{1}^{r}a^{n-3}\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)dbda=\int_{1}^{r}\frac{a^{n-1-l}}{l-2}da=\begin{cases}\frac{r^{n-l}-1}{(l-2)(n-l)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{\ln r}{n-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l=n\\
\frac{1-r^{n-l}}{(l-2)(l-n)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l>n,\end{cases}

then combining (
A.4
), we have

\int_{0}^{r}a^{n-3}\int_{a}^{\infty}bf(b)dbda=\frac{1}{n-2}(\frac{1}{l-2}+\frac{1}{n-l_{1}})+\begin{cases}\frac{r^{n-l}-1}{(l-2)(n-l)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }2<l<n\\
\frac{\ln r}{n-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l=n\\
\frac{1-r^{n-l}}{(l-2)(l-n)}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l>n.\end{cases}

The left calculations are direct, where for the last lower bound, we used that for
|x|\geq 1
,
|x|^{2-n}\leq|x|^{2-l}
when
l<n
, and
|x|^{2-l}\leq|x|^{2-n}
when
l>n
.
∎

Appendix B
Integral estimates for the distorted Fourier transform

Lemma B.1
.

Suppose
0\leq\lambda<\infty
,
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
,
\int_{0}^{1}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx<\infty
,
that is, either
(a,b)\in(-1,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}
or

(a,b)\in\{-1\}\times(-\infty,-1)
holds, then for
0\leq x_{0}\leq x_{1}\leq 1/2
, we have

(B.1)

\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx\leq C_{1}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x_{1}^{a+1}(-\ln x_{1})^{b}&\mbox{if \ }a>-1\\
(-\ln x_{1})^{b+1}-(-\ln x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{if \ }a=-1,b<-1\end{cases}&\mbox{for \ }0\leq\lambda\leq x_{1}^{-1}\\
\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}+\begin{cases}0&\mbox{if \ }a>-1\\
(\ln\lambda)^{b+1}-(-\ln x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{if \ }a=-1,b<-1\end{cases}&\mbox{for \ }x_{1}^{-1}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-1}\\
\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}&\mbox{for \ }\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-1},\end{cases}

where the constant
C_{1}>0
only depends on
a,b
, and the case
\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-1}
is vacuum when
x_{0}=0
.

For
0\leq x_{0}\leq 1/2<x_{1}
, we have

(B.2)

\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx\leq C_{2}\begin{cases}1&\mbox{for \ }0\leq\lambda\leq 2\\
\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}+\begin{cases}0&\mbox{if \ }a>-1\\
(\ln\lambda)^{b+1}-(-\ln x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{if \ }a=-1,b<-1\end{cases}&\mbox{for \ }2\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-1}\\
\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}&\mbox{for \ }\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-1},\end{cases}

where the constant
C_{2}>0
only depends on
a,b,~\int_{1/2}^{x_{1}}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx
.

Proof.

We first consider the case
0\leq x_{0}\leq x_{1}\leq 1/2
. For
0\leq\lambda\leq x_{1}^{-1}
,

(B.3)

\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx\sim\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx\lesssim\begin{cases}x_{1}^{a+1}(-\ln x_{1})^{b}&\mbox{if \ }a>-1\\
(-\ln x_{1})^{b+1}-(-\ln x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{if \ }a=-1,b<-1,\end{cases}

where for the last step for the case
a>-1
, we used the following calculation. If
a>-1
,

\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx=\frac{1}{a+1}\big[x_{1}^{a+1}(-\ln x_{1})^{b}-x_{0}^{a+1}(-\ln x_{0})^{b}\big]+\frac{b}{a+1}\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b-1}dx.

When
x_{1}\leq C_{3}
with a constant
0<C_{3}<1/2
sufficiently small depending on
a,b
, we have
\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx\lesssim x_{1}^{a+1}(-\ln x_{1})^{b}
. When
C_{3}<x_{1}\leq 1/2
, the estimate holds due to the assumption
\int_{0}^{1}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx<\infty
.

For
\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-1}
,

(B.4)

\displaystyle\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx=\frac{1}{\lambda^{a+1}}\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{x_{1}\lambda}e^{-z}z^{a}(\ln\lambda-\ln z)^{b}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda^{a+1}}\begin{cases}(\ln\lambda)^{b}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }x_{0}^{-1}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-2}\\
e^{-\frac{3x_{0}\lambda}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-2}\end{cases}\ \lesssim\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}.

In order to get the first “
\lesssim
” above, we need the following estimates.

If
x_{1}\lambda\leq\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}
, that is,
\lambda\leq x_{1}^{-2}
, then

(B.5)

\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{x_{1}\lambda}e^{-z}z^{a}(\ln\lambda-\ln z)^{b}dz\sim(\ln\lambda)^{b}\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{x_{1}\lambda}e^{-z}z^{a}dz\lesssim(\ln\lambda)^{b}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}.

If
x_{0}\lambda\geq\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}
, that is,
\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-2}
, then

(B.6)

\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{x_{1}\lambda}e^{-z}z^{a}(\ln\lambda-\ln z)^{b}dz\lesssim e^{-\frac{3x_{0}\lambda}{4}},

since
-\ln x_{1}\leq\ln\lambda-\ln z\leq\ln(\frac{\lambda}{x_{0}\lambda})\leq\frac{\ln\lambda}{2}
,

z^{a}\lesssim\begin{cases}1&\mbox{if \ }a\leq 0\\
\lambda^{a}&\mbox{if \ }a>0\end{cases}
,

(\ln\lambda-\ln z)^{b}\lesssim\begin{cases}1&\mbox{if \ }b\leq 0\\
(\ln\lambda)^{b}&\mbox{if \ }b>0\end{cases}
.

If
x_{0}\lambda\leq\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq x_{1}\lambda
, that is,
x_{1}^{-2}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-2}
, by (
B.5
), (
B.6
), then

\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{x_{1}\lambda}e^{-z}z^{a}(\ln\lambda-\ln z)^{b}dz=\int_{x_{0}\lambda}^{\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\int_{\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{x_{1}\lambda}\cdots\lesssim(\ln\lambda)^{b}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}+e^{-\frac{3\lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}}{4}}\lesssim(\ln\lambda)^{b}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}},

where we used
x_{0}^{-1}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-2}
for the last step.

For
x_{1}^{-1}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-1}
, by (
B.3
), (
B.4
), we have

\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}(-\ln x)^{b}dx=\int_{x_{0}}^{1/\lambda}+\int_{1/\lambda}^{x_{1}}\cdots\lesssim\frac{(\ln\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}+\begin{cases}0&\mbox{if \ }a>-1\\
(\ln\lambda)^{b+1}-(-\ln x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{if \ }a=-1,b<-1.\end{cases}

In sum, we complete the proof of (
B.1
).

For
0\leq x_{0}\leq 1/2<x_{1}
,
\int_{0}^{1/2}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx
can be handled by (
B.1
).
\int_{1/2}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx\leq e^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\int_{1/2}^{x_{1}}x^{a}\langle\ln x\rangle^{b}dx
. Thus we have (
B.2
).
∎

Appendix C
Convolution estimates in finite time

C.1.
Preliminaries

We need the following relationship repetitively: for
s\leq t
and
t\leq t_{*}\leq T
,

(C.1)

\displaystyle(T-s)/2\leq t-s\leq T-s\mbox{ \ for \ }s\leq t-(T-t);

\displaystyle T-t\leq T-s\leq 2(T-t)\mbox{ \ for \ }s\geq t-(T-t);

\displaystyle(t_{*}-s)/2\leq t-s\leq t_{*}-s\mbox{ \ for \ }s\leq t-(t_{*}-t);
