\displaystyle t_{*}-t\leq t_{*}-s\leq 2(t_{*}-t)\mbox{ \ for \ }s\geq t-(t_{*}-t).

Lemma C.1
.

Given
x,q\in{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}
,
p>0
,
b\geq 0
, and
L>0
,
0\leq L_{1}\leq L_{2}\leq\infty
, we have

(C.2)

\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{L}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{L_{1}\leq|y-q|\leq L_{2}\}}dy\lesssim\begin{cases}0&\mbox{if \ }L_{1}=L_{2}\\
\begin{cases}L^{\frac{d}{2}}L_{1}^{-b}&\mbox{if \ }L\leq L_{1}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }L_{1}^{2}<L\leq L_{2}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }L>L_{2}^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }L_{1}<L_{2}.\end{cases}

In particular, for
L_{3}\geq CL>0
with a constant
C>0
, we have

(C.3)

\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{L}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq\sqrt{L_{3}}\}}dy\lesssim L^{\frac{d}{2}}L_{3}^{-\frac{b}{2}}.

Remark C.1
.

The estimate for the case
b<0
is different, and we do not analyze it here.

Proof of (
C.2
).

For
L_{1}=L_{2}
, the conclusion is trivial.
For
L_{1}<L_{2}
,

\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{L}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{L_{1}\leq|y-q|\leq L_{2}\}}dy=L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|\tilde{x}-z|^{p}}|z|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}dz,

where
\tilde{x}=(x-q)L^{-\frac{1}{2}}
.
If
L_{1}=0
,

\displaystyle L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|\tilde{x}-z|^{p}}|z|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}dz\leq L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|z|^{p}}|z|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\infty&\mbox{ \ if \ }b\geq d\\
\begin{cases}L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }L\leq L_{2}^{2}\\
L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }L>L_{2}^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d.\end{cases}

If
L_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|\tilde{x}-z|^{p}}|z|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}dz\leq L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|\tilde{x}-z|^{p}}\min\{|z|^{-b},(L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}})^{-b}\}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}dz

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle L^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c|z|^{p}}\left[(L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}})^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|z|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{L_{1}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}<|z|\leq L_{2}L^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]dz,

whose upper bound is presented in (
C.2
) for the case
L_{1}<L_{2}
.
∎

Next, we want to establish the basic calculation of the time variable.
Given
0\leq L_{1}\leq L_{2}\leq\infty
,
t>0
, for
s<t
, we set

(C.4)

g(s):=\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}}L_{1}^{-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq L_{1}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }L_{1}^{2}<t-s\leq L_{2}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s>L_{2}^{2},\end{cases}

where we use the convention
L_{1}^{-1}L_{2}=1
if
L_{1}=L_{2}=0
.

Claim:
for
\delta>0
, if
\frac{d}{2}+1>d_{*}>\frac{d}{2}+1-\delta
,

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\leq\infty
,
and for
\delta=0
,
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
,

(C.5)

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\}}{L_{2}^{2}})\rangle L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\}}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\}}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d;\end{cases}

and if
\delta>0
,
d_{*}\leq\frac{d}{2}+1-\delta
,

(C.6)

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}\leq 1-\delta\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }1-\delta<d_{*}\leq 1+\frac{d-b}{2}-\delta\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}-\delta\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}\leq 1-\delta\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}>1-\delta\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}(\max\{x,L_{2}^{2}\})^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}\leq 1-\delta\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }d_{*}>1-\delta\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

Proof.

For
x\leq L_{1}^{2}
, when
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
, then
\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim x^{\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{-b}
; For
L_{1}^{2}<x\leq L_{2}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds=\Big(\int_{t-L_{1}^{2}}^{t}+\int_{t-x}^{t-L_{1}^{2}}\Big)g(s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}+\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{\frac{d}{2}+1-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}},&b<d+2-2d_{*}\\
\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}}),&b=d+2-2d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&b>d+2-2d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle,&d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle^{2},&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}}),&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}\sim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{\frac{d}{2}+1-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}},&b<d+2-2d_{*}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle,&b=d+2-2d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&b>d+2-2d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle,&d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle^{2},&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d;\end{cases}

For
x>L_{2}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds=\Big(\int_{t-L_{2}^{2}}^{t}+\int_{t-x}^{t-L_{2}^{2}}\Big)g(s)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&b<d+2-2d_{*}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&b=d+2-2d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&b>d+2-2d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle^{2},&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}+\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})L_{2}^{d-b},&d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}<1\\
\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}

\displaystyle\sim\

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
L_{2}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b},&d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle,&d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}},&d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

Thus,
for
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
, if
x\leq L_{1}^{2}
,

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim x^{\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{-b}
;
if
L_{1}^{2}<x\leq L_{2}^{2}
,

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{\frac{d}{2}+1-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d+2-2d_{*}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d+2-2d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d+2-2d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle^{2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d;\end{cases}

if
x>L_{2}^{2}
,

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{2}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

Then for
\delta\in\mathbb{R}
, if

x\leq L_{1}^{2}
,

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*}\\
\infty&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*};\end{cases}

if
L_{1}^{2}<x\leq L_{2}^{2}
,

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b\leq d+2-2d_{*}-2\delta\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d+2-2d_{*}-2\delta\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d+2-2d_{*}\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d+2-2d_{*}\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d+2-2d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle^{2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{d-b-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d;\end{cases}

if
x>L_{2}^{2}
,

\displaystyle x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})\rangle L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{d-b-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle^{2}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
\begin{cases}x^{-\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 0\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>0\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

In particular,
for
\delta=0
,

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}\leq\frac{d}{2}+1\\
\infty&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>\frac{d}{2}+1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }x\leq L_{1}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle^{2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }L_{1}^{2}<x\leq L_{2}^{2};\end{cases}

if
x>L_{2}^{2}
,

\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{2}^{2}})\rangle L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{x}{L_{1}^{2}})\rangle L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

For
\delta>0
,

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*}\\
\infty&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>\frac{d}{2}+1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }x\leq L_{1}^{2}\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}\leq 1+\frac{d-b}{2}-\delta\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}-\delta\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }L_{1}^{2}<x\leq L_{2}^{2};\end{cases}

if
x>L_{2}^{2}
,

x^{-\delta}\int_{t-x}^{t}g(s)ds\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{2}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}-2\delta}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle^{2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x^{1-d_{*}-\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta\leq 1-d_{*}\\
L_{2}^{2-2d_{*}-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}&\mbox{if \ }\delta>1-d_{*}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d-b}\langle\ln(\frac{L_{2}}{L_{1}})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
L_{2}^{-2\delta}L_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

∎

Lemma C.2
.

Given
c>0
,
p>0
,
b\geq 0
,
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
,
c_{1}>0
,
t\in[0,T]
, suppose

(C.7)

\displaystyle v(s)\geq 0\mbox{ \ for \ }s\in[0,T],\quad C_{l}^{-1}l_{i}(t)\leq l_{i}(s)\leq C_{l}l_{i}(t)\mbox{ \ for \ }i=1,2,\ s\in\left[\left[t-(T-t)\right]_{+},t\right],

\displaystyle 0\leq l_{1}(s)\leq l_{2}(s)\leq C(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mbox{ \ for \ }s\in[0,T]

with some constants
C_{l}\geq 1
,
C>0
independent of
T
, then for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq c_{1}(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}-\frac{b}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-d_{*}+\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}},

(C.8)

\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds\lesssim\tilde{P}_{1}+\tilde{P}_{2},

where
c_{+}:=\max\left\{c,0\right\}
for any
c\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{1}:=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-d_{*}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{2}:=\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-d_{*}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{2}^{2}(t)})\rangle l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
l_{2}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<d_{*}<1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}\\
l_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
l_{1}^{2-2d_{*}}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-d_{*}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}=1\\
l_{1}^{d+2-b-2d_{*}}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d_{*}>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

Remark C.2
.

When
b=0<d
, the cases
d_{*}=1+\frac{d-b}{2}
and

d_{*}>1+\frac{d-b}{2}
are vacuum.

Proof.

For the first part,
by (
C.3
),
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq c_{1}(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\Big)v(s)(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}}(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}-\frac{b}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-d_{*}+\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}.

For the second part,
by (
C.2
),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{p}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\cdots:=P_{1}+P_{2}.

For
P_{1}
, since
(T-s)/2\leq t-s\leq T-s
,
l_{2}^{2}(s)\leq C(T-s)
, we have
P_{1}\lesssim\tilde{P}_{1}
.

For
P_{2}
, since
T-t\leq T-s\leq 2(T-t)
,
d_{*}<\frac{d}{2}+1
, we have

P_{2}\lesssim\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-d_{*}}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}-d_{*}}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-d_{*}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds\lesssim\tilde{P}_{2}

by (
C.5
) (used for the second “
\lesssim
”) and
l_{2}^{2}(t)\leq C(T-t)
.
∎

C.2.
Convolution involving
v(t)|x-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(t)\leq|x-q|\leq l_{2}(t)\}}

Proposition C.1
.

Let
d\geq 1
be an integer,
b\geq 0
,
0\leq t<T
,
q\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Given
\Gamma(x,t,y,s)
in Proposition
6.3
and
|f(y,s)|\leq v(s)|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}
for
(y,s)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(0,T)

with functions
v,l_{1},l_{2}
satisfying (
C.7
), denote

\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t):=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)f(y,s)dyds
.
Then, using the convention
C_{1}/C_{2}=1
if
C_{1}=C_{2}=0
, we have

(C.9)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-\frac{d}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d<2\\
l_{2}^{2-b}(t)\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{2}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=2\\
l_{2}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>2,b<2\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2\\
l_{1}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d<2\\
\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=2\\
l_{1}^{2-d}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>2\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}(T-t)^{1-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d<2\\
l_{1}^{2-b}(t)\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=2\\
l_{1}^{2-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>2\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

(C.10)

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{1-b}(t)\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{2}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{2}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1,b<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=1\\
l_{1}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{1}^{1-d}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}l_{1}^{1-b}(t)\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{1}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d.\end{cases}

(C.11)

\left|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)\right|\lesssim\tilde{T}_{31}+\tilde{T}_{32}+\tilde{T}_{33},

where

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{31}:=(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{d}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds,

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{32}:=\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\begin{cases}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(t)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(t)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-\frac{d}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{33}:=\int_{t}^{T}(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}v(s)\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds.

For
0<\alpha<1
,

(C.12)

\left|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)\right|\lesssim C(\alpha)\big(\tilde{T}_{41}+\tilde{T}_{42}\big)+\tilde{T}_{43},

where

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{41}:=\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds,

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{42}:=\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{2}^{2}(t)})\rangle l_{2}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{2}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1,b<1\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=1\\
l_{1}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\begin{cases}\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{1}^{1-d}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
\begin{cases}\langle\ln(\frac{T-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle l_{1}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d=1\\
l_{1}^{1-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }d>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{43}:=\int_{t}^{T}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds.

For
0<\alpha<1
and
t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
, suppose additional assumption

(C.13)

C_{l}^{-1}l_{i}(t)\leq l_{i}(s)\leq C_{l}l_{i}(t)\mbox{ \ for \ }i=1,2,\quad s\in[t,(T+t)/2],

we have

(C.14)

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\alpha)(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{\alpha}(\tilde{T}_{1}^{1}+\tilde{T}_{2}^{1}+\tilde{T}_{3}^{1}),

where for
\gamma\in\mathbb{R}
, we define

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{1}^{\gamma}:=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds,

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{2}^{\gamma}:=\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<2-\gamma-\alpha\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2-\gamma-\alpha\\
l_{1}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2-\gamma-\alpha,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{3}^{\gamma}:=\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+},t_{*}]}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b\leq 2-\gamma-\alpha\\
l_{1}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2-\gamma-\alpha.\end{cases}

For
0<\alpha<1
and
t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
, suppose
d>2-\alpha
, (
C.13
) additionally, we have

(C.15)

|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\alpha)(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{\alpha}(\tilde{T}_{1}^{0}+\tilde{T}_{2}^{0}+\tilde{T}_{3}^{0}).

All “
\lesssim
” above are independent of
T
.

Proof of (
C.9
), (
C.10
).

(
C.9
) and (
C.10
) are derived by (
6.3
) and (
C.8
).
∎

Proof of (
C.11
).

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x,T,y,s))f(y,s)dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x,T,y,s))f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{T}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

By (
6.3
), one has

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|({\partial}_{t}\Gamma)(x,\theta t+(1-\theta)T,y,s)\right||f(y,s)|dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left[\theta t+(1-\theta)T-s\right]^{-1-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{\theta t+(1-\theta)T-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}v(s)|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}d\theta dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds\lesssim\tilde{T}_{31},

where we used (
C.1
) for the third “
\lesssim
”; (
C.2
),
l_{2}^{2}(s)\leq C(T-s)
for the fourth “
\lesssim
”.

By (
6.3
), we estimate

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c\big(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}}\big)^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds\ \lesssim\tilde{T}_{32},

where we used (
C.2
),
l_{2}^{2}(s)\leq C(T-s)
in the second “
\lesssim
”; and (
C.1
) in the third “
\lesssim
”.

By (
6.3
), (
C.2
), and
l_{2}^{2}(s)\leq C(T-s)
, we have
|I_{3}|\lesssim\tilde{T}_{33}
.
∎

Proof of (
C.12
).

\displaystyle{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s))f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{T}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

For
I_{1}
, by (
6.5
) and (
C.2
),

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(\alpha)(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(T-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big[(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}})^{2-\delta}}\big]

\displaystyle\times v(s)|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds\lesssim C(\alpha)(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{b+1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\begin{cases}(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(T-s)^{-\frac{d+1+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle C(\alpha)(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\cdots\Big):=C(\alpha)(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}(I_{11}+I_{12}).

For
I_{11}
, by
t-s\sim T-s\gtrsim l_{2}^{2}(s)
, we have

I_{11}\lesssim\left(T-t\right)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\tilde{T}_{41}
.

For
I_{12}
, by (
C.7
), (
C.5
),

\displaystyle I_{12}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}\ \lesssim(T-t)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\tilde{T}_{42}.

For
I_{2}
, by (
6.3
), (
C.2
), we have
|I_{2}|\lesssim\tilde{T}_{43}
.
∎

Proof of (
C.14
) and (
C.15
).

For brevity, denote
|X-X_{*}|=|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}
.
By (
6.5
), (
6.3
), (
6.4
),

\displaystyle\big|{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}|{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)||f(y,s)|dyds+\int_{t}^{t_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}|{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)||f(y,s)|dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(\alpha)|X-X_{*}|^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[(t-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}\Big]|y-q|^{-b}

\displaystyle\times\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds+\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds,

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}|\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)||f(y,s)|dyds+\int_{t}^{t_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}|\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)||f(y,s)|dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(\alpha)|X-X_{*}|^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}\Big]|y-q|^{-b}

\displaystyle\times\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds+\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds.

These two cases can be solved uniformly by the following Lemma.

Lemma C.3
.

Given a constant
\gamma
satisfying
d>2-\gamma-\alpha
,
\gamma+\alpha<2
, denote

\displaystyle I_{1}:=\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{2-\delta}}+(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}\Big]|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds,

\displaystyle I_{2}:=\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x_{*}-y|}{\sqrt{t_{*}-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{l_{1}(s)\leq|y-q|\leq l_{2}(s)\}}dyds.

Then we have
I_{1}\lesssim\tilde{T}_{1}^{\gamma}+\tilde{T}_{2}^{\gamma}+\tilde{T}_{3}^{\gamma}
. Suppose
t_{*}-t\leq(T-t)/2
, (
C.13
) additionally, then

I_{2}\lesssim C(\alpha)(t_{*}-t)^{\alpha/2}\tilde{T}_{3}^{\gamma}
.

The remaining text in this part will be dedicated to proving the above Lemma. For
I_{1}
, by (
C.2
),

\displaystyle I_{1}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{b+\gamma}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{b+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t_{*}-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t_{*}-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}^{t}\cdots:=I_{11}+I_{12}+I_{13}.

For
I_{11}
, by (
C.1
), (
C.7
), we have
t-s\sim t_{*}-s\sim T-s\gtrsim l_{2}^{2}(s)
. Then
I_{11}\lesssim\tilde{T}_{1}^{\gamma}
.

For
I_{12}
, denote
{\rm Int}_{12}:=[[t-(T-t)]_{+},[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}]
. By (
C.1
), (
C.7
), (
C.2
),
d>2-\gamma-\alpha
,
\gamma+\alpha<2
,

\displaystyle I_{12}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in{\rm Int}_{12}}v(t_{1})\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{b+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma+\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in{\rm Int}_{12}}v(t_{1})\begin{cases}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<2-\gamma-\alpha\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2-\gamma-\alpha\\
l_{1}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2-\gamma-\alpha\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t_{*}-t\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<2-\gamma-\alpha\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}^{2}(t)}{t_{*}-t})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=2-\gamma-\alpha\\
(t_{*}-t)^{\frac{2-\gamma-b-\alpha}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>2-\gamma-\alpha\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{\frac{2-\gamma-d-\alpha}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t_{*}-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{\frac{2-\gamma-d-\alpha}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-t\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
(t_{*}-t)^{\frac{2-\gamma-d-\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t_{*}-t>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}\ \lesssim\tilde{T}_{2}^{\gamma},

where for the second “
\lesssim
”, we used
d>2-\gamma-\alpha
and the following calculations. First, we check the case
t_{*}-t>l_{2}^{2}(t)
directly; Next, for
l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-t\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)
, we split
\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}=\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-l_{2}^{2}(t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-l_{2}^{2}(t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}
, and then apply the estimate for
t_{*}-t>l_{2}^{2}(t)
(with
t_{*}-t=l_{2}^{2}(t)
) to
\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-l_{2}^{2}(t)]_{+}}
and direct calculation to
\int_{[t-l_{2}^{2}(t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}
. It follows the desired upper bound in the second “
\lesssim
” for the case
l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-t\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)
;
Finally, the case
t_{*}-t\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)
is deduced by
\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}=\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-l_{1}^{2}(t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-l_{1}^{2}(t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}
, where we used the estimate for
l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-t\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)
(with
t_{*}-t=l_{1}^{2}(t)
) to
\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-l_{1}^{2}(t)]_{+}}
, and
\gamma+\alpha<2
to
\int_{[t-l_{1}^{2}(t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}
.

For
I_{13}
, denote
{\rm Int}_{13}:=[[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+},t]
. By (
C.1
), (
C.7
),

\displaystyle I_{13}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in{\rm Int}_{13}}v(t_{1})(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}^{t}\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }t-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in{\rm Int}_{13}}v(t_{1})(t_{*}-t)^{1-\frac{\gamma+\alpha}{2}}\begin{cases}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{if \ }t_{*}-t\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t_{*}-t}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-t\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{d}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-t)^{-\frac{d}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{if \ }t_{*}-t>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}\ \lesssim\tilde{T}_{3}^{\gamma},

where we used (
C.6
),
d>2-\gamma-\alpha
,
\gamma+\alpha\leq 2
for the last “
\lesssim
”. For
I_{2}
, by (
C.2
),

I_{2}\lesssim\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t_{*}-s}{l_{1}^{2}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(s)<t_{*}-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(s)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(s)}{l_{1}(s)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(s)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s>l_{2}^{2}(s)\end{cases}ds.

Suppose
t_{*}-t\leq(T-t)/2
, (
C.13
) additionally, similar to
I_{13}
,
by (
C.6
),
d>2-\gamma-\alpha
,
\gamma+\alpha\leq 2
, then

\displaystyle I_{2}\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t,t_{*}]}v(t_{1})\int_{t_{*}-(t_{*}-t)}^{t_{*}}\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s\leq l_{1}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{b}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{t_{*}-s}{l_{1}^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }l_{1}^{2}(t)<t_{*}-s\leq l_{2}^{2}(t)\\
\begin{cases}(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{2}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b<d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}\langle\ln(\frac{l_{2}(t)}{l_{1}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }b=d\\
(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{d+\gamma}{2}}l_{1}^{d-b}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }b>d\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }t_{*}-s>l_{2}^{2}(t)\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(\alpha)(t_{*}-t)^{\alpha/2}\tilde{T}_{3}^{\gamma}.

∎

C.3.
Convolution involving
v(t)|x-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q|\geq(T-t)^{1/2}\}}

Proposition C.2
.

Let
d\geq 1
be an integer,
b\geq 0
,
0\leq t<T
,
q\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Given
\Gamma(x,t,y,s)
in Proposition
6.3
,

v(s)\geq 0
for
s\in[0,T]
,
|f(y,s)|\leq v(s)|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq(T-s)^{1/2}\}}
for
(y,s)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(0,T)
, denote
\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t):=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)f(y,s)dyds
.
Then,

(C.16)

\left|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)\right|\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds,\quad\left|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)\right|\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds,

(C.17)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{b}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-\frac{b}{2}}+\int_{t}^{T}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds,

(C.18)

\left|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)\right|\lesssim C(\alpha)\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\tilde{S}_{41}+\int_{t}^{T}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{1+b}{2}}ds,

where

\tilde{S}_{41}:=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{1+b+\alpha}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{1-b-\alpha}{2}}.

For
0<\alpha<1
,
0\leq t<t_{*}\leq T
,

(C.19)

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\alpha)(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{\alpha}\tilde{S}_{51}+\tilde{S}_{52},

where

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{51}:=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{1+b+\alpha}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{\frac{1-b-\alpha}{2}},

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{52}:=\mathbf{1}_{\{t_{*}\leq\frac{T+t}{2}\}}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t,t_{*}]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{-\frac{b}{2}}(t_{*}-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{t_{*}>\frac{T+t}{2}\}}\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds.

For
0<\alpha<1
,
0\leq t<t_{*}\leq T
,

(C.20)

|\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim C(\alpha)(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{\alpha}\tilde{S}_{61}+\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds,

where

\tilde{S}_{61}:=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b+\alpha}{2}}ds+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-\frac{b+\alpha}{2}}.

Proof of (
C.16
).

(
C.16
) is deduced by (
6.3
) and (
C.3
) directly.
∎

Proof of (
C.17
).

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x,T,y,s))f(y,s)dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x,T,y,s))f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{T}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

By (
6.3
) and (
C.3
),

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|({\partial}_{t}\Gamma)(x,\theta t+(1-\theta)T,y,s)\right||f(y,s)|d\theta dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}v(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{b}{2}}ds,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\lesssim\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{t-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}})^{2-\delta}}|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds\lesssim\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[[t-(T-t)]_{+},t]}v(t_{1})(T-t)^{1-\frac{b}{2}},

|I_{3}|\lesssim\int_{t}^{T}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}(T-s)^{-\frac{d}{2}}e^{-c(\frac{|x-y|}{\sqrt{T-s}})^{2-\delta}}v(s)|y-q|^{-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y-q|\geq(T-s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}dyds\lesssim\int_{t}^{T}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds.

∎

Proof of (
C.18
).

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\left({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s)\right)f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{T}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,T,y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

For
I_{1}
, by (
6.5
), (
C.3
),

|I_{1}|\lesssim C(\alpha)(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\left[\left(t-s\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(T-s\right)^{-\frac{b+\alpha}{2}}+\left(T-s\right)^{-\frac{1+b+\alpha}{2}}\right]ds\lesssim C(\alpha)\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\tilde{S}_{41}.

For
I_{2}
, by (
6.3
), (
C.3
), we have
|I_{2}|\lesssim\int_{t}^{T}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{1+b}{2}}ds
.
∎

Proof of (
C.19
).

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\left({\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)\right)f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{t_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}{\partial}_{x_{i}}\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

For
I_{1}
, by (
6.5
), (
C.3
),

|I_{1}|\big(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}\big)^{-\alpha}\lesssim C(\alpha)\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\left(t_{*}-s\right)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left[\left(t-s\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\left(t_{*}-s\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right]\left(T-s\right)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds\lesssim C(\alpha)\tilde{S}_{51},

where in the last step, we split the integral into
\int_{0}^{t}=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}^{t}
to estimate.

For
I_{2}
, by (
6.3
), (
C.3
), then
|I_{2}|\lesssim\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(t_{*}-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds\lesssim\tilde{S}_{52}
.
∎

Proof of (
C.20
).

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{d}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\left(\Gamma(x,t,y,s)-\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)\right)f(y,s)dyds-\int_{t}^{t_{*}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{d}}\Gamma(x_{*},t_{*},y,s)f(y,s)dyds:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

For
I_{1}
, by (
6.4
), (
C.3
),

|I_{1}|\big(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}\big)^{-\alpha}\lesssim C(\alpha)\int_{0}^{t}v(s)\left(t_{*}-s\right)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left(T-s\right)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds\lesssim C(\alpha)\tilde{S}_{61},

where for the last step, we split the integral into
\int_{0}^{t}=\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}+\int_{[t-(t_{*}-t)]_{+}}^{t}
to estimate and used (
C.1
).
For
I_{2}
, by (
6.3
), (
C.3
), then
|I_{2}|\lesssim\int_{t}^{t_{*}}v(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{b}{2}}ds
.
∎

Appendix D
Derivation of the weighted topology for the outer problem

Proposition D.1
.

Given
|f(x,t)|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\big(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\varrho_{3}
with
\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},\varrho_{3}
given in (
4.31
), suppose

\displaystyle 0<\Theta<\beta<1/2,\ 0<\alpha<1,\ \Theta<\alpha/2,

\displaystyle 0<\sigma_{0}<\beta,\ \beta-\sigma_{0}<\alpha/2,\ 1-\sigma_{0}-(1+\alpha)(1-\beta)<0,\ \Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,

(D.1)

\displaystyle 0<A_{\rm{o,h}}<\min\{\Theta+(1-\beta)(1-\alpha),1-2\sigma_{0}-\alpha(1-\beta),1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\},

then for

\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t):=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{2}}\Gamma(x,t,y,s)f(y,s)dyds
with
\Gamma(x,t,y,s)
given in Proposition
6.3
with dimension
d=2
, we have

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim|\ln T|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}(0)R(0),\quad|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0),

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R,\quad|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta},

and for
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\lesssim(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{\alpha}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(t),

\left|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t_{*})\right|\lesssim T^{A_{\rm{o,h}}}(t_{*}-t)^{\alpha/2}.

Proof.

Convolution estimates about
\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}
.
For
|f|\lesssim\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
with
\lambda_{*}R\leq(T-t)^{1/2}
provided
\beta<1/2
,
by (
C.9
),

(D.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(s)(T-s)^{-1}(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(s)ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}|\ln(T-t)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)(s)(T-s)^{-1}ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}\lambda_{*}R|\ln(T-t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)(0)|\ln T|

provided

(D.3)

\beta<1/2,\quad 1+\Theta-\beta>0.

By (
C.10
),

(D.4)

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)

provided

(D.5)

\beta<1/2,\quad\beta-\Theta<1/2,\quad\Theta\geq 0.

By (
C.11
),

(D.6)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(s)(T-s)^{-2}(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(s)ds

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\big[\mathbf{1}_{\{t-s\leq(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)\}}+(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)(t-s)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{t-s>(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)\}}\big]ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R

\displaystyle+\int_{t}^{T}(T-s)^{-1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(s)ds\ \lesssim(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}(s)R(s)(T-s)^{-2}ds

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R|\ln(T-t)|+\int_{t}^{T}(T-s)^{-1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}(s)R(s)ds\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}R|\ln(T-t)|

provided

(D.7)

0<\beta-\Theta<1.

By (
C.12
),

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}

(D.8)

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{t}^{T}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}ds\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}

provided

(D.9)

0<\alpha<1,\quad\beta<1/2,\quad\beta-\Theta<1/2,\quad\Theta<\alpha/2.

By (
C.14
), for
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,

(D.10)

\displaystyle\left|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})\right|\big(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}\big)^{-\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(s)(\lambda_{*}R)(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(t)\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(t)

provided

(D.11)

\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)<0,\ \beta<1/2.

By (
C.15
), for
0<\alpha<1
,
\beta<1/2
, and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
, and

(D.12)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t_{*})\right|(t_{*}-t)^{-\alpha/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}(s)R(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{1-\alpha}\lesssim\big(\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{1-\alpha}\big)(0).

Convolution estimates about
\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}}
.
Consider

|f|\leq\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big(\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}}\big).

We will use Propositions
C.1
and
C.2
repetitively hereafter. Provided

\sigma_{0}<1
,

(D.13)

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\frac{\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)|\ln(T-s)|}{T-s}ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}|\ln(T-t)|^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)}{T-s}ds\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\ln T)^{2}.

(D.14)

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(T-s)^{-1}ds\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(0),

where for the second “
\lesssim
”, we used integration by part for
\int_{0}^{t}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(t-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(T-s)^{-1}ds
, and

(D.15)

\sigma_{0}<\beta<1/2.

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-2}|\ln(T-s)|ds

\displaystyle~+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t}\begin{cases}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-2}(t)&\mbox{ \ if \ }t-s\leq(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)\\
(t-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)})\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)<t-s\leq T-t\end{cases}ds

\displaystyle~+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}|\ln(T-t)|+\int_{t}^{T}(T-s)^{-1}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)|\ln(T-s)|ds+(T-t)\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-2}ds

(D.16)

\displaystyle~+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}+\int_{t}^{T}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-1}ds\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}\ln^{2}(T-t),

where we used
\int_{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}^{t-(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)}(t-s)^{-1}\langle\ln(\frac{t-s}{(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)})\rangle ds\leq\int_{1}^{(T-t)/(\lambda_{*}R)^{2}(t)}z^{-1}\langle\ln z\rangle dz\lesssim\ln^{2}(T-t)
, and

(D.17)

0<\sigma_{0}<1,\quad\beta<1/2.

For
0<\alpha<1
,

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}+\int_{t}^{T}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds

\displaystyle+\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big[\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(T-t)^{\frac{-1-\alpha}{2}}\Big]+\int_{t}^{T}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3}{2}}ds

(D.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1},

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we discussed three cases
1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}<0,=0,>0
, and used

(D.19)

\beta<1/2,\quad\sigma_{0}<1/2,\quad\beta-\sigma_{0}<\alpha/2.

For
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,

(D.20)

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{-\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1-\alpha}(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-\frac{3+\alpha}{2}}ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(t)(T-t)^{\frac{-1-\alpha}{2}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1-\alpha}(t),

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we discussed three cases
1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}<0,=0,>0
and required

(D.21)

\beta<1/2,\quad 1-\sigma_{0}-(1+\alpha)(1-\beta)<0.

For
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,
by (
C.15
), (
C.20
), and
\beta<1/2
,

(D.22)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t_{*})|(t_{*}-t)^{-\alpha/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(s)(T-s)^{-1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}|\ln(T-s)|ds+\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(0),

where the last step is guaranteed by the restriction (
D.15
).

Convolution estimates about
\varrho_{3}=T^{-\sigma_{0}}
. Consider
|f|\leq\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq\sqrt{T-t}\}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>\sqrt{T-t}\}}
. Then

(D.23)

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim T,\quad|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim T^{1/2},\quad|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)|\ln(T-t)|.

For
0<\alpha<1
,

(D.24)

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}(T-s)^{-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}ds+(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

For
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,

(D.25)

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|})^{-\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{[t-(T-t)]_{+}}(T-s)^{-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}ds+(T-t)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}.

For
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
,
by (
C.15
), (
C.20
),

(D.26)

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t_{*})|(t_{*}-t)^{-\alpha/2}\lesssim T^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

In sum, for
|f|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\big(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\varrho_{3}
,
combining (
D.2
), (
D.13
) and (
D.23
), we have

|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)(0)|\ln T|+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\ln T)^{2}+T^{1-\sigma_{0}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)(0)|\ln T|,

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we require

(D.27)

\Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,\ \sigma_{0}>0.

Combining (
D.4
), (
D.14
) and (
D.23
), we have

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(0)+T^{\frac{1}{2}-\sigma_{0}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0),

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we used

(D.28)

\Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,\ \sigma_{0}>0,\ \beta<1/2.

Combining (
D.6
), (
D.16
) and (
D.23
), then

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)|\ln(T-t)|+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}\ln^{2}(T-t)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}(T-t)|\ln(T-t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)|\ln(T-t)|,

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we used

(D.29)

\Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,\ \sigma_{0}>0.

Combining (
D.8
), (
D.18
) and (
D.24
), then

|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,T)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}+T^{-\sigma_{0}}T^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}(T-t)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta},

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we used

(D.30)

\Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,\ \sigma_{0}>0,\ \Theta<\alpha/2,\ \Theta+\sigma_{0}<1/2.

Combining (
D.10
), (
D.20
), (
D.25
), for
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\nabla\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x_{*},t_{*})|\big(|x-x_{*}|+\sqrt{|t-t_{*}|}\big)^{-\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(t)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1-\alpha}(t)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}T^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(t),

where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we used

(D.31)

\Theta+2\sigma_{0}-\beta<0,\ \sigma_{0}>0,\ \Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)<0,\ \Theta+\sigma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}-\alpha(\frac{1}{2}-\beta)<0.

Combining (
D.12
), (
D.22
), (
D.26
),
for
0<\alpha<1
and
0<t<t_{*}\leq(T+t)/2
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t)-\mathcal{T}_{2}^{\rm{out}}[f](x,t_{*})\right|(t_{*}-t)^{-\alpha/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{1-\alpha}\big)(0)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}(0)(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}(0)+T^{-\sigma_{0}}T^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\lesssim T^{A_{\rm{o,h}}},

provided

(D.32)

0<A_{\rm{o,h}}<\min\{\Theta+(1-\beta)(1-\alpha),1-2\sigma_{0}-\alpha(1-\beta),1-\sigma_{0}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\}.

Collecting (
D.3
), (
D.5
), (
D.7
), (
D.9
), (
D.11
), (
D.15
), (
D.17
), (
D.19
), (
D.21
), (
D.27
), (
D.28
), (
D.29
),
(
D.30
), (
D.31
), and (
D.32
), we conclude the restrictions (
D.1
) on the parameters.
∎

Appendix E
Estimates of
\mathcal{G}
in
(\ref{G-def})

E.1.
Estimates for terms involving
U_{*}
,
\Phi_{\rm{out}}
,
\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
,
\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}

First, we prepare some useful formulas. By (
2.5
),

(E.1)

\left|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2},\quad\left|\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}.

(E.2)

\left|\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\lambda_{*}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2}.

\displaystyle|U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}|=\

\displaystyle\Big|\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}\Big|\lesssim\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}

(E.3)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2},

where for the last step, we used that for any fixed
j=1,2,\dots,N
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big[\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}+\sum\limits_{m\neq j}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big(\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}\sum\limits_{m\neq j}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\Big)\sim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2};

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2}.

By the same argument for (
E.3
), then

(E.4)

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\bigg|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{3}.

Notice

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}U_{*}|^{2}+U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}=\bigg|\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg|^{2}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}.

Then

(E.5)

\displaystyle\left||\nabla_{x}U_{*}|^{2}+U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}+\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\lambda_{m}^{-2}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-4}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2},

where for the last step, we used that for any fixed
j=1,2,\dots,N
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big[\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{j}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{m\neq j}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\left(\lambda_{m}^{-1}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{m}^{-2}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-4}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\right)\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big[\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\sum\limits_{m\neq j}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\left(\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\right)\Big]\sim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\right);

\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\Big(\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}+\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq m}\lambda_{m}^{-2}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-4}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\Big)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2}.

\displaystyle\left|U_{*}\wedge\Delta_{x}U_{*}\right|=\bigg|U_{*}\wedge\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right|^{2}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}

(E.6)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{3},

where we used (
E.4
) for the last step.

Next, we derive some estimates about
\Phi_{\rm{out}}
,
\Phi_{\rm{in}}
that will be used frequently in the estimate of
\mathcal{G}
.

For
\Phi_{\rm{out}}\in B_{\rm{out}}
defined in (
4.35
), since
\Phi_{\rm{out}}(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)=0
for all
j=1,2,\dots,N
, then

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{\rm{out}}(x,t)\right|=\left|\Phi_{\rm{out}}(x,t)-\Phi_{\rm{out}}(x,T)+\Phi_{\rm{out}}(x,T)-\Phi_{\rm{out}}(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},T)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left[|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+(T-t)\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)\right].

Thus

(E.7)

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right|\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\min\Big\{|\ln T|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}(0)R(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})},

\displaystyle|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+(T-t)\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\inf_{j=1,\dots,N}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)\Big\}.

Combining (
3.2
) and the parameter restriction
\Theta<\beta
, we have

(E.8)

\left|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{j}\rho_{j}\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

By (
4.34
), we have

(E.9)

|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\leq\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right).

For
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R
, by (
3.2
), then for
T\ll 1
, we have
|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R
. Recall
\Phi
given in (
4.1
), then

\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)\right).

By (
4.28
) and (
3.23
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left[\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(z_{j}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}<t+T\}}+T|\ln T|^{-1}z_{j}^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{z_{j}^{2}\geq t+T\}}\right)\right]

(E.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right].

By (
4.28
), we get

\displaystyle\big|\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\big)\big|=\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\nabla_{x}\big(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\big)+Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\
