\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l-1}+(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l}

(E.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}.

By (
3.23
), we have

(E.12)

\big|\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)\big)\big|=\big|\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)+\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}.

Combining (
E.11
) and (
E.12
), we have

(E.13)

|\nabla_{x}(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}})|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big(\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+1\Big)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big].

By (
3.23
) and (
4.28
), it holds that

\displaystyle|\Delta_{x}(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}})|=\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\Big(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Big)+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\nabla_{x}\Big(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Big)+Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},t)\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\nabla_{x}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)+\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}(r_{j},t)\Delta_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\Big|

(E.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big(\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-2}+\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\Big)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\Big].

Combining (
E.8
) and (
E.10
), we have

(E.15)

\displaystyle|\Phi|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)\left(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

Integrating (
E.9
), (
E.13
), we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}\Phi|\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)\left(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+1\right)

(E.16)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

By (
E.15
), (
E.16
), then

(E.17)

\displaystyle|\Phi||\nabla_{x}\Phi|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-1}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\Theta}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}^{2}.

Recalling
\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0
in (
4.1
) yields

\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\cdot U_{*}=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right),

which implies

(E.18)

\bigg|\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\cdot U_{*}\bigg|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|\lambda_{*}\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}.

By (
E.18
), (
3.23
) and (
E.8
), we obtain

(E.19)

\displaystyle|\Phi\cdot U_{*}|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\Big)\Big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha})\big(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\big)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

Using (
4.28
), (
E.11
), (
E.1
), we have

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla_{x}\Big[\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\Big]\Big|=\Big|(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\cdot\nabla_{x}\Big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)+\Big(\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Big)\cdot\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\big(\lambda_{*}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+\lambda_{*}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}\big)

(E.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}.

(
3.23
), (
E.12
), and (
E.1
) imply

\displaystyle\big|\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\cdot U_{*}\big)\big|=\big|U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\big)+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\big|

(E.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big(1+\lambda_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\sum\limits_{k=1}^{N}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}\Big)\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}.

By (
E.8
), (
E.9
) and (
E.1
), we have

(E.22)

\left|\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi_{\rm out}\cdot U_{*}\right)\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+1\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

Combining (
E.20
), (
E.21
) and (
E.22
), we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})|\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+1\right)

(E.23)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

E.2.
Estimates of
\nabla_{x}A

Claim:
Suppose that
l>0,0<\delta_{0}<\nu<1
given in (
4.29
),

(E.24)

\Theta>0,\quad\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0,\quad\Theta+\beta-1<0,\quad\beta<1/2,\quad\Theta+\beta+4\delta_{0}-4\nu+1<0,\quad 3\beta<\Theta+1,

then for
\nabla_{x}A
given in (
4.11
) and
\epsilon>0
sufficiently small, we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}A=-U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}-\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+O\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle~\times\left(\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)\Big]

(E.25)

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\bigg).

Using (
E.3
), (
E.17
), then

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}A=O\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle~\times\left(\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-1}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\Theta}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\left(\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)\Big]

(E.26)

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\bigg).

Proof of Claim.

First, let us simplify (
4.11
).

\displaystyle|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2}=|\Phi|^{2}+(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2},\quad U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi=(1-|U_{*}|^{2})(\Phi\cdot U_{*}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}(|\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi|^{2})=2\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})^{2}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+2(|U_{*}|^{2}-2)(\Phi\cdot U_{*})\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)=(1-|U_{*}|^{2})\nabla_{x}(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-2(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}.

By (
4.5
), (
3.4
), (
3.2
) and (
4.3
), we have

(E.27)

(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)=1+O\big(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\big),\mbox{ \ then \ }\big[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi)\big]^{-1}=1+O\big(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\big).

Thus we obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}A=-\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)\Big\{(1+A)^{2}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)^{2}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle+\left(\left|U_{*}\right|^{2}-2\right)\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)+(1+A)\left[\left(1-|U_{*}|^{2}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)-2\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right]\Big\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)\Big\{\left[1+A(2+A)-2(1+A)\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)+\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)^{2}\right]U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}+\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi

\displaystyle+\left(\left|U_{*}\right|^{2}-2\right)\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)+(1+A)\left(1-|U_{*}|^{2}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\Big\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}-\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+\left(2\Phi\cdot U_{*}+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}-O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi

\displaystyle-\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)\Big[\left(\left|U_{*}\right|^{2}-2\right)\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)+(1+A)\left(1-|U_{*}|^{2}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\Big],

where we used
A=O(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2})\ll 1
by (
4.5
) and (
4.3
).

By (
E.15
), (
E.19
), and parameter assumption (
4.29
), we have

\displaystyle|\Phi\cdot U_{*}|+\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)\Big]

(E.28)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}.

Notice
\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}=\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+\Theta+\beta}
. Then using (
E.3
) and (
E.28
), we get

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\Phi\cdot U_{*}+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\bigg\{\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-2}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\right)\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\lambda_{*}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}

(E.29)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\lambda_{*}^{2}

for some
\epsilon>0
, where for the last “
\lesssim
”, we require

(E.30)

\Theta>0,\quad\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0,\quad\Theta+\beta-1<0,\quad\beta<1/2.

By (
E.15
) and (
E.16
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left|O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\Phi\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right|\lesssim\big(|\Phi|^{3}+\lambda_{*}|\Phi|\big)\left|\nabla_{x}\Phi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\lambda_{*}^{4\nu-4\delta_{0}-1}+\lambda_{*}^{3}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{3}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{2-l}+|\ln(T-t)|^{3}\lambda^{\nu-\delta_{0}+3\Theta+2}_{*}R^{3}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\Theta+1}R+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\left(\lambda_{*}^{3}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{3}+|\ln(T-t)|^{3}\lambda^{3\Theta+3}_{*}R^{3}+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+2}_{*}R\right)\Big]

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{4}\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4}\left(\lambda_{*}^{3}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{3}+|\ln(T-t)|^{3}\lambda^{3\Theta+3}_{*}R^{3}+\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+2}_{*}R\right)\Big]

(E.31)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{4},

where we require (
4.29
) and additional parameters restriction for the last “
\lesssim
”

(E.32)

\beta<1/2,\quad\Theta+\beta+4\delta_{0}-4\nu+1<0,\quad\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0,\quad\Theta+2\beta<2.

Combining (
E.19
) and (
E.23
), we have

(E.33)

\displaystyle\left|\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\right|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+1+\Theta}R+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+|\ln(T-t)|^{2}\lambda^{2\Theta+1}_{*}R^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+1}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}^{2},

where for the last step, we require

(E.34)

\beta<1/2,\quad 3\beta<\Theta+1,\quad\Theta+\beta<\nu-\delta_{0}+1.

By (
3.4
), (
E.23
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(1-|U_{*}|^{2}\right)\nabla_{x}\left(\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon+1}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}

(E.35)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}

provided

(E.36)

\Theta+\beta<1,\quad\beta<1/2.

Under the parameters restriction (
E.24
), which is the combination of (
E.30
), (
E.32
), (
E.34
), and (
E.36
),
we conclude the validity of (
E.25
) from (
E.29
), (
E.31
), (
E.33
), and (
E.35
).
∎

E.3.
Complete estimates of
\mathcal{G}

Lemma E.1
.

For
\mathcal{G}
given in (
4.21
),
suppose that the ansatz (
3.2
) holds,

\Phi_{\rm{out}}\in B_{\rm{out}}
defined in (
4.35
),

\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\leq\Lambda_{\rm{in}}
,
\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0
for
j=1,2,\dots,N
, under the parameter assumptions

\displaystyle l>0,\quad 0<\Theta<\beta<1/2,\quad\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad 3\beta<1+\Theta,\quad\beta(l+1)-1+\nu-\delta_{0}-\Theta>0,

(E.37)

\displaystyle\Theta+2\beta-1<0,\quad 0<\delta_{0}<\nu<1,\quad 2\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad\Theta+\beta+1+3\delta_{0}-3\nu<0,

then there exists
0<\epsilon\ll 1
such that

\|\mathcal{G}\|_{**}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}
with the norm
\|\cdot\|_{**}
defined in (
4.33
).

Remark E.1
.

In the process of the analysis, there is a delicate cancellation for
\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}
. See (
E.65
) and (
E.62
), (
E.64
).

Proof.

\bullet

Recall
\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
given in (
4.2
).
By (
3.2
),
R(t)=\lambda_{*}^{-\beta}(t)
with
\beta>0
given (
4.3
),
T\ll 1
, then
1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\leq\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq\lambda_{*}R/2\}}
and
\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq\lambda_{*}R/2\}}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\sim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq\lambda_{*}R/2\}}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|
.
By (
E.1
), (
E.7
), and (
E.9
), we have

\displaystyle\big|(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge)\big[|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi_{\rm{out}}-2\nabla_{x}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq\lambda_{*}R/2\}}\big(|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{j}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}|\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq\lambda_{*}R/2\}}\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\big\{(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})})\lambda_{*}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-4}\big[|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R

\displaystyle+(T-t)\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})})\big]\big\}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}(\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}).

\bullet

\displaystyle\Big|\left(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Delta_{x}(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\Big|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big|\left(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}-(1-\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\Big\{-\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}{\partial}_{t}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[2\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle-2\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\Big\}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2d_{q}\}}\big(\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq d_{q}\}}\big[\big(\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\lambda}_{j}|+|\dot{\gamma}_{j}|\big)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{d_{q}\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2d_{q}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3d_{q}\}}\big(\lambda_{*}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-2}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|R^{-1}+|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq d_{q}\}}\big[\big(|\dot{\lambda}_{j}|+\lambda_{j}|\dot{\gamma}_{j}|\big)+\lambda_{j}|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{d_{q}\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2d_{q}\}}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\big(\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\big),

where we used (
3.50
), (
3.9
), (
3.23
), (
E.1
) for the first “
\lesssim
”,
and (
3.2
) for the second and third “
\lesssim
”.

\bullet

By (
2.14
), (
3.48
), we have

\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big(\tilde{M}_{0}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+e^{i\theta_{j}}\tilde{M}_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+e^{-i\theta_{j}}M_{-1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}^{-1}}\big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big(|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\big)\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}.

\bullet

Using
|\dot{\gamma}_{j}(t)|\leq C_{\gamma}(T-t)^{-1}
,

|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(t)|\leq C_{\xi}\lambda_{*}^{\epsilon_{\xi}}(t)
in (
3.2
), one has

\left|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left[\left(\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\lambda}_{j}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\dot{\gamma}_{j}J\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\right|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(T-t)^{-1}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

provided

(E.38)

\Theta+\delta_{0}+\beta-\nu<0.

\bullet

Using
\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\leq\Lambda_{\rm{in}}
,
\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0
, and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\left|Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left\{-\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\left(W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\left(2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big|\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\nabla\eta)\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{*}R}\Big)\cdot\Big(\frac{\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{*}R}+\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{*}R}\frac{(\lambda_{*}R)^{\prime}}{\lambda_{*}R}\Big)

\displaystyle+\left(a-bW^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big[\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-2}(\Delta\eta)\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{*}R}\Big)+2(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}(\nabla\eta)\Big(\frac{x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}{\lambda_{*}R}\Big)\cdot\lambda_{j}^{-1}\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big[(T-t)^{-1}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l}+(\lambda_{*}R)^{-2}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l}

(E.39)

\displaystyle+(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l-1}\Big]\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-2}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}R^{-l}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}

provided

(E.40)

\beta<1/2,\quad\nu-\delta_{0}+\beta l-(1-\beta)>\Theta.

\bullet

Using

U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})=W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=0
, we have

\displaystyle(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\Big\{\Delta_{x}\Big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)+\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Big(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{x}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\nabla_{x}\Big[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\Big)\Big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\Big\{\Delta_{x}\Big(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)-2\nabla_{x}\Big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Big(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\nabla_{x}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}.

Here, by (
3.4
), (
3.23
), (
E.1
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left|\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\wedge\left[\Delta_{x}\left(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big|\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\wedge\Big[\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle-2\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\nabla_{x}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-2\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}\left(1+\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\lambda_{*}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3d_{q}\}}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}.

\bullet

By (
3.4
), similar to the estimate in (
E.39
),

\left|\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\wedge\left[Q_{\gamma_{j}}\left(\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\right|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\left|\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}.

\bullet

By (
3.4
),

\displaystyle\big|(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Delta_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\rangle^{-l-2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}

provided

(E.41)

\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0.

\bullet

For a fixed
j
, by (
3.4
), (
E.1
),

\displaystyle\left|\left(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\wedge\left[\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm out}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\right|\lesssim\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}\big(|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+|\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\lambda_{j}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\big).

We claim that

(E.42)

\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\gtrsim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\mbox{ \ for \ }j\neq k.

Indeed, for
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|/2
, then
\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\sim\lambda_{*}^{-1}
, which implies

\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\sim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\sim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}.

For
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|/2
, similarly, we have
\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\sim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}
.
For
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|>|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|/2
and
|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|>|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|/2
, then

\langle\rho_{j}\rangle\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\sim\lambda_{*}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}||x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\gtrsim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}.

Recall
\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}
given in (
4.34
). For
j\neq k
, by (
E.42
),

\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\lambda_{j}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}.

Under the restriction
\Theta<\beta<1/2
, by (
E.8
),

\displaystyle\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}|\Phi_{\rm{out}}|\lambda_{j}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\lambda_{*}^{2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}+\lambda_{*}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-3}\right)+\sum\limits_{m=1,m\neq j,k}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+4}_{*}R+\lambda_{*}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{m=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{3}\Big]\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\big(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\big).

\bullet

By (
3.4
), (
E.1
), and (
E.42
),

\displaystyle\Big|(a-bU_{*}\wedge)\Big\{\nabla_{x}\big[\Phi\cdot(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\Big|\lesssim\Big(|\nabla_{x}\Phi|\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-1}+|\Phi|\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}\Big)\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{x}\Phi\right|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\left(\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\right)^{-1}+\left|\Phi\right|\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\left(\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\right)^{-2}.

By (
E.7
), (
E.10
), and the assumption
\delta_{0}<\nu
in (
4.29
), we have

\left|\Phi\right|\left(\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\right)^{-2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}.

By (
E.9
) and (
E.13
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{x}\Phi\right|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\left(\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\right)^{-1}\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\left(\lambda^{\Theta}_{*}(0)+\|Z_{*}\|_{C^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{N}\left[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{m}\rangle^{-l-1}+1\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\right]\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\Big(\sum\limits_{m=1}^{N}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\Big)

provided

(E.43)

\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0.

\bullet
To estimate

\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\Big\{-2\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}\Big[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\Big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}
.
For
k\neq j
,
by (
E.1
), and
\|\cdot\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}
given in (

),

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla_{x}\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\lambda_{k}^{-1}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l-1}+\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle+\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\leq|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big]\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\big(\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l-1}+\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l}\lambda_{*}^{2}\big)\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}

when

(E.44)

\nu-\delta_{0}>\Theta+\beta-1;

additionally, by (
3.23
), (
E.1
),

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{x}\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\left(\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|\leq 2d_{q}\}}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}.

\bullet

To estimate
\sum_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\sum_{k=1,k\neq j}^{N}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}+\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)
.
For
k\neq j
, by (

), (

),

\displaystyle\left||\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta,l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-l}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}},\quad|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}|\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}}_{0}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}

under the assumption
2+\nu-\delta_{0}>\Theta+\beta-1
.

\bullet

To estimate
a\Phi\sum_{j,k=1,j\neq k}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}
. For

,
by (

), (

), (

), and (

\left|\Phi\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim|\Phi|\lambda_{*}^{-2}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\langle\rho_{k}\rangle^{-2}\lesssim|\Phi|\left(\min\left\{\langle\rho_{j}\rangle,\langle\rho_{k}\rangle\right\}\right)^{-2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}

under the assumption (
E.44
).

\bullet

By (
4.5
), (
3.9
), (
E.28
), and the ansatz (

),

\displaystyle\left|[(\Phi\cdot U_{*})-A]{\partial}_{t}U_{*}\right|\lesssim\left(|\Phi\cdot U_{*}|+\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left[\left(\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\lambda}_{j}|+|\dot{\gamma}_{j}|\right)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\bigg\{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)\Big]

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\left(1+\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left(\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\lambda}_{j}|+|\dot{\gamma}_{j}|+\lambda_{j}^{-1}|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\right)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\bigg)

provided

(E.45)

\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0,\quad\beta<1/2.

\bullet
To estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\Big[-2a\left(\nabla_{x}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)+a|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle+\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big].

We estimate term by term. First, by (
2.5
), (
3.4
),

\displaystyle\left|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\left(\nabla_{x}W^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right|\lesssim\lambda_{j}^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\|\Phi_{\rm{in}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\|_{{\rm in},\nu-\delta_{0},l}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

provided

(E.46)

\Theta+\delta_{0}+\beta-\nu<0.

Next, by (
3.4
), (
E.1
), (

),

\left|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right|\lesssim\|\Phi_{\rm{out}}\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\left(|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R+\lambda_{j}\rho_{j}\right)\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

provided
\Theta<\beta<1/2
. Finally, by (
3.4
) and (
3.41
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\Big[\Big\{-{\partial}_{t}(\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})+\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\left[\Delta_{x}\Phi_{0}^{*{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}-2\nabla_{x}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\Big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{*}\left(|\dot{\xi}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}+|\lambda_{j}|^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}\right)\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3}.

\bullet

By (
E.27
), we have

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[(1+A)|U_{*}|^{2}+\left(U_{*}\cdot\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\right]^{-1}\left[\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(AU_{*}+\Pi_{U_{*}^{\perp}}\Phi\right)\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\right)\left[\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[AU_{*}+\Phi-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]-\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle+(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[\Phi+\left(A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle+(\Phi\wedge U_{*})O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\left[\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[AU_{*}-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right).

For above terms, we estimate by (
4.5
) and the ansatz
|\Phi|\ll 1
in (
4.3
),

\displaystyle\Big|(\Phi\wedge U_{*})\left[\Phi+\left(A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle+(\Phi\wedge U_{*})O\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}\right)\left[\Phi+\left(1+A-\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right)U_{*}\right]\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[AU_{*}-(\Phi\cdot U_{*})U_{*}\right]\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\Big|\lesssim\left(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}+\left|\Phi\cdot U_{*}\right|\right)\left|\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right|.

Using (
E.28
) and (
E.14
), we have

\displaystyle(\lambda_{*}+|\Phi|^{2}+|\Phi\cdot U_{*}|)|\Delta_{x}(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big(\lambda_{*}^{3\nu-3\delta_{0}-2}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+\nu-\delta_{0}-1}R+\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}+1+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big]\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\Big)

provided

(E.47)

\Theta+\beta+1+3\delta_{0}-3\nu<0,\quad\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad 2\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0,\quad\beta<1/2.

We need more refined estimates for the other part. Recalling (
4.1
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\Phi\wedge U_{*}\right)\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]-\Phi\wedge\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)=-\Phi\wedge\left\{\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)-\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U_{*}\right\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}+\Phi_{\rm out}\Big)\wedge\left\{\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)-\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U_{*}\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\left\{\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U_{*}\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\left\{\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)-\left[U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right\}.

By (
3.23
) and (
E.8
), one has

\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}+\Phi_{\rm out}\Big|\lesssim\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\lambda_{j}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}.

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{d_{q}}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Phi^{*{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}_{0}+\Phi_{\rm out}\Big)\wedge\left\{\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)-\left[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}\left(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}}\right)\right]U_{*}\right\}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+1+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}+\Theta-1}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-2}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big]\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\Big)

provided

(E.48)

By (

\displaystyle\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}(Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\big\{\big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\cdot\Delta_{x}(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}})\big]U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\big[U_{*}\cdot\Delta_{x}(\Phi-\Phi_{\rm{out}})\big](U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\big\}\big|

(E.49)

provided

(E.50)

Since
