\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left\{\left[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left\{\left[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left\{\left[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right\}\bigg]

\displaystyle+2\left(a-bU_{*}\wedge\right)\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left\{\left[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}2\left(a-bU^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\wedge\right)\Big\{\left[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)-\left[\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big\}.

Due to the cut-off functions, (
E.1
), (
E.16
), and (
3.23
), (
E.8
) with
\Phi_{\rm{out}}\in B_{\rm{out}}
and
\Theta<\beta
, (
E.9
), it follows that

\displaystyle\Big|(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi)\Phi-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\Big|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bigg|\left(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\right)\Phi-\bigg[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi\bigg)\bigg(\Phi-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)+\bigg[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\bigg(\Phi-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\bigg(\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l-1}+1\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\left(\lambda_{*}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda^{\Theta+1}_{*}R\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\bigg\}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-l}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\left(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-\delta_{0}-1}+|\ln(T-t)|\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+\nu-\delta_{0}-1}R\right)+\mathbf{1}_{\{3\lambda_{*}R<|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-1}\Big]+\mathbf{1}_{\{\cap_{j=1}^{N}\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle T^{\epsilon}\bigg(\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\bigg)

provided
\delta_{0}<\nu<1
in (
4.29
),

(E.60)

\Theta+\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0,\quad 2\beta+\delta_{0}-\nu<0.

We estimate by (
E.1
), (
E.11
) that

\displaystyle\big|\big[\nabla_{x}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big]\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)-\big[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big]\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}\big|\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big|\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-1}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

under the assumption
\delta_{0}<\nu
in (
4.29
) and
\Theta+\beta-1<0
in (
4.32
).
By (
3.4
), (
E.1
), (
E.11
),

\big|(U_{*}-U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\wedge\big[\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big]\big(\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}Q_{\gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\rm in}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}}\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2\delta_{0}-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2l-3}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

provided

(E.61)

\Theta+\beta+2\delta_{0}-2\nu<0.

The other terms in this collection can be handled in the same way.

\bullet

Before proceeding, we take a closer look at
\Delta_{x}U_{*}
and
\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}=2(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}
.
In the single bubble case
N=1
,
\Delta_{x}U_{*}
can be neglected by the
U_{*}
-operation and
\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}
vanishes automatically. However, in the case of multiple bubbles, the phenomenon is different. There exists delicate cancellation for
\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}
.

Recall the definition of
f_{\mathcal{C}_{j}^{-1}}
given in (
2.14
). Claim:

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}U_{*}=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\Big\{16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{i(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j})}e^{-i\theta_{j}}

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}-i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{-i(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j})}e^{i\theta_{j}}\Big\}_{\mathcal{C}_{j}^{-1}}

(E.62)

\displaystyle+O(T^{\epsilon})\bigg[\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\big(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\varrho_{3}\bigg]-\Xi_{1}(x,t)U_{*}

for some scalar function
\Xi_{1}(x,t)
when

(E.63)

\Theta+2\beta-1<0.

Under the assumption (
E.63
), then

(E.64)

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}(|U_{*}|^{2})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}=2(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{m\neq j}\Big\{16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{m}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{i(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j})}e^{-i\theta_{j}}

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{m}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}-i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{-i(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j})}e^{i\theta_{j}}\Big\}_{\mathcal{C}_{j}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+O(T^{\epsilon})\bigg[\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\big(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)+\varrho_{3}\bigg].

In particular,

(E.65)

\Delta_{x}U_{*}-2\left(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}=O(T^{\epsilon})\bigg[\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\left(\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)+\varrho_{3}\bigg]-\Xi_{1}(x,t)U_{*}.

Proof of (
E.62
).

Given
j\in\{1,\dots,N\}
,

|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})+U_{*}]
.
By (
E.1
), (
3.4
),

\displaystyle\big||\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2},

\displaystyle\big|\big(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big)|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{3}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-4}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\big(\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\big).

The estimate
\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}
is too rough and can not be controlled by the outer topology. More sophisticated analysis will be applied. Indeed, by (
2.5
) and the representation (
2.11
),

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)=-2\lambda_{j}^{-2}|\nabla_{y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{2}+1)^{-1}Q_{\gamma_{k}}\begin{bmatrix}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1},y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2},-1\end{bmatrix}^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-16\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}(\rho_{k}^{2}+1)^{-1}\Big[e^{i\gamma_{k}}\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

For
k\neq j
,

\displaystyle\rho_{k}^{2}=\lambda_{k}^{-2}|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{2}=\lambda_{k}^{-2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{2}\left\{1+|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left[|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+2(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\cdot(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}})\right]\right\},

\displaystyle\left(\rho_{k}^{2}+1\right)^{-1}=\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left\{1+|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left[\lambda_{k}^{2}+|x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}+2(x-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\cdot(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}})\right]\right\}^{-1}.

In particular,

(E.66)

\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left(\rho_{k}^{2}+1\right)^{-1}=\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right),

which implies

(E.67)

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)=-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}(\rho_{k}^{2}+1)^{-1}\Big[e^{i\gamma_{k}}\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)\Big[e^{i\gamma_{k}}\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

Notice by (
2.3
),

\displaystyle y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}=\

\displaystyle\big[\lambda_{j}y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(\lambda_{j}y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]\lambda_{k}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\rho_{j}e^{i\theta_{j}}+\lambda_{k}^{-1}\big[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big].

Recall
\mathbf{f}_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}
defined in (
2.13
). Combining (
2.20
), we have

\displaystyle\Big(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\Big[e^{i\gamma_{k}}\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\Big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left[\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\rho_{j}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{k}^{-1}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\},

\displaystyle\Big[e^{i\gamma_{k}}\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left[\left(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]-\frac{\rho_{j}^{2}-1}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}

(E.68)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]-\frac{\rho_{j}^{2}-1}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}+\lambda_{k}^{-1}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}.

Then

(E.69)

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\left\{\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\left[|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\right]\right\}_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}=-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\rho_{j}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\bigg\}

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}.

Here, due to
\Theta+\beta-1<0
given in (
4.32
),

\displaystyle\Big|16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big\{\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\rho_{j}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big\}\Big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-3}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},

\displaystyle\Big|16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}\Big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}R\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},

where for the second inequality, we require

(E.70)

\Theta+2\beta-1<0.

\displaystyle\Big|-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\ln T|^{-1}\ln^{2}(T-t)\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}.

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}e^{-i\theta_{j}}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}-i\left(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{-i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}e^{i\theta_{j}},

which will be put into mode
-1
and mode
1
respectively.

For the projection in
U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
, by (
E.67
), (
E.68
), we calculate

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*}\right)\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\sum\limits_{k\neq j}\lambda_{k}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big[\lambda_{j}\lambda_{k}^{-1}\frac{2\rho_{j}^{2}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]-\frac{\rho_{j}^{2}-1}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}+\lambda_{k}^{-1}\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[k]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}\Big].

Thus by
U_{*}
-operation and (
3.4
),

\big|\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{2}[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\cdot U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}](U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-U_{*})\big|\lesssim\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-4}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}.

In sum, we conclude the validity of (
E.62
). ∎

Proof of (
E.64
).

Note that

2(U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*})\cdot\nabla_{x}U_{*}=2\Big(\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}U_{*}\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)\cdot\sum\limits_{n=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}=2\Big[\sum\limits_{j=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{m\neq j}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\cdot\sum\limits_{n=1}^{N}\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}
.
For any fixed
j\in\{1,\dots,N\}
and any
m\neq j
,
n\neq j
, by (
E.1
),

\displaystyle\left|\left[\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}\right|\lesssim\left|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right|\left|\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\langle\rho_{j}\rangle^{-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}|<3d_{q}\}}\langle\rho_{n}\rangle^{-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\cap\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[n]$}}}}}|\geq 3d_{q}\}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{3},

\displaystyle\big|(1-\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}})\big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R/2\leq|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|\leq d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{3}|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|^{-4}+\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}|>d_{q}\}}\lambda_{*}^{2}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\big(\varrho_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\varrho_{3}\big).

A more refined analysis is needed for
2\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big[\sum\limits_{m\neq j}(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}
.
For any
m\neq j
,

\Big[\big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\big)\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\cdot\nabla_{x}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big[\big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\big)\cdot{\partial}_{x_{k}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]\partial_{x_{k}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=\lambda_{j}^{-2}\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big[\big(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\big)\cdot{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}.

Recall (
2.3
). By (
2.8
) and (
2.5
), we write in polar coordinates

\displaystyle{\partial}_{y_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=

\displaystyle\cos\theta_{j}{\partial}_{\rho_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\rho_{j}^{-1}\sin\theta_{j}{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=-2(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-1}\big(\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big),

\displaystyle{\partial}_{y_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=

\displaystyle\sin\theta_{j}{\partial}_{\rho_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\rho_{j}^{-1}\cos\theta_{j}{\partial}_{\theta_{j}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}=-2(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-1}\big(\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\big),

which implies

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}^{-2}\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 4\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\Big\{\left[\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot\left(\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left[\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot\left(\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\right]\left(\sin\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\cos\theta_{j}Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right)\Big\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 4\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\left\{\left[\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+\left[\left(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}\right)\cdot Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right]Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\right\}.

The representation (
2.11
) then reads

U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty}=2(|y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{2}+1)^{-1}Q_{\gamma_{m}}\begin{bmatrix}y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1},y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2},-1\end{bmatrix}^{\mathsf{tr}}=2(\rho_{m}^{2}+1)^{-1}\Big[e^{i\gamma_{m}}(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}.

From this and (
E.68
), it follows that

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{j}^{-2}\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 4\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\Big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}+i(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot Q_{\gamma_{j}}E_{2}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 8\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}(\rho_{m}^{2}+1)^{-1}\Big(\Pi_{U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}\perp}}\Big[e^{i\gamma_{m}}\big(y^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+iy^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\big),-1\Big]^{\mathsf{tr}}\Big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 8\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}(\rho_{m}^{2}+1)^{-1}\Big[\lambda_{j}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\rho_{j}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left[e^{i\left(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{m}^{-1}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}\Big].

Combining (
E.66
), we have

\displaystyle 2\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big(\lambda_{j}^{-2}\sum\limits_{k=1}^{2}\Big[(U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}-U_{\infty})\cdot{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big]{\partial}_{y_{k}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}}U^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\Big)_{\mathcal{C}_{j}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\lambda_{m}^{2}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)\Big[\lambda_{j}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\rho_{j}\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\big[e^{i(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j})}\big]+\frac{2\rho_{j}}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}\Big]

\displaystyle+16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-2}\lambda_{m}|\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\left(1+O\left(\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}R\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big(1-\frac{2}{\rho_{j}^{2}+1}{\rm{Re}}\Big)\left\{\left[\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+i\left(\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-\xi^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\right)\right]e^{i\left(-\theta_{j}+\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}\rho_{j}^{2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{m}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}+i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{i(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j})}e^{-i\theta_{j}}

\displaystyle-16\eta_{R}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}\lambda_{j}^{-2}(\rho_{j}^{2}+1)^{-3}\lambda_{m}|q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}|^{-2}\big[q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{1}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{1}-i\big(q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}}_{2}-q^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[m]$}}}}}_{2}\big)\big]e^{-i(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{j})}e^{i\theta_{j}}+O(T^{\epsilon})\varrho_{1}^{{\mbox{{\tiny{$[j]$}}}}},

where for the last equality, we used the assumption (
E.70
) and the same estimates for (
E.69
). ∎

Combining
l>0,0<\delta_{0}<\nu<1
given in (
4.29
), (
E.24
) (These are used for the estimate of
\nabla_{x}A
in (
E.25
)), (

), (

), (

),
we get the parameter requirement (

).
